//@version=6
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Liquidity + Supply/Demand + Price Action — v3.5
//
// v3.5 — ENTRY PRECISION PASS (FIX-X1 … FIX-X9). Nothing here adds a new
// signal family; every change is about WHERE the order sits, WHAT price is
// recorded for it and WHICH setups are allowed to reach it.
//   FIX-X1  A CE limit fills only on a real touch. `sbCeTol` counted the fill
//           while the low stopped short of the CE, and then wrote the exact CE
//           as the plan price — a price the bar never traded, with MODULE 18B's
//           slippage guard deliberately waived for it. The tolerance now serves
//           the REJECTION path only, where the plan price is the traded price.
//   FIX-X2  The deferred (next-bar-open) engine order is re-validated at the
//           price it actually fills at: slippage guard, risk cap and min RR.
//           v3.4 re-derived the TP ladder from the new price but re-tested
//           nothing, so a gap open bypassed the whole viability gate.
//   FIX-X3  The ORDINARY machine path places a limit at the CE of its locked
//           FVG, exactly as the SB path already did. Same anchor and same stop,
//           but v3.4 could only take the proximal edge — roughly double the
//           risk distance for half the RR on identical structure. The
//           confirmation candle stays as the fallback (`mcEntryMode`), and a
//           machine CE fill is a resting order, so it is never deferred.
//   FIX-X4  Two structural stops. The sweep-wick stop is tried first; when it
//           breaks the risk cap the ANCHOR stop is used before the cap logic
//           clamps or rejects, so a trade is no longer stopped at "entry minus
//           1.5 ATR", a price with no structure behind it (`stopTier`).
//   FIX-X5  Premium / discount is a rule for the machine too (`mcReqPD`), not
//           just for SB and the A+ tier — with the tier filter at its default
//           the ordinary path was free to buy premium. Waived when the HTF bias
//           and external structure both agree (`mcPdWaive`).
//   FIX-X6  An opposing displacement no longer kills a stage-3 setup when the
//           candle closes back on the right side of the anchor: that candle is
//           the RETEST the setup was waiting for.
//   FIX-X7  Volume alone can no longer manufacture a STRONG sweep. The FIX-F2
//           bump now needs an outer-30% close or a displacement body as well,
//           so the RAW grade the A+ tier demands stays independent evidence.
//   FIX-X8  `minScore` judges the BASE alignment; the SB context bonus (up to
//           15) is excluded from the gate but still shown (`scoreOnBase`).
//   FIX-X9  The machine's confirmation candle must engage its own anchor —
//           close past the CE and in its own outer 30% (`mcStrictCnf`).
//
// Liquidity + Supply/Demand + Price Action — v3.1.1
// Built from 2.version-3.1-silverbullet.pine (left untouched) — SB-9 … SB-14.
// Unified Context → Liquidity → POI → Structure → Trigger → Execution engine
//
// v3 REBUILD NOTES (V2 kept as the reference implementation — see the v2 file):
//   ARCH-1 One SETUP STATE MACHINE per direction is now the primary signal
//          path: IDLE → SWEPT → AT POI → SHIFTED (MSS/CHoCH/CISD) → AWAIT
//          RETEST → ENTRY → ACTIVE → COMPLETED/INVALIDATED. Engine outputs
//          (CRT / Turtle soup / trendline / zone 50% / inside bar / doji) are
//          TRIGGER CANDIDATES graded through the same layer model instead of
//          independent signal generators. No duplicate signals from one event:
//          every stage is edge-triggered and expires.
//   ARCH-2 Signal TIERS: C = bare technical trigger · B = trigger + one core
//          layer (liquidity or POI) · A = liquidity + POI + structure trigger
//          + execution · A+ = A + HTF alignment + session/context + strong
//          sweep + premium/discount on the correct side. A display filter
//          hides tiers below the chosen minimum (default B and above).
//   ARCH-3 Sweep QUALITY grades (per spec): 1 WEAK = level taken + reclaimed
//          but the close held only the inner half · 2 VALID = taken + firm
//          reclaim · 3 STRONG = valid + outer-half close + displacement body ·
//          4 INSTITUTIONAL = strong sweep followed by a structure shift while
//          the raid is in play. Only quality ≥ 2 arms the state machine;
//          quality ≥ 3 earns the score bonus.
//   ARCH-4 MSS is now classified separately from CHoCH: CHoCH = displaced
//          close through the protected swing; MSS = that same break while a
//          quality sweep is in play (liquidity event + displacement +
//          meaningful shift). BOS stays the continuation break.
//   ARCH-5 Market REGIME classifier (TRENDING / TRANSITION / RANGING / CHOP):
//          external-structure event recency + external range width in ATR.
//          Machine entries are suppressed in CHOP unless the arming sweep was
//          institutional grade (the one classic exception).
//   ARCH-6 Zone engine carries a KIND per zone: S/D (FVG-imbalance geometry),
//          OB (origin candle before displacement — separate geometry), RB
//          (rejection block wick), BREAKER, MITIGATION. One generic geometry
//          is no longer reused for different concepts. OB boxes are created
//          alongside the S/D imbalance when the origin candle is distinct.
//   ARCH-7 FVG registry (chart TF) tracks top/bottom/mid, creation bar, age,
//          direction, partial-fill and full-fill state instead of a single
//          latest-FVG pair of floats. FEM still LOCKS its first execution FVG
//          (V2 FIX-11 preserved).
//   ARCH-8 Per-session liquidity: Asia / London / NY raids are separate
//          events with separate weights (London/NY raid arms the machine,
//          Asia raid only scores) — a London sweep ≠ an Asia sweep.
//   ARCH-9 Setup EXPIRY at every stage (bars input per stage) + explicit
//          INVALIDATION (sweep undone / POI violated / opposing displacement /
//          protected swing broken) with an alert and a dashboard reason.
//   ARCH-10 Validation counters (A/A+ setups, TP1/TP2/SL outcomes of machine
//          entries) shown in the debug table. Counted mechanically — NOT a
//          statistically reliable backtest.
//   ARCH-11 Debug mode: second table with the raw state of every engine
//          (liquidity / POI / structure / FEM / CRT / TBS / CISD / machine /
//          score components / last invalidation reason).
//   KEPT   From V2 unchanged in concept (audited, verified): CISD strict
//          series (FIX-16) · IFC pool+displacement (FIX-17) · protected-swing
//          structure (FIX-13) · edge-triggered 50% tap (FIX-10) · FVG-gap S/D
//          geometry (FIX-9) · turtle-soup age via -ta.lowestbars()[1]+1
//          (VERIFIED correct: lowestbars returns ≤ 0, so the value is the
//          positive bar-age of the extreme — do not "fix" the sign again) ·
//          breaker-vs-mitigation rule (FIX-14) · HTF POI freshness (FIX-15) ·
//          session RAID vs BREAK split (FIX-18) · candidate→validated
//          trendlines (FIX-19) · layered score (FIX-21) · scheduled
//          volatility windows are NOT a live news calendar.
//   REMOVED Setups ① breakout-rejection-trap and ② pin-bar-trap watchers:
//          they duplicate the turtle-soup / sweep narrative at lower quality
//          and were the main source of C-tier spam. Their idea (trapped
//          breakout crowd) lives on in the turtle-soup and sweep engines.
//   ADDED  MODULE 16B — ◬ LIQUIDITY TRAP ENGINE: 7 breakout-trap patterns
//          (LT-1…LT-7) with per-direction trap state machines, a configurable
//          confidence score, wick-cluster pool tracking and alerts. Fully
//          additive — with the engine disabled v3 behaves exactly as before.
//
// v3.1 — ◆ ICT SILVER BULLET UPGRADE (additive; sbEnable = false ⇒ v3 behaviour
//        except for V31-FIX-1, which is a defect repair, not a feature):
//   SB-1  MODULE 4B SESSION CLOCK + SB WINDOW ENGINE. The killzone /
//         volatility flags moved OUT of MODULE 8 to here so they exist before
//         the liquidity engine. Adds the three doctrine windows (New York
//         time, from the sbSes* inputs): London 03:00–04:00 · NY AM
//         10:00–11:00 (priority) · NY PM 14:00–15:00, plus a pre-window
//         arming grace, minute-accurate "window remaining", per-window close
//         edges (the exit clock) and the SB window range boxes.
//   SB-2  V31-FIX-1 (defect repair). In v3 `recentSsl / sslInPlay / recentBsl
//         / bslInPlay` were evaluated in MODULE 5 ABOVE the PDH/PDL stamps and
//         above MODULE 8's session-raid stamps, so a PD-level or session-level
//         raid could never satisfy the state machine's `lastSslBar ==
//         bar_index` arming test — only swing and EQ sweeps ever armed a
//         setup. The four lines are now evaluated after every stamp, and the
//         session H/L tracker moved into MODULE 5. Logic unchanged; only the
//         evaluation POINT moved. Silver Bullet depends on exactly those pools.
//   SB-3  Sweep POOL KIND memory (lastSslKind / lastBslKind): 1 swing ·
//         2 EQH/EQL pool · 3 PDH/PDL · 4 prior-session H/L. Kind ≥ 2 is a
//         "preferred" (engineered) SB pool. Inside an SB window an ASIA
//         session raid also counts as a pool (its classic NY-AM role);
//         outside the windows Asia keeps its v3 score-only role.
//   SB-4  Dedicated SB ARMING PATH on the MODULE 17 machine: arming inside an
//         SB context window with the window's own minimum sweep quality
//         (London strictest) and a preferred pool stamps a WINDOW TAG on the
//         setup. The tag drives shortened stage clocks (12 / 8 / 10), a
//         MANDATORY clean displacement FVG anchor, the CE entry rule, the
//         window-close kill and the exit clock. Tag 0 = the untouched v3 path.
//   SB-5  SB ENTRY: limit at the CE (50%) of the locked FVG; the proximal edge
//         is accepted only with a strong rejection / CISD / IFC candle. HTF
//         bias alignment and the correct premium/discount side are required.
//   SB-6  SB EXIT ENGINE (MODULE 19, SB-tagged trades only — non-SB trades
//         keep v3's path and counter semantics exactly): break-even + ATR
//         buffer after TP1 · protected-swing trail after TP2 · TP3 = nearest
//         opposing session H/L, PDH/PDL or unspent opposite POI that still
//         pays ≥ TP2 · forced full/partial exit at the window close when TP1
//         is unhit · exit of the remainder on an opposing MSS/CISD of equal
//         or higher grade than the shift that produced the entry.
//   SB-7  SB CONTEXT score component (0–15, NY AM weighted highest); the total
//         is clamped at 100, so a non-SB bar scores exactly as in v3. SB
//         entries are held to a higher minimum tier (A, or A+ by input) and
//         non-SB engine candidates are down-weighted one tier (or muted)
//         while a clean SB setup is live.
//   SB-8  Visuals / alerts: brighter per-window background band (reusing v3's
//         Silver Bullet output slot — the 64-output budget is FULL), window
//         range boxes, the locked SB FVG with its CE line, the planned
//         liquidity target, distinct ◆ SB labels, and ONE consolidated
//         alertcondition plus a rich alert() feed for every SB lifecycle event.
//
// v3.1.1 — six SB-only refinements (every non-SB path byte-for-byte unchanged):
//   SB-9   RELAXED SAME-BAR ARMING for the SB path only. The non-SB path still
//          requires `lastSslBar == bar_index`; a setup that clears the full SB
//          gate may also arm while the sweep is still in play, up to
//          SB_ARM_GRACE (5) bars after it, and only ONCE per sweep.
//   SB-10  Sweep-in-play memory is `sweepLook + 5` inside an SB context and
//          exactly `sweepLook` outside it.
//   SB-11  CE fills need TOUCH + CONFIRMATION: close at/through the CE, or a
//          genuine rejection bar (pin / engulf / IFC / CISD). Pure-wick fills
//          are refused. The proximal edge still needs a rejection and the
//          entry mode that permits it.
//   SB-12  DEALING-RANGE LOCK: premium/discount for an SB-tagged setup is read
//          from the range frozen at the SB window OPEN, cleared when the window
//          ends with no SB setup or SB trade live. Non-SB consumers keep the
//          live `pdPos`.
//   SB-13  CONFLICT HIERARCHY replaces the both-directions mutual kill:
//          SB machine > ordinary machine > pure engine. Only an equal-rank
//          clash still mutes both sides.
//   SB-14  SB exits: adaptive break-even after TP1 (max of the ATR buffer and
//          15% of the TP1 distance) and a post-TP2 trail that never passes
//          through the last two bars' low / high.
//
// v3.2.0 — AUDIT FIX RELEASE (REVIEW_AND_PLAN_BN.md). Defect repairs, not
//          features. Every item below changes BEHAVIOUR on purpose.
//   FIX-P0-1  sslInPlay / bslInPlay now require the RECLAIM to still hold
//             (close on the correct side of the swept level). Without the
//             lower bound a FAILED sweep that turned into a real breakout
//             still read as "liquidity grab live", arming longs in downtrends
//             and shorts in uptrends. Single biggest source of bad signals.
//   FIX-P0-2  Sweep QUALITY is stamped per EVENT. The engineered-pool stamps
//             (EQ / PDH-PDL / session / SB-Asia) used math.max against the
//             PREVIOUS sweep's grade, so a weak poke inherited an old
//             institutional 4 and walked through every quality gate.
//   FIX-P0-3  DETECTION is separated from DRAWING. showSesHL / showEq only
//             control line + label creation now; session H/L and EQH/EQL
//             levels are always tracked. With the toggles off, pool kinds 2
//             and 4 used to be permanently dead — which killed Silver Bullet.
//   FIX-P0-4  ENTRY PRICE is the actual plan price: the locked FVG's CE (or
//             the anchor edge) for machine / SB entries, not the bar close.
//             Every RR, TP level and counter was computed off the wrong price.
//             A slippage guard rejects a fill the bar ran too far away from.
//   FIX-P0-5  Per-engine STOPS. sigSLLong / sigSLShort were shared globals
//             that every engine overwrote, so a signal could ship the stop of
//             a DIFFERENT engine that the tier filter had already muted.
//   FIX-P0-6  An ACTIVE tracked trade is no longer silently overwritten by the
//             next signal (its TP/SL outcome was never counted).
//   FIX-P0-7  The SB chop bypass now also clears the MODULE 18 final gate. A
//             tagged SB setup used to complete the whole chain and then die
//             silently at the last filter.
//   FIX-P0-8  The 10:00 volatility window no longer blanks the first ten
//             minutes of the NY AM Silver Bullet window (newsAllowSb).
//   FIX-P1-1  Sweep quality is split: QualRaw (earned by the sweeping candle,
//             never rewritten) vs QualEff (with the MSS institutional
//             upgrade). Tier A+ and the liquidity score bonus read RAW, so
//             "strong sweep" stops being a condition the MSS grants itself.
//   FIX-P1-2  Machine tier is graded from real components instead of being
//             forced to A, and its premium/discount test reads the LOCKED SB
//             range for a tagged setup (it used to read the live range and
//             contradict the entry rule).
//   FIX-P1-3  The retest anchor must be an FVG cut at or after the SHIFT bar,
//             not any FVG from the last TRIG_LOOK bars.
//   FIX-P1-4  Trendline touches are EDGE-triggered (price must leave and come
//             back) and a line expires. Touch counts used to inflate every bar.
//   FIX-P1-5  Liquidity-trap signals are gated by session / volatility /
//             regime / HTF agreement and a HIGH score floor. They used to
//             bypass every filter the rest of the script applies.
//   FIX-P1-6  "Await retest" is real: entry may not land on the shift bar.
//   FIX-P1-7  Risk is CAPPED (maxRiskAtr) and a minimum RR (minRR) is enforced;
//             a sweep-wick stop 20 bars away used to make TP1 unreachable.
//   FIX-P1-8  sesPts no longer inflates non-intraday context · an unknown
//             external range is RANGING, not CHOP · zone caps drain fully ·
//             TP1 and TP2 on the same bar are both counted.
//   FIX-P1-9  DEALING RANGE CONTAINMENT. Live charts printed "Discount -30%"
//             and "Premium 463%" — `pdPos` had no bound, so once price left the
//             external swing range the premium/discount reading was meaningless
//             AND it was still gating tier A+, the context score and the SB
//             entry rule. The range now resolves to whichever swing source
//             still contains price, expands to include it as a last resort, and
//             the reading is clamped to 0–100%.
//   FIX-P1-10 The dashboard VERDICT names the gate that actually blocked. It
//             read "NO TRADE · chop" while the sweep was INSTITUTIONAL — a
//             grade the chop filter exempts — and never mentioned `minScore`,
//             which is the most common silent blocker at the v3.2 defaults.
//   FIX-P1-11 The NEXT BUY / NEXT SELL label defaults to the middle of its line
//             instead of the right edge, where it printed on top of the
//             dashboard table.
//   FIX-P1-12 THE DEALING RANGE IS THE CURRENT LEG. After FIX-P1-9 every chart
//             read 0% or 100% permanently: a pivot needs `extPiv` bars of
//             right-side confirmation, so the last CONFIRMED swing high always
//             sits behind price and containment could never hold. The range is
//             now the running high-to-low span since the older anchoring swing
//             — price is inside it by construction and premium / discount means
//             something again.
//   FIX-P1-13 Display honesty: the NEXT level label says "price INSIDE" when
//             the candle is in the band (it used to print "0% above"), and an
//             empty demand / supply row loses its tint so "none" cannot be
//             mistaken for a live level.
//
// v3.2.1 — SECOND-PASS AUDIT, PHASE A (DEEP_AUDIT_v3.2_BN.md)
//   FIX-N1    ONE TIER PER SIGNAL. `finalTierL/S` replaces the four surviving
//             `math.max(tierLong, sigMCLong ? 3 : 1)` — the label header, the
//             A/A+ counters and the A/A+ alertconditions now read the same
//             value the label body prints ("A · 55" above "◉ SETUP A+" on one
//             label, and an "A LONG" alert for an A+ entry, are gone).
//   FIX-N1b   THE REGIME MEASURES ITS OWN RANGE (pivot to pivot, per ARCH-5).
//             FIX-P1-12 widened `rngW` to the leg's running extremes and the
//             regime kept reading it, so CHOP was under-detected.
//   FIX-N3    THE LT VETO OBEYS SB-13. An UNCONFIRMED trap may mute pure
//             engine candidates only; a completed machine / SB entry is never
//             vetoed. (The tooltip also said "OFF by default" — it is ON.)
//   FIX-N4    TP3 = max(3, rrMult + 1) R — a TP2 multiple above 3 used to sit
//             past a hardcoded 3R TP3 and invert the ladder.
//
// v3.2.1 — PHASE B (accuracy)
//   FIX-N5    MSS requires an EXTERNAL CHoCH by default (`mssNeedExt`). The
//             chart drew an internal CHoCH as minor grey "iCHoCH" while the
//             logic graded it MSS — display and logic now agree.
//   FIX-N7    Stages 1–2 of the machine re-check the sweep RECLAIM (close
//             back through the swept level, with tolerance), not only the wick
//             extreme. Arming demanded the reclaim; continuation now does too,
//             until the structure shift makes the sweep's job complete.
//   FIX-F2    Optional RELATIVE-VOLUME confirmation: displacement candles
//             must carry volume ≥ volMult × SMA, a raid on volume earns one
//             sweep grade (capped at STRONG). OFF by default — FX tick volume
//             is weak; turn ON for crypto / indices / futures / stocks.
//   FIX-F8    Score RECENCY DECAY: shift / CISD trigger points fade linearly
//             across TRIG_LOOK instead of dropping off a cliff on bar 11.
//             Tiers stay boolean.
//   FIX-N12   Six symbol-sensitive constants became inputs under "Advanced
//             tuning" (NEAR_LVL_ATR, SL_PAD_ATR, SWEEP_CTX_ATR, POI_NEAR_ATR,
//             TRIG_LOOK, CHOP_RANGE_ATR). Defaults unchanged.
//   RETRACTED audit item BUG-N2 ("HTF signals one HTF candle late"): WRONG.
//             `expr[1]` under `lookahead_off` hands every chart bar inside HTF
//             bar N the CLOSED bar N-1 — zero extra delay and no repaint.
//             Removing the `[1]` would introduce lookahead bias on history and
//             repainting in realtime. The `[1]` stays. Recorded here so it is
//             not "fixed" again.
//
// v3.2.1 — PHASE C (features)
//   FIX-F1    HTF STRUCTURE BIAS. The protected-swing engine (BOS / CHoCH)
//             also runs on the bias timeframe (`f_htfStruct`, own pivots and
//             break latch, `[1]` + lookahead_off). `biasMode` picks EMA only ·
//             Structure only · Structure + EMA (default; disagreement = neutral
//             0, which blocks nothing and rewards nothing). Every consumer of
//             the bias — longOK/shortOK, LT gates, score, tiers, SB entry —
//             reads the one combined `htfB`.
//   FIX-F3    INVERTED FVG. A gap that price CLOSES through flips (recolour,
//             "IFVG", fresh clock, opposite array) instead of being deleted;
//             a wick-only fill still just clears it; a second close-through
//             deletes it. The inversion is handed to MODULE 13 as a zone of
//             kind "IFVG" so it is a real POI for NEXT level, the machine and
//             the retest score.
//   FIX-F5    HTF POI REGISTRY: three live HTF FVGs per side instead of one
//             (a new gap no longer erases the morning's untested POI). The
//             working POI is the nearest UNTESTED slot, else the nearest.
//   FIX-N6    HTF POI freshness = SEPARATE VISITS (leave + return), the same
//             definition as the chart-TF zone engine. `hPoiMaxTouch` default
//             3 → 2 to match `zoneMaxTests`.
//   FIX-F6    The working HTF POI competes for the NEXT BUY / SELL level
//             (kind "HTF", grade T3); nearest to price wins.
//   FIX-F4    ADR EXHAUSTION. Day range ÷ average daily range (closed daily
//             bars, non-repainting). Buying the top 30% / selling the bottom
//             30% of a day already past `adrMax` × ADR costs 10 context points
//             and the A+ tier. Dashboard row 18 shows ADR % and HTF POI counts.
//   Budget    still 8 plot + 14 plotchar + 5 bgcolor + 37 alertcondition = 64.
//             request.security calls: 7 (was 5) — limit is 40.
//   ADD-1     NEXT BUY / NEXT SELL level. The nearest UNSPENT demand below
//             price and the nearest unspent supply above it, drawn as a dashed
//             line + shaded band + a label carrying the zone grade, its price
//             range and the distance from price. This is WHERE THE ENGINE IS
//             WAITING — a location marker, NOT a signal. The chain still has to
//             run sweep → POI → shift → retest before anything fires. Built
//             from line/box/label objects because the 64-output budget is full.
//
// v3.2.1 — PHASE D (verification)
//   FIX-F9    STRATEGY TWIN. `version_02_strategy.pine` is this file with the
//             `indicator()` line swapped for `strategy()` and one added module
//             (19S) that hands MODULE 19's tracked trade to the broker
//             emulator: market entry at the signal bar's close, fixed
//             fractional risk, 50% at TP1, remainder at TP2 (TP3 for SB),
//             `trdSL` re-issued every bar so break-even / trail is honoured,
//             `close_all` when the indicator flattens for a non-price reason.
//             Detection code is byte-identical — the Strategy Tester measures
//             the same signals this chart shows. THIS file stays the chart /
//             alert tool; the twin is for numbers only.
//
// Signals evaluate on CLOSED bars only — use "Once per bar close" alerts.
// The confluence score is NARRATIVE ALIGNMENT, not win probability.
// Educational tool, not financial advice. Backtest before live use.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// v3.2.2 — THIRD-PASS AUDIT (THIRD_PASS_AUDIT_v3.2.1_BN.md). Defect repairs and
//          execution realism. Every item below changes BEHAVIOUR on purpose.
//   FIX-T1  POI INVALIDATION IS ANCHOR-SPECIFIC. `zoneInvalidatedUp/Dn` fires
//           when ANY zone in the registry is closed through, including one far
//           from the setup. It killed valid setups anchored on a different
//           zone, reporting only "POI violated". The machine now tests the
//           anchor it actually tapped.
//   FIX-T2  THE TRACKER NO LONGER SWALLOWS SIGNALS. A non-SB trade had no time
//           stop, so one stuck trade blocked tracking of every later entry AND
//           reset that entry's machine stage to IDLE. Trades now expire
//           (maxTrdBars), an untracked entry keeps its ACTIVE stage and is
//           counted separately, and a non-SB runner to TP3 is available.
//   FIX-T3  NON-SB ARM GRACE. Only the SB path could arm after the sweep bar;
//           a blocker on that single bar threw the whole sweep away. `armGrace`
//           gives the ordinary path the same once-per-sweep grace.
//   FIX-T4  `shiftSrc`: a bare CISD is no longer a structural shift by default.
//           CISD stays an execution confirmation, which is what it is.
//   FIX-T5  `poiSrc`: the OTE band alone no longer counts as a POI tap, so a
//           setup can no longer reach stage 2 with no anchor at all.
//   FIX-T6  THE HTF PROTECTED SWING IS USED. `hsProtLo / hsProtHi` were fetched
//           and never read. A close through the HTF protected swing now
//           invalidates an opposing setup (htfProtInval).
//   FIX-T7  ENGINE ENTRIES CAN FILL AT THE NEXT BAR'S OPEN (engFillMode). The
//           bar close is a price no order was placed at; the machine already
//           uses its plan price, the engines did not.
//   FIX-T8  CE FILL MODEL (sbFillMode). A resting limit at the CE fills on the
//           TOUCH. Requiring a close through it hid every fill that failed
//           immediately, which biased the counters. Default is the realistic
//           touch model; the slippage guard is waived for a CE limit fill,
//           because the touch itself proves price traded there.
//   FIX-T9  `sbPdPos` is clamped like `pdPos`, and a price outside the frozen
//           SB range falls back to the live reading instead of extrapolating.
//   FIX-T10 The CHOP bypass needs a RAW strong sweep AND an MSS, not the
//           effective grade the MSS itself wrote.
//   FIX-T12 REGIME. A live XAU M5 chart delivering 4,250 -> 4,400 read "CHOP"
//           and refused an A-tier long. Chop now needs three agreeing facts —
//           a narrow external pivot range, a narrow CURRENT LEG, and price
//           still inside that pivot range — and REGIME_LOOK is an input (40),
//           because 30 bars is shorter than the interval between external
//           events when extPiv = 12 on M5.
//   FIX-T13 QUIET DEFAULTS. The CISD / IFC chips and the LT "armed" chips print
//           on most bars, bury the candles and exhaust the 500-label budget.
//           They are context marks, not signals: default OFF (display only).
//   FIX-T14 The HTF bias is checked where a setup is BORN, not only at the last
//           gate. A short chain used to walk the whole sequence under a bullish
//           bias and die at the end, while the dashboard advertised "AT POI".
//   FIX-T15 The NEXT BUY / SELL label sits at the LEFT end of its line — the
//           middle is still underneath the dashboard table at a normal zoom.
//   FIX-T16 A 6-ATR NET MOVE IS NEVER CHOP. External pivots confirm too slowly
//           on M5 to keep a vertical leg in TRENDING, and the ATR that leg
//           inflates made both ranges look narrow — BTC and USTEC printed CHOP
//           at new highs. Price now gets a vote (`regThrust`).
//   FIX-T17 ONE chop exemption: arming read the EFFECTIVE sweep grade while the
//           final gate demanded RAW + MSS, so chop produced "SWEPT" setups that
//           could never fire. `instSweepL/S` is now read at both ends.
//   FIX-T18 A reachable score: a NEUTRAL HTF bias scores 5 instead of 0, and
//           `minScore` defaults to 50. Calibrate it in the Strategy Tester —
//           the dashboard's idle-bar number is not what a signal scores.
//   FIX-T19 `minZoneAtr` (0.15): a 0.05-ATR band is not a POI.
//   FIX-T20 A demand and a supply may no longer share a band. The newer zone
//           wins it; the older keeps its own side or is dropped.
//   TOKEN   v3.2.2 first build hit the Pine compiled-token ceiling (102,145 of
//           100,256 — error CE10117). The engine was untouched; the budget came
//           out of PRESENTATION and duplicated helpers: the dashboard and debug
//           tables were rebuilt with merged rows, the SB alert() feed shortened,
//           the five HTF-bias security calls became two (the selected bias TF
//           and D), the session-minute parser is called once per window instead
//           of twice, the zone 50% companion LINE was dropped (the 50% TAP
//           logic is unchanged — it was only ever a drawn line), and two
//           display-only features were removed: the order-flow zigzag
//           (`showOF`, default OFF) and the 00:00 / 08:30 opening lines.
//           No detection, gate, tier, score or exit rule changed.
//   FIX-T11 Freshness only outranks distance inside a distance window, the A+
//           premium/discount test is waived on a range too tight to have one,
//           LT-7 requires a chart-TF supply zone, and relVol uses a sane floor.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// v3.3 — FOURTH-PASS AUDIT (FOURTH_PASS_AUDIT_v3.2.2_BN.md · UPGRADE_PLAN_v3.3_BN.md)
//        "One direction · SB fast path". Gate repairs; no engine rewritten.
//   FIX-V1  NEUTRAL BIAS IS A DECISION. `htfB == 0` used to pass both `>= 0` and
//           `<= 0`, so every bias gate opened both ways. `neutralMode` (block /
//           follow chart structure / allow) resolves to ONE `biasOkL / biasOkS`
//           that every consumer reads — arm, final gate, trap gates, SB entry.
//   FIX-V2  MASTER DIRECTION LOCK (`oneDir`). An open trade or a setup at POI or
//           beyond owns the chart: the opposite side may not arm, fire or confirm
//           a trap. Machine + tracker state moved into MODULE 15A (was 16A) so the
//           lock reads the previous bar. A quality raid of the OTHER side kills
//           a stage-1 setup ("range swept both ways").
//   FIX-V3  FIX-T20 for EVERY zone: `f_claimBand` runs for OB, RB, IFVG and after
//           breaker / mitigation flips (a live BTC chart had demand top == supply
//           OB bottom). Flip claims run after the loop — index-safe.
//   FIX-V4  `poiConflict`: next demand and next supply within one ATR = no POI
//           points either side, no machine tap, verdict says why.
//   FIX-V5  Trap narrative gate: no trap against a TRENDING external structure
//           without a RAW strong raid; counter-trend −20, fighting the OTE −15.
//   FIX-V6  THE TRAP ENGINE IS AN ENGINE CANDIDATE. `sigLTLong/Short` pass
//           MODULE 18 (tier · score · cooldown · lock · SB-13 hierarchy) and
//           18B (risk cap · min RR), carry their own stop, print in the merged
//           label with E/SL/TP lines and are tracked. Raw `sslSweep/bslSweep`
//           are no longer a confirmation (the retest bar confirmed itself);
//           confirmation must land AFTER the retest bar; one signal per level.
//   FIX-V7  An SB entry outranks the same-direction cooldown (SB-13 rank 3).
//   FIX-V8  A final-gate mute names its gate (`gate: …`) and returns the setup
//           to SHIFTED for a retry instead of silently spending the sweep.
//   FIX-V9  SB SHIFT = LTF MODEL (`sbShiftSrc`): internal CHoCH / CISD cut by a
//           displacement candle (`sbDispAtr`); the raid IS the POI, so a tagged
//           setup goes stage 1 → 3 on the shift. Non-SB path unchanged.
//   FIX-V10 One SB entry per window per direction.
//   FIX-V11 An engineered pool in play keeps its KIND through a nearby swing sweep.
//   FIX-V12 Zone text only on ★HP / kind tags; rejection blocks capped at 2/side.
//   FIX-V13 (v3.3.1) An HTF gap taller than `hPoiNextMaxAtr` × ATR cannot be the
//           NEXT level (a 2,900-pt 240m gap hijacked NEXT BUY on BTC 15m), and
//           the POI-conflict test only compares chart-TF zones.
//   FIX-V14 (v3.3.1) `fastShift`: the ordinary machine may go SWEPT → SHIFTED on
//           a displaced shift while the sweep is in play (2022 model), anchoring
//           on the shift-leg FVG — it no longer dies waiting for a POI tap.
//   Budget  plot 8 + plotchar 14 + bgcolor 5 + alertcondition 35 = 62 of 64.
//
// v3.4 — FIFTH-PASS AUDIT (REVIEW_AND_PLAN_v3.3.1.md). Four live M5 charts
//        (XAUUSD / BTCUSDT / NAS100USD / USOIL) all sat at "almost": a setup at
//        POI that could never score, and a verdict naming the wrong gate. The
//        doctrine layer was not the problem — the plumbing between a setup and
//        its own score was. No new engine; no new output slot.
//   FIX-W1  ONE SWEEP EVENT PER LEVEL (OP-1). `lastSup` only changes when a new
//           pivot confirms, so `sslSweep` was true on EVERY bar that dipped
//           under the same swing low and closed back above it. The stamp moved
//           forward each of those bars, which (a) kept the sweep eternally
//           "fresh" so `sslInPlay` never aged out, (b) defeated FIX-T3 /
//           SB-9's once-per-sweep arm guards — they compare against
//           `lastSslBar`, which was no longer fixed — and (c) let a limp
//           re-poke rewrite the grade the real raid earned. A level now
//           produces one event; it re-arms on a new pivot or after price has
//           travelled SWEEP_CTX_ATR × ATR away. Same latch for PDH / PDL,
//           which are one price all day (FIX-W1b).
//   FIX-W2  THE SCORE GRADES THE SETUP'S OWN POI (OP-2). `poiLong` / `retestLong`
//           read the NEXT-ZONE marker, but a live setup's anchor is
//           `suLAnchorT/B` — and after FIX-V14 that anchor is a shift-leg FVG
//           which is not in the `zones` registry at all. The entry bar lost up
//           to 20 points and failed `minScore`, so FIX-V8 handed the stage back
//           and it died on the retest clock. From stage 2 the score reads the
//           anchor. MODULE 16A moved above the score module (now 15A) to make
//           that possible; every write still happens in MODULE 17 / 19.
//   FIX-W3  A POI CONFLICT IS RESOLVED, NOT MUTUALLY KILLED (OP-3). FIX-V4
//           zeroed both sides whenever the two POIs were within one ATR — the
//           NORMAL geometry inside a balanced range. A live NAS100 chart could
//           not reach `minScore` in either direction. The side that agrees with
//           the bias (else the direction lock, else external structure) keeps
//           its points; only a genuine tie is still a mutual kill.
//   FIX-W4  THE VERDICT NAMES THE ACTIVE SIDE'S OWN BLOCKER (OP-4). The
//           `dirLock` branch was tested first, so the chart said "SHORT
//           narrative live · AT POI" while that short was refused on its own
//           score. The lock is now a suffix, not the verdict.
//   FIX-W5  The MSS institutional upgrade must belong to the raid it broke from
//           (OP-5): the shift has to land within TRIG_LOOK bars of the sweep.
//   FIX-W6  `mcFillMode` — the standard machine entry is confirmed by a CLOSED
//           candle, so it fills at the next open like an engine signal (OP-6).
//           The SB CE limit is a resting order and is untouched.
//   FIX-W7  A cancelled pending order is booked as untracked (OP-7).
//   FIX-W8  The trap engine may not arm on a `na` level (OP-8).
//   FIX-W9  EQ pools expire (`eqMaxAge`) — four-hour-old "engineered liquidity"
//           was still clearing `sbPoolPref` (OP-9).
//   FIX-W10 A session level can be raided more than once: the latch clears once
//           price has walked a full ATR off it (OP-10).
//   FIX-W11 A closed trade's ladder comes off the chart (OP-14).
//   FIX-W12 NEXT BUY / SELL labels are staggered and the line is short (OP-13).
//   FIX-W13 FOCUS MODE — a drawing budget. Boxes further than `focusAtr` × ATR
//           from price go transparent (state untouched), and the two full-width
//           washes are off. Display only (OP-12).
//   FIX-W14 The dashboard's NEXT rows show DISTANCE, not a price range the chart
//           label already carries (OP-15). FIX-W15 ADR says "(early)" (OP-16).
//   FIX-W16 RISK GOVERNANCE — daily signal limit and daily loss limit, counted
//           from tracked entries, enforced at the final gate (OP-17).
//   FIX-W17 SYMBOL PROFILE — FX / Metals / Indices / Crypto preset the nine
//           ATR-scaled constants. "Manual" = v3.3 (OP-18). STARTING POINTS.
//   FIX-W18 Spread + commission (`costTicks`) enter the min-RR gate and the
//           printed RR. The drawn SL / TP levels do not move (OP-19).
//   FIX-W19 One machine-readable journal line per signal on the alert() feed
//           (OP-20).
//   TOKEN   The first v3.4 build hit the compiled-token ceiling again — 104,324
//           of 100,256 (CE10117), the same wall v3.2.2 hit. As in v3.2.2 the
//           budget came out of PRESENTATION, not the engine. Nothing in this
//           list changes a gate, a score, a tier, a zone or a signal:
//             · the journal line, the SB alert() feed and the two LT alert()
//               messages carry the same facts in far fewer concatenations;
//             · the symbol profile resolves through ONE integer (`profIdx`)
//               instead of ~45 option-string comparisons;
//             · the debug table lost its Context row (the dashboard already
//               shows the regime) and merged LT + SB + the day book into one;
//             · ~47 tooltips were cut to the sentence that decides something;
//             · REMOVED DRAWINGS: the premium / discount wash, the OTE box and
//               the two wick-cluster pool lines. The MATH behind all three is
//               untouched — `pdPos`, `rangeHolds`, `inOTEbuy` / `inOTEsell`,
//               `p3PoolLow` and `p4PoolHi` still drive the context score, the
//               A+ premium/discount test, the SB entry rule and LT-3 / LT-4.
//               `showFlow`, `showRange` and `ltShowPools` are gone with them.
//             · Box captions (HTF POI, machine anchor, NEXT level) shortened.
//   OP-21   DONE — `liqudity_trap_strategy.txt`, GENERATED by scripts/mktwin.py
//           from this file, so the detection code is identical by construction.
//           Re-run that script after every change here; never hand-edit it.
//   OPEN    The calibration protocol (REVIEW_AND_PLAN_v3.3.1.md §4). Until the
//           twin has actually been run, `minScore`, `minRR`, `maxRiskAtr`, the
//           governance limits and the four symbol profiles are reasoned
//           defaults — opinions, not measurements.
indicator("Liquidity + S/D + Price Action [MTF] v3.5", "LQ-SD-PA v3.5", overlay = true,
     max_boxes_count = 500, max_lines_count = 500, max_labels_count = 500)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 1
// INPUTS
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
grpG = "General"
pivLen     = input.int(5,     "Internal pivot length",                 minval = 2,  group = grpG)
extPivIn   = input.int(12,    "External pivot length (major swings)",  minval = 5,  group = grpG)
atrLen     = input.int(14,    "ATR length",                            minval = 1,  group = grpG)
biasLen    = input.int(50,    "Bias EMA length (MTF table)",           minval = 5,  group = grpG)
showLevels = input.bool(true, "Show last swing high/low key levels",   group = grpG)
// ◆ FIX-W17 (v3.4, OP-18) SYMBOL PROFILE. Every ATR-scaled constant in this
// file was tuned on M5 spot FX. The same numbers on gold, an index and crypto
// do not describe the same market: the four live charts reviewed were run on
// identical settings and produced CHOP on NAS100 and RANGING on BTCUSDT within
// the same hour. A profile presets the nine sensitive constants; "Manual"
// leaves every input exactly where you set it (and is v3.3 behaviour).
// THESE ARE STARTING POINTS, NOT TRUTH — calibrate them in the Strategy Tester.
symProfile = input.string("Manual", "Symbol profile",
     options = ["Manual", "FX majors", "Metals / XAU", "Indices", "Crypto"], group = grpG,
     tooltip = "Overrides the external pivot, min zone height, displacement body and the six Advanced constants. The profile wins unless it is Manual.")
// TOKEN: the profile resolves to ONE integer here; every constant below then
// compares ints. The string form cost ~45 option-string comparisons and was
// part of what pushed the first v3.4 build over CE10117.
profIdx = symProfile == "Manual" ? 0 : symProfile == "FX majors" ? 1 : symProfile == "Metals / XAU" ? 2 : symProfile == "Indices" ? 3 : 4
profMan = profIdx == 0
extPiv  = profIdx == 3 ? 10 : profMan ? extPivIn : 12      // FIX-W17

grpH = "HTF context"
biasFilter = input.string("60", "HTF bias filter — block counter-trend entries",
     options = ["Off", "5", "15", "60", "240", "D"], group = grpH,
     tooltip = "The last CLOSED candle of this TF must agree. Off = scored, never blocking.")
// ◆ FIX-F1 (v3.2.1 phase C) HTF STRUCTURE BIAS. The EMA-50 read is a moving
// average — lagging and whipsaw-prone, and not how this engine defines bias
// anywhere else. The same protected-swing structure engine (BOS / CHoCH) now
// also runs on the bias timeframe and can be the bias, alone or agreeing with
// the EMA. Disagreement = neutral (0): nothing is blocked, nothing is rewarded.
biasMode = input.string("Structure + EMA", "HTF bias source",
     options = ["EMA only", "Structure only", "Structure + EMA"], group = grpH,
     tooltip = "Structure only falls back to the EMA before the first HTF break. Both = agreement required, else neutral.")
htfStructPiv = input.int(5, "HTF structure pivot length (HTF bars)", minval = 2, maxval = 20, group = grpH,
     tooltip = "Swing size on the bias timeframe. 5 on 1H = five-hour swings.")
// ◆ FIX-T6: the HTF protected swing was fetched and never used. A close through
// it on the bias timeframe means the HTF narrative the setup leans on is gone.
htfProtInval = input.bool(true, "HTF protected swing invalidates a setup", group = grpH,
     tooltip = "A long dies on a close below the HTF protected LOW (short: above the protected HIGH).")
// ◆ FIX-V1 (v3.3, audit F-1): a NEUTRAL bias used to pass BOTH `htfB >= 0` and
// `htfB <= 0`, so every bias gate opened both ways whenever structure and EMA
// disagreed — the single biggest source of a long and a short on one chart.
neutralMode = input.string("Follow chart structure", "When the HTF bias is NEUTRAL",
     options = ["Block both sides", "Follow chart structure", "Allow both (v3.2.2)"], group = grpH,
     tooltip = "Block = no entry either way. Follow = use the chart's external structure. Allow = v3.2.2.")
useHtfCrt = input.bool(true, "HTF CRT + Turtle-soup trigger candidates", group = grpH)
htfSel    = input.string("Auto", "HTF for CRT / TBS / POI", options = ["Auto", "15", "30", "60", "240", "D"], group = grpH)
hPoiMaxAge   = input.int(40, "HTF POI max age (HTF candles)",           minval = 5, group = grpH)
// ◆ FIX-N6 (LOGIC-N5): freshness is counted in SEPARATE VISITS (leave + return),
// the same definition the chart-TF zone engine uses — not "HTF candles inside",
// which let a three-candle consolidation spend a POI without ever leaving it.
hPoiMaxTouch = input.int(2,  "HTF POI spent after N separate visits",  minval = 1, group = grpH,
     tooltip = "A visit = price enters the POI after being outside it. 3 live POIs per side; the nearest untested one is used.")

grpL = "Liquidity"
showSweeps = input.bool(true,  "Mark liquidity sweeps (◆ / ◈)",         group = grpL)
sweepLook  = input.int(12,     "Sweep stays in play (bars)", minval = 1, group = grpL,
     tooltip = "Losing the reclaim ends the sweep regardless of this clock.")
showEq     = input.bool(true,  "Track equal highs / lows (EQH / EQL)",  group = grpL)
eqTol      = input.float(0.15, "Equal level tolerance (ATR ×)", step = 0.05, minval = 0.05, group = grpL)
// ◆ FIX-W9 (v3.4, OP-9): an EQ pool was removed only when it was swept or when
// MAX_EQ_POOLS overflowed, so a pool engineered four hours ago still counted as
// "engineered liquidity" (kind 2) — which is what `sbPoolPref` gates every
// Silver Bullet arm on. Old liquidity is history, not a target.
eqMaxAge   = input.int(240, "EQ pool expires after N bars", minval = 20, group = grpL,
     tooltip = "The pool stops being tracked at this age even if it was never taken.")
minArmQual = input.int(2,      "Min sweep quality to arm a setup (1–4)", minval = 1, maxval = 4, group = grpL,
     tooltip = "1 weak (reclaimed, shallow) · 2 valid (firm reclaim) · 3 strong (+outer-half close & displacement body) · 4 institutional (strong + structure shift while in play).")
showPdwl   = input.bool(true,  "Previous day / week high–low lines",    group = grpL)

grpM = "Structure"
showStruct = input.bool(true,  "Label BOS / CHoCH / MSS",               group = grpM)
structDisp = input.float(0.5,  "Structural break body ≥ ATR × (0 = any close)", step = 0.1, minval = 0, group = grpM)
showProt   = input.bool(true,  "Plot protected swing (external)",       group = grpM)
// ◆ FIX-N5 (LOGIC-N3): MSS is an EXTERNAL structure event by default. The
// chart already draws an internal CHoCH as a muted grey "iCHoCH" (minor), yet
// `mssUp` treated it exactly like the external one — a 5-bar wobble plus a
// sweep became MSS grade, trigger score 12, quality upgrade 4 and SB shift
// grade 2. Display and logic now agree.
mssNeedExt = input.bool(true, "MSS requires an EXTERNAL CHoCH (internal = CISD grade)", group = grpM,
     tooltip = "ON (doctrine): only an EXTERNAL displaced break while a sweep is in play is an MSS.")

grpF = "FVG"
showFvg    = input.bool(true, "Draw live chart-TF FVGs",                group = grpF)
fvgMaxKeep = input.int(4,     "Max live FVGs per side", minval = 1, maxval = 10, group = grpF)
fvgMaxAge  = input.int(120,   "FVG max age (bars)", minval = 10, group = grpF)
// ◆ FIX-F3 (v3.2.1 phase C) INVERTED FVG. A bull FVG that price CLOSES through
// used to be deleted. In ICT it becomes an IFVG — the same gap now acting as
// supply. The registry flips it (box recolours, text "IFVG") and the zone
// engine receives it as a POI of kind "IFVG", so NEXT SELL, the machine's POI
// tap and the retest score can all use it. A wick-only fill still just clears
// the gap. A second violation deletes it.
useIfvg    = input.bool(true, "Flip violated FVGs into IFVG (inverted) POIs", group = grpF)

grpZ = "Supply / Demand / Order blocks"
showZones  = input.bool(true,   "Show zones",                           group = grpZ)

dispFactor = profIdx == 3 ? 1.1 : profMan ? dispFactIn : 1.2      // FIX-W17
maxZoneAtr = input.float(2.0,   "Max zone height (ATR ×)",   step = 0.5, minval = 0.5, group = grpZ)
maxZones   = input.int(6,       "Max zones per side", minval = 1, maxval = 20, group = grpZ)
showOB     = input.bool(true,   "Also mark the ORDER BLOCK origin candle", group = grpZ,
     tooltip = "The opposing candle before the displacement leg, when its range is distinct from the imbalance.")
confirmWin = input.int(5,       "Max bars from 50% tap to confirmation", minval = 1, maxval = 20, group = grpZ)
showFlip   = input.bool(true,   "Flip violated zones into breaker / mitigation blocks", group = grpZ)
showRB     = input.bool(true,   "Rejection blocks (swept pivot wick + displacement away)", group = grpZ)
// ◆ FIX-T19: a live ETHUSD chart offered "NEXT BUY 2731.89–2732.25" — a band
// 0.05 ATR tall. A zone that thin is not a point of interest: its 50% and its
// edges are the same price, the stop behind it is noise and the RR built on it
// is fiction. Zones under this height are never created.
minZoneIn    = input.float(0.15, "Min zone height (ATR ×)", step = 0.05, minval = 0, group = grpZ,
     tooltip = "0 = v3.2.1 (any height). Raise on symbols where one-tick gaps are common.")
minZoneAtr   = profMan ? minZoneIn : profIdx == 1 ? 0.15 : profIdx == 4 ? 0.25 : 0.20      // FIX-W17
zoneMaxTests = input.int(2,     "Zone spent after N separate tests", minval = 1, maxval = 6, group = grpZ,
     tooltip = "Each separate visit (leave + return) consumes the zone.")

grpNX = "Next buy / sell level"
showNextLvl  = input.bool(true, "Show NEXT BUY / NEXT SELL level", group = grpNX,
     tooltip = "Where the engine is WAITING. A location marker, not a signal — the chain still has to run.")
nextLvlAnchor = input.string("Proximal edge", "Line sits on", options = ["Proximal edge", "Zone 50% (CE)"], group = grpNX,
     tooltip = "Proximal = the first price touched. 50% = the CE, where the machine's limit logic works.")

nextLvlPos   = input.string("Left end of the line", "Label position",
     options = ["Left end of the line", "Right edge"], group = grpNX,
     tooltip = "Right edge puts the label where the dashboard table sits and the two overlap.")
// ◆ FIX-F6: the HTF FVG POI competes for "next level" too — it is often the
// more important target than any chart-TF zone, and the machine can anchor on it.
nextInclHtf  = input.bool(true, "Include the HTF FVG POI as a NEXT level candidate", group = grpNX,
     tooltip = "The nearest untested HTF FVG competes with the chart zones; the closer one wins.")
// ◆ FIX-V13 (v3.3.1): a live BTC 15m chart showed "NEXT BUY T3 HTF 81878–84778"
// — a 240-min gap 2,900 points (3.4%) tall. Its top sat above every chart
// demand, so it hijacked the NEXT level with price permanently "INSIDE", and it
// overlapped the chart-TF supply above price, which read as a POI conflict.
// A zone that tall is a RANGE, not a point of interest on this timeframe.
hPoiNextMaxAtr = input.float(4.0, "HTF POI may be the NEXT level only if height ≤ ATR ×", step = 0.5, minval = 1, group = grpNX,
     tooltip = "A taller HTF gap still feeds FEM and the HTF tap; it just cannot be the NEXT marker.")

grpC = "CRT / Turtle soup / CISD / IFC"
showCRT    = input.bool(true,  "CRT 3-candle trigger candidates",       group = grpC)
crtRangeAtr = input.float(1.2, "CRT min candle-1 range (ATR ×)", step = 0.1, minval = 0.5, group = grpC)
crtDispAtr  = input.float(0.5, "CRT min candle-3 body (ATR ×)",  step = 0.1, minval = 0.1, group = grpC)
showTBS    = input.bool(true,  "Turtle soup trigger candidates",        group = grpC)

     tooltip = "An extreme older than this is stale liquidity — its sweep no longer traps a fresh breakout crowd.")
// ◆ FIX-T13 QUIET BY DEFAULT. On M5 the CISD / IFC chips print on most bars and
// bury the chart (and burn the 500-label budget) — they are context marks, not

showCisd   = input.bool(false, "Label CISD (change in state of delivery)", group = grpC)

showIFC    = input.bool(false, "Mark IFC candles (pool grab + displacement)", group = grpC)

grpB = "Inside bar / Doji (context-gated)"
showS3     = input.bool(true,  "Inside Bar Breakout trigger",           group = grpB)

     tooltip = "0 = same bar only. The sweep must still be in play, and each sweep arms one setup.")

// ◆ FIX-T5: OTE is a price area, not a point of interest. Tapping it used to

     options = ["Zone / HTF POI only", "Zone / HTF POI + OTE"], group = grpQ)
// ◆ FIX-T14: the HTF bias filter only ran at the FINAL gate, so a setup facing

armNeedHtf = input.bool(true, "Machine: do not arm against the HTF bias", group = grpQ,
     tooltip = "ON: check the bias where the setup is BORN, not only at the last gate.")
// ◆ FIX-V2 (v3.3, audit F-2): the two machines were independent, so a long at
// POI and a short arming three bars later was legal. One narrative at a time.
oneDir = input.bool(true, "One direction at a time (machine · engines · traps)", group = grpQ,
     tooltip = "While a trade is open or a setup is AT POI or beyond, the opposite side is locked out.")
// ◆ FIX-V14 (v3.3.1): the ordinary chain was sweep → POI → shift → retest, in
// that order only. A live BTC 5m chart swept the high, printed a displaced
// external CHoCH and fell 2,000 points — and the machine sat in SWEPT waiting
// for a supply tap that never came ("sweep expired (no POI tap)"). Sweep →
// displacement shift → retest of the shift-leg FVG is the 2022 model; the raid
// IS the POI. Same rule the SB path already uses (FIX-V9b).
fastShift = input.bool(true, "Machine: a displaced shift while the sweep is in play skips the POI stage", group = grpQ,
     tooltip = "ON: sweep → shift anchors on the shift-leg FVG (2022 model). OFF: a POI tap must come first.")
// ◆ FIX-X3 THE ORDINARY MACHINE PATH GETS THE CE TOO.
// The SB path places a resting limit at the consequent encroachment (the 50%
// of its locked FVG); the ordinary path — which is where most entries come
// from — could only ever take the PROXIMAL EDGE of the same anchor, on a
// closed confirmation candle. Same anchor, same stop under the distal edge,
// but the entry sat a full gap-height worse: roughly double the risk distance
// and therefore half the RR, on identical structure. With an FVG anchor the
// machine now rests a limit at the CE as well, and keeps the confirmation
// candle as the fallback for a gap price never reaches.
mcEntryMode = input.string("CE preferred + confirmation fallback", "Machine (non-SB) entry rule",
     options = ["Proximal edge + confirmation (v3.4)", "CE preferred + confirmation fallback", "CE (50%) only"],
     group = grpQ,
     tooltip = "CE = 50% of the locked shift-leg FVG. A zone / OTE / HTF anchor has no CE and always uses the confirmation candle.")
// ◆ FIX-X5 PREMIUM / DISCOUNT IS A RULE, NOT A TIE-BREAK.
// Only the SB path checked it (`sbReqPD`) and only the A+ tier scored it — with
// the tier filter at its default "A and above", the ordinary machine path was
// free to buy in premium and sell in discount, which is the one thing the model
// it implements never does. The gate applies to a dealing range wide enough to
// mean something (≥ 2 ATR, the FIX-T11 test), and can be waived when the HTF
// bias and external structure BOTH agree — in a genuine trend the discount half
// may never trade again.
scoreOnBase = input.bool(true, "Min score judges the BASE alignment (SB context bonus excluded)", group = grpQ,
     tooltip = "The label still shows the full score. OFF restores v3.4, where the SB bonus could carry a weak bar over the threshold.")

     tooltip = "Also demands a close in the candle's outer 30%. OFF restores v3.4's bare pin / engulf / IFC / CISD test.")
mcReqPD  = input.bool(true,  "Machine: entries must be on the correct side of the dealing range", group = grpQ)
mcPdWaive = input.bool(true, "… waived when HTF bias and external structure both agree", group = grpQ,
     tooltip = "A one-way trend rarely returns to its own discount; this keeps continuation entries legal.")

     tooltip = "Liquidity target: next opposite POI / key level when it pays ≥ TP2, else 3R. RR is a plan, never a guarantee.")
rrMult    = input.float(2.0, "TP2 R multiple", step = 0.5, minval = 0.5, group = grpR)
// ◆ FIX-P1-7: the machine anchors its stop on the SWEEP WICK, which can sit 20
// bars away. That made R enormous, TP1 unreachable and the break-even shift
// dead. Risk is capped, and a trade whose structural stop cannot fit inside
// the cap is REJECTED rather than quietly re-priced into a worse plan.
maxRiskAtr = input.float(1.5, "Max risk (ATR ×, 0 = off)", step = 0.25, minval = 0, group = grpR,
     tooltip = "With Reject on, a setup needing a wider stop is dropped — you are late to it.")
riskMode   = input.string("Reject the signal", "When the structural stop is wider than the cap",
     options = ["Reject the signal", "Clamp the stop"], group = grpR)
// ◆ FIX-X4 TWO STRUCTURAL STOPS, NOT ONE STOP AND A CLAMP.
// The machine always anchored its stop on the SWEEP WICK. After `fastShift`
// that wick can be twenty bars and several ATR away, so the plan blew through
// the risk cap and was then either rejected or CLAMPED — and a clamped stop
// sits at "entry − 1.5 ATR", a price with no structure behind it, which is
// precisely the price that gets run. The retest anchor's own distal edge is a
// real level: if the raid stop does not fit the cap, the ANCHOR stop is tried
// before the cap logic is reached, and only a plan that fails both is clamped
// or rejected.
stopTier   = input.bool(true, "Stop: fall back to the anchor stop when the sweep-wick stop breaks the risk cap", group = grpR,
     tooltip = "OFF restores v3.4 (always the sweep wick, then clamp / reject).")
minRR      = input.float(1.5, "Min RR to TP2 (0 = off)", step = 0.25, minval = 0, group = grpR)
// ◆ FIX-W18 (v3.4, OP-19) COSTS ARE PART OF THE PLAN. Every RR on this chart
// was gross: spread and commission were not in it. On XAU M5 a 25-30 cent
// round trip turns a "1.5R" plan into roughly 1.2R, and the `minRR` gate was
// letting exactly those through. The cost widens the risk and shortens the
// reward for the GATE only — the drawn SL / TP levels stay where the structure
// put them, because that is where the orders go.
costTicks  = input.float(0, "Round-trip cost (ticks: spread + commission)", step = 1, minval = 0, group = grpR,
     tooltip = "Your spread plus commission, in ticks. Applied to the min-RR gate and the printed RR.")
// ◆ FIX-P0-4: entry price = the plan price (locked FVG CE / anchor edge), not
// the bar close. The guard drops a fill the bar ran too far past to be real.
slipGuard  = input.float(0.5, "Max distance close → plan entry (ATR ×, 0 = off)", step = 0.1, minval = 0, group = grpR,
     tooltip = "If the bar closed far past the plan price the fill is fiction. Waived for a CE limit touch — the touch is the proof.")
// ◆ FIX-T7: an engine signal is detected at the CLOSE of its bar, so the
// earliest real fill is the NEXT bar's open. Pricing it at the close flatters

     options = ["Next bar open (realistic)", "Bar close (v3.2.1)"], group = grpR,
     tooltip = "Engine signals only. The label prints on the signal bar; the tracked fill and its TP ladder come from the next open.")
// ◆ FIX-W6 (v3.4, OP-6): the STANDARD machine entry is confirmed by a CLOSED
// candle (`zoneBullConf`), so like an engine signal the earliest price a real

// ◆ FIX-F4 (v3.2.1 phase C) ADR EXHAUSTION. A day that has already travelled

// ◆ FIX-W16 (v3.4, OP-17) RISK GOVERNANCE. The engine graded every setup and
// had no concept of the operator's day: no trade count, no loss count, no stop.
// A desk's first filter is "how many times have I been wrong today" — the
// second and third loss of a session are statistically the worst trades most

// ◆ FIX-W13 (v3.4, OP-12) A DRAWING BUDGET. At defaults the last forty bars
// carry: up to 6 zones per side + OB companions + 2 rejection blocks per side +
// IFVGs + 4 FVG boxes per side + 2 HTF POI boxes + the NEXT buy / sell boxes,
// lines and labels + the SB window box + two order-flow rectangles + the OTE
// box + the trade ladder. Every live chart reviewed was unreadable where it
// matters most. Focus mode keeps the drawings within `focusAtr` of price and
// drops the two full-width washes; nothing about DETECTION changes — the zone,
// FVG and range engines run exactly as before, they are just not all painted.
focusMode = input.bool(true, "Focus mode — draw only what is near price", group = grpU,
     tooltip = "OFF = draw everything. Display only.")
focusAtr  = input.float(4.0, "Focus distance (ATR ×)", step = 0.5, minval = 1, group = grpU)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 2
// CONSTANTS + SMALL UTILITIES
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ◆ FIX-N12 (v3.2.1 phase B): the six symbol- and timeframe-sensitive
// constants are INPUTS now, grouped under "Advanced tuning". Defaults are the
// old values, so an untouched chart behaves exactly as before. Everything that
// is genuinely a definition (strong-close %, CISD neutral %) stays a constant.
grpADV = "Advanced tuning (defaults = v3.2 behaviour · ignored unless the profile is Manual)"
nearLvlIn  = input.float(0.25, "'At the level' tolerance (ATR ×)",       step = 0.05, minval = 0.05, group = grpADV,
     tooltip = "Doji-at-level tests, trendline touch distance, sweep 'firm reclaim' half-distance.")
slPadIn    = input.float(0.25, "Stop padding beyond structure (ATR ×)",  step = 0.05, minval = 0,    group = grpADV,
     tooltip = "Raise on wide-spread symbols so the stop is not sitting on the wick.")
sweepCtxIn = input.float(3.0,  "Sweep stays in play within (ATR ×)",     step = 0.5,  minval = 1,    group = grpADV,
     tooltip = "How far price may travel from the swept level before the sweep stops counting as live. Volatile symbols may need 4–5.")
poiNearIn  = input.float(1.5,  "'Near the POI' distance (ATR ×)",        step = 0.25, minval = 0.5,  group = grpADV)
trigLookIn = input.int(10,     "Shift / CISD stays a live trigger (bars)", minval = 3, maxval = 30,   group = grpADV,
     tooltip = "10 suits M5. On M15 try 6–8; on M1 12–15.")
// FIX-W17: the profile resolves to the SAME names the whole file already uses,
// so not one call site changes — only where the number comes from.
//                      FX      XAU     Index   Crypto
NEAR_LVL_ATR  = profMan ? nearLvlIn  : profIdx == 1 ? 0.25 : profIdx == 4 ? 0.35 : 0.30
SL_PAD_ATR    = profMan ? slPadIn    : profIdx == 1 ? 0.25 : profIdx == 2 ? 0.35 : profIdx == 3 ? 0.30 : 0.40
SWEEP_CTX_ATR = profMan ? sweepCtxIn : profIdx == 1 ? 3.0  : profIdx == 4 ? 5.0  : 4.0
POI_NEAR_ATR  = profMan ? poiNearIn  : profIdx == 1 ? 1.5  : profIdx == 4 ? 2.0  : 1.75
TRIG_LOOK     = profMan ? trigLookIn : profIdx == 3 ? 8    : profIdx == 4 ? 12   : 10
var float STRONG_CLOSE_PCT = 0.30 // close in outer 30% of candle = strong close
var float CISD_NEUTRAL_PCT = 0.10 // body ≤ 10% of range = neutral candle
var int   IFC_LOOK       = 3     // prior bars whose extreme an IFC must take
var float BRK_BODY_ATR   = 0.5   // min body for a meaningful break close
var int   IB_WAIT_BARS   = 4     // bars allowed for the inside-bar break
var int   TRADE_BARS     = 15    // length of entry/SL/TP lines
var int   DOJI_WINDOW    = 5     // lookback window for counting dojis
var int   MAX_EQ_POOLS   = 8     // max EQH/EQL pools per side
var float OTE_1          = 0.62
var float OTE_2          = 0.79
// ◆ FIX-T12: 30 bars of external-event memory is too short for `extPiv` = 12 on
// M5 — an external BOS only prints every few hours, so a running trend fell out
// of TRENDING and, with a narrow pivot pair, was classified CHOP.
REGIME_LOOK = input.int(40, "Regime: external-event memory (bars)", minval = 10, maxval = 200, group = grpADV,
     tooltip = "How long an external BOS / CHoCH keeps the regime TRENDING or TRANSITION. Raise it on fast timeframes (M5: 40–60) or when a clear trend keeps reading CHOP.")
chopRngIn = input.float(6.0, "CHOP: external range narrower than (ATR ×)", step = 0.5, minval = 2, group = grpADV,
     tooltip = "Pivot-to-pivot external range below this, with no recent external event, classifies the regime as CHOP.")
CHOP_RANGE_ATR = profMan ? chopRngIn : profIdx <= 2 ? 6.0 : 7.0   // FIX-W17
// v3.1.1 ◆ SB-only tuning constants (no effect on any non-SB path)
var int   SB_ARM_GRACE   = 5     // SB-9  bars after the sweep the SB path may still arm
var int   SB_MEM_EXTRA   = 5     // SB-10 extra bars of sweep-in-play memory inside an SB context
var float SB_BE_TP1_PCT  = 0.15  // SB-14 min break-even buffer as a share of the TP1 distance
var color COL_BULL       = #089981
var color COL_BEAR       = #f23645

mkSize   = uiMicroBig ? size.small : size.tiny
drawSize = uiDrawOpt == "Tiny" ? size.tiny : uiDrawOpt == "Normal" ? size.normal : uiDrawOpt == "Large" ? size.large : size.small
sigSize  = uiSizeOpt == "Tiny" ? size.tiny : uiSizeOpt == "Normal" ? size.normal : uiSizeOpt == "Large" ? size.large : size.small
dashSize = uiDashOpt == "Large" ? size.large : uiDashOpt == "Normal" ? size.normal : size.small

// chip = text on a solid pill — readable on any candle background
f_chip(bool below, string txt, color bg, color tc, string sz) =>
    label.new(bar_index, na, txt,
         yloc = below ? yloc.belowbar : yloc.abovebar,
         style = below ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down,
         color = bg, textcolor = tc, size = sz)

f_recent(int b, int look) => not na(b) and bar_index - b <= look

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 3
// CORE SERIES + CANDLE ANATOMY
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
atr     = ta.atr(atrLen)
body    = math.abs(close - open)
rng     = high - low
upWick  = high - math.max(open, close)
dnWick  = math.min(open, close) - low
bullBar = close > open
bearBar = close < open
confirmed = barstate.isconfirmed

// FIX-F2 relative volume. `volOK` is the displacement gate: true when volume
// confirmation is off, when the symbol has no volume, or when the candle
// carries volMult × its average. `volHot` is the sweep-grade bonus test.
relVol = na(volume) or volume <= 0 ? na : volume / math.max(ta.sma(volume, volLen), 1)   // FIX-T11 floor is a volume, not a tick size
volOK  = not useVol or na(relVol) or relVol >= volMult
volHot = useVol and volQual and not na(relVol) and relVol >= volMult

// confirmation candles (pin / engulfing)
bullPin    = rng > 0 and dnWick >= 2 * body and upWick <= body and dnWick >= minWickAtr * atr
bearPin    = rng > 0 and upWick >= 2 * body and dnWick <= body and upWick >= minWickAtr * atr
bullEngulf = bullBar and bearBar[1] and close >= open[1] and open <= close[1] and body > body[1]
bearEngulf = bearBar and bullBar[1] and close <= open[1] and open >= close[1] and body > body[1]
bullConfirm = bullPin or bullEngulf
bearConfirm = bearPin or bearEngulf
doji        = rng > 0 and body <= dojiPct / 100 * rng

// ---- CISD engine (V2 FIX-16 preserved) ----------------------------------------
// Strict consecutive delivery: neutral candle (≤10% body) voids both series;
// CISD = displacement-grade close through the origin open of a ≥2-candle
// opposite run. Latched once per run.
cisdNeutral = rng == 0 or body <= CISD_NEUTRAL_PCT * rng
cisdBull    = bullBar and not cisdNeutral
cisdBear    = bearBar and not cisdNeutral
// CE10295 refactor: the strict CISD run tracker (V2 FIX-16, unchanged logic)
// keeps its run state inside a function and returns the two events plus the
// two run lengths the debug table shows.
f_cisd() =>
    var float _bullRunOpen = na
    var float _bearRunOpen = na
    var int   _bullRunLen  = 0
    var int   _bearRunLen  = 0
    var bool  _cisdDnDone  = false
    var bool  _cisdUpDone  = false
    // SCOPE: written as assignments instead of if-branches — Pine counts every
    // branch as a local scope and the script is near that ceiling. Logic is
    // identical: a neutral candle voids both runs, a new run starts at 1, a
    // continuing run counts up.
    if confirmed
        bool _newBull = cisdBull and not cisdBull[1]
        bool _newBear = cisdBear and not cisdBear[1]
        _bullRunOpen := cisdNeutral ? na : _newBull ? open : _bullRunOpen
        _bearRunOpen := cisdNeutral ? na : _newBear ? open : _bearRunOpen
        _bullRunLen  := cisdNeutral ? 0 : _newBull ? 1 : cisdBull ? _bullRunLen + 1 : _bullRunLen
        _bearRunLen  := cisdNeutral ? 0 : _newBear ? 1 : cisdBear ? _bearRunLen + 1 : _bearRunLen
        _cisdDnDone  := _newBull ? false : _cisdDnDone
        _cisdUpDone  := _newBear ? false : _cisdUpDone
    _cisdDn = confirmed and not na(_bullRunOpen) and not _cisdDnDone and cisdBear and close < _bullRunOpen and _bullRunLen >= 2 and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr and close <= low + STRONG_CLOSE_PCT * rng
    _cisdUp = confirmed and not na(_bearRunOpen) and not _cisdUpDone and cisdBull and close > _bearRunOpen and _bearRunLen >= 2 and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr and close >= high - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT * rng
    _cisdDnDone := _cisdDn ? true : _cisdDnDone
    _cisdUpDone := _cisdUp ? true : _cisdUpDone
    if showCisd and _cisdDn
        f_chip(false, "CISD", color.new(COL_BEAR, 55), color.white, mkSize)
    if showCisd and _cisdUp
        f_chip(true, "CISD", color.new(COL_BULL, 55), color.white, mkSize)
    [_cisdDn, _cisdUp, _bullRunLen, _bearRunLen]
[cisdDn, cisdUp, bullRunLen, bearRunLen] = f_cisd()

// ---- IFC engine (V2 FIX-17 preserved) -------------------------------------------
// Institutional funding candle: takes a REAL pool (prior IFC_LOOK-bar extreme)
// with meaningful penetration, then closes past the prior midpoint with a
// displacement body and an outer-30% close.
prevMid  = (high[1] + low[1]) / 2
ifcPoolL = ta.lowest(low, IFC_LOOK)[1]
ifcPoolH = ta.highest(high, IFC_LOOK)[1]
ifcBull = low < ifcPoolL and ifcPoolL - low >= NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr and bullBar and close > prevMid and close >= high - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT * rng and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr
ifcBear = high > ifcPoolH and high - ifcPoolH >= NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr and bearBar and close < prevMid and close <= low + STRONG_CLOSE_PCT * rng and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr
if showIFC and ifcBull and confirmed
    f_chip(true, "IFC", color.new(color.gray, 55), color.new(COL_BULL, 0), mkSize)
if showIFC and ifcBear and confirmed
    f_chip(false, "IFC", color.new(color.gray, 55), color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), mkSize)

// execution confirmation used by zones / trendlines / machine
zoneBullConf = bullConfirm or ifcBull or (cisdAsConf and cisdUp)
zoneBearConf = bearConfirm or ifcBear or (cisdAsConf and cisdDn)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 4
// PIVOT ENGINE + EQ POOLS + TRENDLINE ANCHORS
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
ph = ta.pivothigh(high, pivLen, pivLen)
pl = ta.pivotlow(low, pivLen, pivLen)

var float lastRes    = na   // last confirmed swing high → buy-side liquidity
var float lastSup    = na   // last confirmed swing low  → sell-side liquidity
var int   lastResBar = na
var int   lastSupBar = na
var float prevPH     = na
var float prevPL     = na
var bool  resBroken  = false  // once-per-level break latch (V2 FIX-2)
var bool  supBroken  = false

var int   prevPHBar = na
var int   prevPLBar = na
var float tlUpSlope = na
var float tlUpPrice = na
var int   tlUpBar   = na
var int   tlUpTouch = 0
var line  tlUpLn    = na
var float tlDnSlope = na
var float tlDnPrice = na
var int   tlDnBar   = na
var int   tlDnTouch = 0
var line  tlDnLn    = na

var eqhLvls = array.new<float>()
var eqhLns  = array.new<line>()
var eqhBars = array.new<int>()      // FIX-W9: creation bar, for the age-out
var eqlLvls = array.new<float>()
var eqlLns  = array.new<line>()
var eqlBars = array.new<int>()

// ◆ FIX-P0-3: DETECTION is no longer gated by the display toggle. `showEq`
// only decides whether the pool gets a LINE. With it off, v3.1.1 never filled
// this array at all, so `eqhSwept` / `eqlSwept` could never fire, pool KIND 2
// never existed, and `sbPoolPref` (on by default) silently blocked every
// Silver Bullet arm. The array is now always populated; `eqhLns` simply holds
// `na` where no line was drawn, and every consumer already null-checks.
if not na(ph) and confirmed
    if not na(prevPH) and math.abs(ph - prevPH) <= eqTol * atr
        lvl = math.max(ph, prevPH)
        array.push(eqhLvls, lvl)
        array.push(eqhBars, bar_index)
        array.push(eqhLns, showEq ? line.new(bar_index - pivLen, lvl, bar_index + 1, lvl,
             color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 25), style = line.style_dotted, width = 2) : na)
        if array.size(eqhLvls) > MAX_EQ_POOLS
            line.delete(array.get(eqhLns, 0))
            array.remove(eqhLns, 0)
            array.remove(eqhLvls, 0)
            array.remove(eqhBars, 0)
    // trendline: lower high → candidate line off the last two pivot highs.
    // A validated line (≥1 touch) is NOT re-anchored (V2 FIX-19).
    if not na(prevPH) and not na(prevPHBar) and ph < prevPH and (na(tlDnSlope) or tlDnTouch == 0)
        line.delete(tlDnLn)
        tlDnLn    := na
        tlDnSlope := (ph - prevPH) / (bar_index - pivLen - prevPHBar)
        tlDnPrice := ph
        tlDnBar   := bar_index - pivLen
        tlDnTouch := 0
        if showTL
            tlDnLn := line.new(prevPHBar, prevPH, bar_index - pivLen, ph,
                 color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 30), width = 2, extend = extend.right,
                 style = line.style_dotted)
    prevPH     := ph
    prevPHBar  := bar_index - pivLen
    lastRes    := ph
    lastResBar := bar_index - pivLen
    resBroken  := false
if not na(pl) and confirmed
    if not na(prevPL) and math.abs(pl - prevPL) <= eqTol * atr
        lvl = math.min(pl, prevPL)
        array.push(eqlLvls, lvl)
        array.push(eqlBars, bar_index)
        array.push(eqlLns, showEq ? line.new(bar_index - pivLen, lvl, bar_index + 1, lvl,
             color = color.new(COL_BULL, 25), style = line.style_dotted, width = 2) : na)
        if array.size(eqlLvls) > MAX_EQ_POOLS
            line.delete(array.get(eqlLns, 0))
            array.remove(eqlLns, 0)
            array.remove(eqlLvls, 0)
            array.remove(eqlBars, 0)
    if not na(prevPL) and not na(prevPLBar) and pl > prevPL and (na(tlUpSlope) or tlUpTouch == 0)
        line.delete(tlUpLn)
        tlUpLn    := na
        tlUpSlope := (pl - prevPL) / (bar_index - pivLen - prevPLBar)
        tlUpPrice := pl
        tlUpBar   := bar_index - pivLen
        tlUpTouch := 0
        if showTL
            tlUpLn := line.new(prevPLBar, prevPL, bar_index - pivLen, pl,
                 color = color.new(COL_BULL, 30), width = 2, extend = extend.right,
                 style = line.style_dotted)
    prevPL     := pl
    prevPLBar  := bar_index - pivLen
    lastSup    := pl
    lastSupBar := bar_index - pivLen
    supBroken  := false

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 4B
// SESSION CLOCK + ◆ SILVER BULLET WINDOW ENGINE                        (v3.1)
// Moved up from MODULE 8 (V31-FIX-1): the session / killzone flags must exist
// BEFORE the liquidity engine, so that MODULE 5 can stamp a session raid into
// the sweep memory in the SAME pass that decides "sweep in play".
// Silver Bullet doctrine windows (New York time, closed bars only):
//   London 03:00–04:00 · NY AM 10:00–11:00 (priority) · NY PM 14:00–15:00
// The windows come from the sbSes* session inputs so they stay user-tunable.
// The minute math parses "HHMM-HHMM[:days]" and does NOT support a window that
// crosses midnight — none of the three does.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
f_inSes(simple string ses) =>
    not na(time(timeframe.period, ses, "America/New_York"))

inAsia = f_inSes(asiaSes)
inLon  = f_inSes(lonSes)
inNY   = f_inSes(nySes)
inSbLO = f_inSes(sbSesLO)
inSbAM = f_inSes(sbSesAM)
inSbPM = f_inSes(sbSesPM)
inSB   = inSbLO or inSbAM or inSbPM
inKZ   = inAsia or inLon or inNY or inSB
kzOK   = not useKZ or not timeframe.isintraday or inKZ

inNews  = f_inSes(news1Ses) or f_inSes(news2Ses) or f_inSes(news3Ses)
// ◆ FIX-P0-8: `newsOK` is evaluated LOWER DOWN now, after `sbActive` /
// `sbWinNow` exist, so the NY AM Silver Bullet window can opt out of the
// 09:55–10:10 block. Only the evaluation POINT moved.

// ---- session-string minute math (NY minute-of-day) -----------------------------
// off 0 = the session's START "HHMM", off 5 = its END "HHMM".
// TOKEN: one call per window (it used to be parsed twice per window)
f_sesMin(simple string s) =>
    float st = na
    float en = na
    if str.length(s) >= 9
        float h1 = str.tonumber(str.substring(s, 0, 2))
        float m1 = str.tonumber(str.substring(s, 2, 4))
        float h2 = str.tonumber(str.substring(s, 5, 7))
        float m2 = str.tonumber(str.substring(s, 7, 9))
        st := na(h1) or na(m1) ? na : h1 * 60 + m1
        en := na(h2) or na(m2) ? na : h2 * 60 + m2
    [st, en]

[sbStartLO, sbEndLO] = f_sesMin(sbSesLO)
[sbStartAM, sbEndAM] = f_sesMin(sbSesAM)
[sbStartPM, sbEndPM] = f_sesMin(sbSesPM)

sbNowMin = timeframe.isintraday ? hour(time, "America/New_York") * 60 + minute(time, "America/New_York") : na
sbTfMin  = math.max(1.0, timeframe.in_seconds() / 60.0)

// pre-window arming grace — the doctrine sequence may start on the approach
sbPreLO = sbUseLO and not inSbLO and not na(sbStartLO) and not na(sbNowMin) and sbNowMin < sbStartLO and sbStartLO - sbNowMin <= sbPreMin
sbPreAM = sbUseAM and not inSbAM and not na(sbStartAM) and not na(sbNowMin) and sbNowMin < sbStartAM and sbStartAM - sbNowMin <= sbPreMin
sbPrePM = sbUsePM and not inSbPM and not na(sbStartPM) and not na(sbNowMin) and sbNowMin < sbStartPM and sbStartPM - sbNowMin <= sbPreMin

// window id: 1 London · 2 NY AM (priority) · 3 NY PM · 0 none
sbWinNow = (sbUseAM and inSbAM) ? 2 : (sbUseLO and inSbLO) ? 1 : (sbUsePM and inSbPM) ? 3 : 0
sbCtxWin = sbWinNow != 0 ? sbWinNow : sbPreAM ? 2 : sbPreLO ? 1 : sbPrePM ? 3 : 0
sbOn     = sbEnable and timeframe.isintraday
sbActive = sbOn and sbWinNow != 0          // inside a window
sbCtx    = sbOn and sbCtxWin != 0          // inside, or in the arming grace

// ◆ FIX-P0-8 (see MODULE 4B top): the scheduled-volatility block, now aware of
// the SB window. The NY AM slot (window id 2) is the one the 10:00 data window
// overlaps; London and NY PM keep the block unconditionally.
newsOK = not useNews or not timeframe.isintraday or not inNews
     or (newsAllowSb and sbActive and sbWinNow == 2)

sbEndMinNow = sbWinNow == 1 ? sbEndLO : sbWinNow == 2 ? sbEndAM : sbWinNow == 3 ? sbEndPM : na
sbRemMin    = sbActive and not na(sbEndMinNow) and not na(sbNowMin) ? math.max(0, sbEndMinNow - sbNowMin) : na
sbRemBar    = na(sbRemMin) ? na : math.floor(sbRemMin / sbTfMin)

// per-window CLOSE edges (closed bars) — this is the MODULE 19 exit clock
sbEndLOEvt = sbOn and confirmed and not inSbLO and inSbLO[1]
sbEndAMEvt = sbOn and confirmed and not inSbAM and inSbAM[1]
sbEndPMEvt = sbOn and confirmed and not inSbPM and inSbPM[1]

f_sbWinTxt(int w) => w == 1 ? "London" : w == 2 ? "NY AM" : w == 3 ? "NY PM" : "—"
sbWinTxt = f_sbWinTxt(sbCtxWin)
// NY AM carries the most weight; London demands the strongest sweep instead
sbWinPts  = sbCtxWin == 2 ? 6 : sbCtxWin == 3 ? 5 : sbCtxWin == 1 ? 4 : 0
sbMinQual = sbCtxWin == 1 ? sbQualLO : sbCtxWin == 2 ? sbQualAM : sbCtxWin == 3 ? sbQualPM : 4

// ---- SB window range boxes ------------------------------------------------------
var float sbBoxHi  = na
var float sbBoxLo  = na
var int   sbBoxX1  = na
var int   sbBoxWin = 0
var sbBoxes = array.new<box>()
if sbOn and sbShowBox
    if sbWinNow != 0
        if nz(sbWinNow[1]) != sbWinNow
            sbBoxHi  := high
            sbBoxLo  := low
            sbBoxX1  := bar_index
