            sbBoxWin := sbWinNow
        else
            sbBoxHi := math.max(nz(sbBoxHi, high), high)
            sbBoxLo := math.min(nz(sbBoxLo, low),  low)
    else if confirmed and nz(sbWinNow[1]) != 0 and not na(sbBoxHi)
        array.push(sbBoxes, box.new(sbBoxX1, sbBoxHi, bar_index, sbBoxLo,
             border_color = color.new(color.teal, 55), bgcolor = color.new(color.teal, 97),
             border_style = line.style_dotted,
             text = "SB " + f_sbWinTxt(sbBoxWin), text_color = color.new(color.teal, 30),
             text_size = size.tiny, text_halign = text.align_center, text_valign = text.align_top))
        sbBoxHi := na
        sbBoxLo := na
        if array.size(sbBoxes) > sbBoxKeep
            box.delete(array.get(sbBoxes, 0))
            array.remove(sbBoxes, 0)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 5
// LIQUIDITY ENGINE — sweeps, quality grades, event classification
// Events per level: FORMING (pivot printed) · SWEPT/RAID (wick through, close
// back inside) · BREAKOUT (confirmed close beyond) · RECLAIMED (close back
// through after a breakout — handled by the once-per-level latch reset).
// Quality: 1 weak · 2 valid · 3 strong · 4 institutional (upgraded later by
// the structure engine when a shift lands while the raid is in play).
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ◆ FIX-W1 (v3.4, OP-1) ONE SWEEP EVENT PER LEVEL.
// `lastSup` only changes when a NEW pivot confirms, so the raw test below was
// true on every bar that dipped under the same swing low and closed back above
// it. `lastSslBar := bar_index` was rewritten each of those bars, which means:
//   · the sweep was eternally "fresh" — `sslInPlay` never aged out and the
//     liquidity score kept its 10 points for as long as price loitered there;
//   · the once-per-sweep arm guards (FIX-T3 `armedSslBar`, SB-9
//     `sbArmedSslBar`) compare against `lastSslBar`, so a moving stamp made
//     them true again on the very next bar — the guarantee did not hold;
//   · `QualRaw` was re-earned every bar, so a limp re-poke could overwrite the
//     grade the real raid earned.
// Structure already had the correct pattern (`resBroken` / `supBroken`). A
// level now produces ONE sweep event. It re-arms when the pivot itself changes
// or when price has travelled more than `SWEEP_CTX_ATR` × ATR away from it —
// a return from that far is a new raid, not the same one still running.
var float sslSpentLvl = na
var float bslSpentLvl = na
if not na(sslSpentLvl) and math.abs(close - sslSpentLvl) > SWEEP_CTX_ATR * atr
    sslSpentLvl := na
if not na(bslSpentLvl) and math.abs(close - bslSpentLvl) > SWEEP_CTX_ATR * atr
    bslSpentLvl := na
sslLvlFresh = na(sslSpentLvl) or (not na(lastSup) and math.abs(lastSup - sslSpentLvl) > NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr)
bslLvlFresh = na(bslSpentLvl) or (not na(lastRes) and math.abs(lastRes - bslSpentLvl) > NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr)
sslSweep = not na(lastSup) and low < lastSup and close > lastSup and sslLvlFresh
bslSweep = not na(lastRes) and high > lastRes and close < lastRes and bslLvlFresh

f_sweepQual(bool isLow) =>
    // graded on the sweeping candle itself; institutional upgrade comes later
    float pen  = isLow ? lastSup - low : high - lastRes
    bool  firm = isLow ? close >= lastSup + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr * 0.5 : close <= lastRes - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr * 0.5
    bool  outr = isLow ? close >= low + (1 - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT) * rng : close <= high - (1 - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT) * rng
    bool  disp = body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr
    int q = 1
    if firm or pen >= NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
        q := 2
    if q == 2 and outr and disp
        q := 3
    // FIX-F2: a raid on real volume is a better raid — one grade up, capped at
    // STRONG. Grade 4 stays the MSS upgrade's alone.
    // ◆ FIX-X7 VOLUME IS A MULTIPLIER, NOT THE WHOLE CASE. Grade 2 is trivially
    // earned (`firm` OR 0.3 ATR of penetration), and relative volume above the
    // multiplier is routine at a session open — so any limp poke inside the
    // first minutes of London / NY was promoted to STRONG, which is exactly the
    // RAW grade the A+ tier and the liquidity bonus demand as INDEPENDENT
    // evidence. The bump now needs one structural quality of the raid candle
    // as well: an outer-30% close or a displacement body.
    if volHot and q >= 2 and (outr or disp)
        q := 3
    q

var int   lastSslBar  = na
var int   lastBslBar  = na
var float lastSslLvl  = na
var float lastBslLvl  = na
var float lastSslExt  = na   // sweep wick extreme — machine invalidation + SL anchor
var float lastBslExt  = na
// ◆ FIX-P1-1 QUALITY IS TWO NUMBERS NOW.
//   QualRaw = what the SWEEPING CANDLE earned. Written once per sweep event
//             and never rewritten. This is the honest grade.
//   QualEff = QualRaw plus the MSS "institutional" upgrade (MODULE 6).
// v3.1.1 had one field, so an MSS promoted the sweep to grade 4 and the A+
// tier's "needs a strong sweep (≥3)" test was then satisfied BY THAT SAME MSS.
// Circular. Tier A+ and the liquidity score bonus read RAW; arming, the chop
// bypass and the SB gates read EFF (an MSS genuinely is extra evidence there).
var int   lastSslQual    = 0    // EFF — with the MSS upgrade
var int   lastBslQual    = 0
var int   lastSslQualRaw = 0    // RAW — earned by the candle, never upgraded
var int   lastBslQualRaw = 0
// SB-3 pool KIND: 1 swing pivot · 2 EQH/EQL pool · 3 PDH/PDL · 4 prior
// session H/L. Kind ≥ 2 is ENGINEERED liquidity — the pool an SB raid takes.
var int   lastSslKind = 0
var int   lastBslKind = 0

// ◆ FIX-P0-2 THE STAMP RULE, applied identically at every pool site below.
// v3.1.1 wrote the engineered pools with `lastBslQual := math.max(lastBslQual,
// ...)`, i.e. against the PREVIOUS sweep's grade — so a limp PDH poke inherited
// an old institutional 4 and walked straight through minArmQual, sbMinQual, the
// chop bypass and the A+ gate. Grades may only merge when two pools are taken
// on the SAME bar; a new bar always starts from what this candle earned.
// (Pine functions cannot assign to globals, so the two quality lines are
// written out at each site. Both must be evaluated BEFORE `lastXslBar` is
// moved to this bar — otherwise the same-bar test is trivially true.)
//
// the grade an engineered-pool raid earns from THIS candle (2 valid · 3 strong)
// FIX-F2: volume on the raid candle lifts a VALID pool raid to STRONG
// ◆ FIX-X7: `or volHot` alone promoted a pool raid to STRONG on volume with no
// quality in the candle at all. Volume now has to accompany one of the two.
qPoolLo = (close >= low  + (1 - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT) * rng or (volHot and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr)) and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr ? 3 : 2
qPoolHi = (close <= high - (1 - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT) * rng or (volHot and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr)) and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr ? 3 : 2

// ◆ FIX-V11 (v3.3, audit F-13): a swing sweep used to overwrite the pool KIND
// unconditionally, so a session / PD / EQ raid still in play was downgraded to
// "swing" by the next minor swing sweep a few bars later — and `sbPoolPref`
// then refused the Silver Bullet arm. An engineered pool within reach of the
// swing level keeps its kind; only its level / extreme / grade are refreshed.
if sslSweep and confirmed
    int qS = f_sweepQual(true)
    lastSslQualRaw := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQualRaw, qS) : qS
    lastSslQual    := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQual, qS)    : qS
    lastSslKind := f_recent(lastSslBar, sweepLook) and lastSslKind >= 2 and math.abs(lastSup - lastSslLvl) <= NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr ? lastSslKind : 1
    lastSslBar  := bar_index
    lastSslLvl  := lastSup
    lastSslExt  := low
    sslSpentLvl := lastSup      // FIX-W1: this level has had its sweep
if bslSweep and confirmed
    int qB = f_sweepQual(false)
    lastBslQualRaw := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQualRaw, qB) : qB
    lastBslQual    := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQual, qB)    : qB
    lastBslKind := f_recent(lastBslBar, sweepLook) and lastBslKind >= 2 and math.abs(lastRes - lastBslLvl) <= NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr ? lastBslKind : 1
    lastBslBar  := bar_index
    lastBslLvl  := lastRes
    lastBslExt  := high
    bslSpentLvl := lastRes      // FIX-W1

// EQ pools: Judas sweep (close back inside) vs real break (pool consumed)
eqhSwept = false
eqlSwept = false
if array.size(eqhLvls) > 0
    for i = array.size(eqhLvls) - 1 to 0
        lvl = array.get(eqhLvls, i)
        if high > lvl and confirmed
            if close < lvl
                eqhSwept    := true
                lastBslQualRaw := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQualRaw, qPoolHi) : qPoolHi
                lastBslQual    := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQual, qPoolHi)    : qPoolHi
                lastBslBar  := bar_index
                lastBslLvl  := lvl
                lastBslExt  := high
                lastBslKind := 2
            line.delete(array.get(eqhLns, i))
            array.remove(eqhLns, i)
            array.remove(eqhLvls, i)
            array.remove(eqhBars, i)
        else if bar_index - array.get(eqhBars, i) > eqMaxAge      // FIX-W9
            line.delete(array.get(eqhLns, i))
            array.remove(eqhLns, i)
            array.remove(eqhLvls, i)
            array.remove(eqhBars, i)
if array.size(eqlLvls) > 0
    for i = array.size(eqlLvls) - 1 to 0
        lvl = array.get(eqlLvls, i)
        if low < lvl and confirmed
            if close > lvl
                eqlSwept    := true
                lastSslQualRaw := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQualRaw, qPoolLo) : qPoolLo
                lastSslQual    := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQual, qPoolLo)    : qPoolLo
                lastSslBar  := bar_index
                lastSslLvl  := lvl
                lastSslExt  := low
                lastSslKind := 2
            line.delete(array.get(eqlLns, i))
            array.remove(eqlLns, i)
            array.remove(eqlLvls, i)
            array.remove(eqlBars, i)
        else if bar_index - array.get(eqlBars, i) > eqMaxAge      // FIX-W9
            line.delete(array.get(eqlLns, i))
            array.remove(eqlLns, i)
            array.remove(eqlLvls, i)
            array.remove(eqlBars, i)


// ---- previous day / week high–low (external liquidity map) ---------------------
[pdh, pdl] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "D", [high[1], low[1]], lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
[pwh, pwl] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "W", [high[1], low[1]], lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
// ◆ FIX-W1b (OP-1): PDH / PDL are the SAME price all day, so without a latch
// every bar that wicked through and closed back re-stamped the pool, refreshed
// the grade and defeated the once-per-sweep arm guards. One raid per level per
// day; the latch clears when the daily level itself changes.
var bool pdhDone = false
var bool pdlDone = false
if not na(pdh) and pdh != nz(pdh[1], pdh)
    pdhDone := false
if not na(pdl) and pdl != nz(pdl[1], pdl)
    pdlDone := false
pdhSweep = confirmed and not na(pdh) and high > pdh and close < pdh and not pdhDone
pdlSweep = confirmed and not na(pdl) and low  < pdl and close > pdl and not pdlDone
pdhDone := pdhSweep ? true : pdhDone
pdlDone := pdlSweep ? true : pdlDone
if pdhSweep
    lastBslQualRaw := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQualRaw, qPoolHi) : qPoolHi
    lastBslQual    := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQual, qPoolHi)    : qPoolHi
    lastBslBar  := bar_index
    lastBslLvl  := pdh
    lastBslExt  := high
    lastBslKind := 3
if pdlSweep
    lastSslQualRaw := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQualRaw, qPoolLo) : qPoolLo
    lastSslQual    := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQual, qPoolLo)    : qPoolLo
    lastSslBar  := bar_index
    lastSslLvl  := pdl
    lastSslExt  := low
    lastSslKind := 3

// ---- per-session liquidity (moved here from MODULE 8 by V31-FIX-1) ------------
// per-session H/L tracking (V2 FIX-18 RAID/BREAK split preserved), returned
// separately per session so Asia ≠ London ≠ NY in the machine and the score
// ◆ FIX-P0-3 the CLOSED-session levels now live in their own `var float`s
// (`hiLvl` / `loLvl`) instead of being read back out of the drawn line with
// `line.get_y1()`. In v3.1.1 the line was the only storage AND it was created
// inside `if showSesHL ...`, so turning the display toggle off left `hiLvl`
// permanently `na`: no session raid could ever be detected, pool KIND 4 never
// existed, and the Silver Bullet's primary pool (Asia / London H-L) was dead.
// Detection now runs unconditionally; `showSesHL` only draws.
f_sesTrack(bool active, string tag, color col) =>
    var float hi   = na
    var float lo   = na
    var float hiLvl = na        // level of the LAST CLOSED session — always tracked
    var float loLvl = na
    var line  hiLn = na
    var line  loLn = na
    var label hiLb = na
    var label loLb = na
    var bool  hiSwept = false
    var bool  loSwept = false
    if active                          // SCOPE: branch → ternary (×3 call sites)
        hi := not active[1] ? high : math.max(hi, high)
        lo := not active[1] ? low  : math.min(lo, low)
    if not active and active[1] and confirmed and not na(hi)
        hiLvl   := hi           // ← detection state, independent of any drawing
        loLvl   := lo
        hiSwept := false
        loSwept := false
        line.delete(hiLn)
        line.delete(loLn)
        label.delete(hiLb)
        label.delete(loLb)
        hiLn := na
        loLn := na
        hiLb := na
        loLb := na
        if showSesHL
            hiLn := line.new(bar_index, hi, bar_index + 1, hi, color = color.new(col, 35), extend = extend.right)
            loLn := line.new(bar_index, lo, bar_index + 1, lo, color = color.new(col, 35), extend = extend.right)
            hiLb := label.new(bar_index, hi, tag + " H", style = label.style_label_left,
                 color = color.new(col, 20), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
            loLb := label.new(bar_index, lo, tag + " L", style = label.style_label_left,
                 color = color.new(col, 20), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
    // ◆ FIX-W10 (v3.4, OP-10): `hiSwept` / `loSwept` latched at the FIRST touch
    // and cleared only at the next session close, so Asia H raided at 09:00 and
    // again at 14:00 produced one pool event — and the second one (the NY-AM
    // raid, the one the Silver Bullet is built on) was invisible to pool kind 4.
    // The latch clears once price has walked a full ATR back off the level: a
    // return from there is a new raid, not the same one still in progress.
    if hiSwept and not na(hiLvl) and close < hiLvl - atr
        hiSwept := false
    if loSwept and not na(loLvl) and close > loLvl + atr
        loSwept := false
    bool hiRaid = false
    bool loRaid = false
    bool hiBrk  = false
    bool loBrk  = false
    // SCOPE: the shared `not active and confirmed` guard folded into both tests
    bool _hiHit = not active and confirmed and not na(hiLvl) and not hiSwept and high > hiLvl
    bool _loHit = not active and confirmed and not na(loLvl) and not loSwept and low < loLvl
    if _hiHit
        hiSwept := true
        hiBrk   := close > hiLvl
        hiRaid  := not hiBrk
        if not na(hiLn)
            line.set_extend(hiLn, extend.none)
            line.set_x2(hiLn, bar_index)
            line.set_style(hiLn, hiRaid ? line.style_dashed : line.style_dotted)
            label.set_text(hiLb, tag + (hiRaid ? " H raided" : " H broken"))
    if _loHit
        loSwept := true
        loBrk   := close < loLvl
        loRaid  := not loBrk
        if not na(loLn)
            line.set_extend(loLn, extend.none)
            line.set_x2(loLn, bar_index)
            line.set_style(loLn, loRaid ? line.style_dashed : line.style_dotted)
            label.set_text(loLb, tag + (loRaid ? " L raided" : " L broken"))
    [hiRaid, loRaid, hiBrk, loBrk, hiLvl, loLvl]

[asiaHiSw, asiaLoSw, asiaHiBk, asiaLoBk, asiaHiLvl, asiaLoLvl] = f_sesTrack(inAsia and timeframe.isintraday, "Asia", color.purple)
[lonHiSw,  lonLoSw,  lonHiBk,  lonLoBk,  lonHiLvl,  lonLoLvl]  = f_sesTrack(inLon and timeframe.isintraday,  "Lon",  color.blue)
[nyHiSw,   nyLoSw,   nyHiBk,   nyLoBk,   nyHiLvl,   nyLoLvl]   = f_sesTrack(inNY and timeframe.isintraday,   "NY",   color.orange)
sesHiSwept = asiaHiSw or lonHiSw or nyHiSw
sesLoSwept = asiaLoSw or lonLoSw or nyLoSw
sesHiBroke = asiaHiBk or lonHiBk or nyHiBk
sesLoBroke = asiaLoBk or lonLoBk or nyLoBk

// ARCH-8: London / NY raids ARM the machine (full-grade engineered sweep);
// Asia raids only add score. Session raids feed the main sweep memory with a
// quality grade so the state machine treats them like swing sweeps.
if confirmed
    if lonLoSw or nyLoSw
        lastSslQualRaw := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQualRaw, qPoolLo) : qPoolLo
        lastSslQual    := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQual, qPoolLo)    : qPoolLo
        lastSslBar  := bar_index
        lastSslLvl  := lonLoSw ? lonLoLvl : nyLoLvl
        lastSslExt  := low
        lastSslKind := 4
    if lonHiSw or nyHiSw
        lastBslQualRaw := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQualRaw, qPoolHi) : qPoolHi
        lastBslQual    := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQual, qPoolHi)    : qPoolHi
        lastBslBar  := bar_index
        lastBslLvl  := lonHiSw ? lonHiLvl : nyHiLvl
        lastBslExt  := high
        lastBslKind := 4
// ◆ SB-3: inside an SB context window an ASIA session raid is also a legitimate
// SB pool — Asia H/L is the classic pool the NY AM window reaches for. Outside
// the SB windows Asia keeps its v3 score-only role, so non-SB behaviour is
// unchanged. A London / NY raid still wins the slot on a shared bar.
if sbCtx and sbAsiaPool and confirmed
    if asiaLoSw and not (lonLoSw or nyLoSw)
        lastSslQualRaw := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQualRaw, qPoolLo) : qPoolLo
        lastSslQual    := lastSslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastSslQual, qPoolLo)    : qPoolLo
        lastSslBar  := bar_index
        lastSslLvl  := asiaLoLvl
        lastSslExt  := low
        lastSslKind := 4
    if asiaHiSw and not (lonHiSw or nyHiSw)
        lastBslQualRaw := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQualRaw, qPoolHi) : qPoolHi
        lastBslQual    := lastBslBar == bar_index ? math.max(lastBslQual, qPoolHi)    : qPoolHi
        lastBslBar  := bar_index
        lastBslLvl  := asiaHiLvl
        lastBslExt  := high
        lastBslKind := 4

// ---- V31-FIX-1 -----------------------------------------------------------------
// "Sweep in play" is evaluated HERE — after EVERY stamp (swing · EQ pool ·
// PDH/PDL · session raid · SB Asia pool). In v3 these four lines sat above the
// PDH/PDL stamps and above MODULE 8's session stamps, so those raids could
// never satisfy the state machine's `lastSslBar == bar_index` arming test and
// only swing / EQ sweeps ever armed a setup. Silver Bullet depends on exactly
// those pools. The logic is byte-identical — only the evaluation point moved.
// ◆ SB-10: inside an SB context window the sweep stays "in play" for
// SB_MEM_EXTRA bars LONGER. The doctrine raid often lands on the approach to
// the window and the shift only follows a few bars later. Outside an SB
// context `sbMemLook` is exactly `sweepLook`, so v3 behaviour is unchanged.
sbMemLook = sbCtx ? sweepLook + SB_MEM_EXTRA : sweepLook
recentSsl = f_recent(lastSslBar, sbMemLook)
recentBsl = f_recent(lastBslBar, sbMemLook)
// ◆ FIX-P0-1 THE RECLAIM MUST STILL HOLD.
// v3.1.1 tested only the UPPER bound:
//     close - lastSslLvl <= SWEEP_CTX_ATR * atr
// With no lower bound that difference goes NEGATIVE once price closes back
// below the swept low — i.e. exactly when the sweep FAILED and turned into a
// genuine breakdown — and the condition stayed true. The engine then read a
// collapsing market as "bullish liquidity grab live": +10 on liqLong, a pass
// at tier B/A, and `mssUp` misclassifying every downside CHoCH as a bullish
// MSS. Mirrored on the short side in uptrends.
// A sweep IS its reclaim. Lose the reclaim and it is not a sweep any more,
// it is a breakout — so it must stop counting as liquidity in play.
sslInPlay = recentSsl and not na(lastSslLvl) and close > lastSslLvl
     and close - lastSslLvl <= SWEEP_CTX_ATR * atr
bslInPlay = recentBsl and not na(lastBslLvl) and close < lastBslLvl
     and lastBslLvl - close <= SWEEP_CTX_ATR * atr

// once-per-level breakout latch (acceptance beyond the level)
brokeUp = not na(lastRes) and not resBroken and close > lastRes
brokeDn = not na(lastSup) and not supBroken and close < lastSup
resBroken := brokeUp and confirmed ? true : resBroken      // SCOPE
supBroken := brokeDn and confirmed ? true : supBroken

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 6
// STRUCTURE — protected-swing engine (internal + external) + MSS grading
// BOS   = displaced continuation close through the latest trend pivot.
// CHoCH = displaced close through the PROTECTED swing (origin of the leg
//         that produced the last BOS). Minor pullback runs = inducement.
// MSS   = CHoCH landing while a quality (≥2) sweep is in play — liquidity
//         event + displacement + meaningful shift. Stronger than CHoCH alone.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
f_structure(bool upBrk, bool dnBrk) =>
    var int   dir    = 0
    var float protLo = na
    var float protHi = na
    var float legLo  = na
    var float legHi  = na
    bool bosU = false
    bool bosD = false
    bool chU  = false
    bool chD  = false
    legLo := na(legLo) ? low : math.min(legLo, low)
    legHi := na(legHi) ? high : math.max(legHi, high)
    if confirmed and body >= structDisp * atr
        if dir == 1
            if upBrk
                bosU   := true
                protLo := legLo
            else if not na(protLo) and close < protLo
                chD    := true
                dir    := -1
                protHi := legHi
                protLo := na
        else if dir == -1
            if dnBrk
                bosD   := true
                protHi := legHi
            else if not na(protHi) and close > protHi
                chU    := true
                dir    := 1
                protLo := legLo
                protHi := na
        else if upBrk
            bosU   := true
            dir    := 1
            protLo := legLo
        else if dnBrk
            bosD   := true
            dir    := -1
            protHi := legHi
        bool _ev2 = bosU or bosD or chU or chD      // SCOPE (×2 call sites)
        legLo := _ev2 ? low : legLo
        legHi := _ev2 ? high : legHi
    [dir, bosU, bosD, chU, chD, protLo, protHi]

[msDir, bosUp, bosDn, chochUp, chochDn, protLowI, protHighI] = f_structure(brokeUp, brokeDn)

// EXTERNAL engine (major swings)
eph = ta.pivothigh(high, extPiv, extPiv)
epl = ta.pivotlow(low, extPiv, extPiv)
var float extRes    = na
var float extSup    = na
var int   extResBar = na
var int   extSupBar = na
var bool  extResBrk = false
var bool  extSupBrk = false
if not na(eph) and confirmed
    extRes    := eph
    extResBar := bar_index - extPiv
    extResBrk := false
if not na(epl) and confirmed
    extSup    := epl
    extSupBar := bar_index - extPiv
    extSupBrk := false
eBrokeUp = not na(extRes) and not extResBrk and close > extRes
eBrokeDn = not na(extSup) and not extSupBrk and close < extSup
extResBrk := eBrokeUp and confirmed ? true : extResBrk     // SCOPE
extSupBrk := eBrokeDn and confirmed ? true : extSupBrk
[extDir, eBosUp, eBosDn, eChochUp, eChochDn, protLowE, protHighE] = f_structure(eBrokeUp, eBrokeDn)

// MSS classification (ARCH-4): CHoCH + a quality sweep still in play.
// The same event also upgrades that sweep to INSTITUTIONAL grade (quality 4).
// FIX-N5: external CHoCH always qualifies; internal only when the input allows
mssUp = (eChochUp or (not mssNeedExt and chochUp)) and sslInPlay and lastSslQual >= 2
mssDn = (eChochDn or (not mssNeedExt and chochDn)) and bslInPlay and lastBslQual >= 2
// ◆ FIX-P1-1 the institutional upgrade writes the EFFECTIVE grade only. The
// RAW grade stays whatever the sweeping candle earned, so the A+ tier's
// "strong sweep" test can no longer be satisfied by the very MSS it is meant
// to be an independent check on.
// ◆ FIX-W5 (v3.4, OP-5) THE UPGRADE BELONGS TO THE RAID IT BROKE FROM.
// `mssUp` only requires `sslInPlay`, and inside an SB context that memory runs
// `sweepLook + 5` bars back — so a CHoCH seventeen bars after a marginal poke
// promoted that poke to INSTITUTIONAL, which then cleared `minArmQual`,
// `sbMinQual` and the SB window gate. The grade-4 write now also needs the
// shift to land within `TRIG_LOOK` bars of the raid itself.
mssBindL = f_recent(lastSslBar, TRIG_LOOK)
mssBindS = f_recent(lastBslBar, TRIG_LOOK)
lastSslQual := mssUp and mssBindL and confirmed ? 4 : lastSslQual       // SCOPE
lastBslQual := mssDn and mssBindS and confirmed ? 4 : lastBslQual

// live trigger memory
// CE10295 refactor: the last-event bar stamps are kept inside a function and
// handed back; the recentXxx flags below are unchanged.
f_trigMem() =>
    var int _lastChUpBar   = na
    var int _lastChDnBar   = na
    var int _lastBosUpBar  = na
    var int _lastBosDnBar  = na
    var int _lastMssUpBar  = na
    var int _lastMssDnBar  = na
    var int _lastCisdUpBar = na
    var int _lastCisdDnBar = na
    var int _lastDispUpBar = na
    var int _lastDispDnBar = na
    if confirmed                       // SCOPE: ten stamps, zero branches
        _lastChUpBar   := chochUp or eChochUp ? bar_index : _lastChUpBar
        _lastChDnBar   := chochDn or eChochDn ? bar_index : _lastChDnBar
        _lastBosUpBar  := bosUp or eBosUp ? bar_index : _lastBosUpBar
        _lastBosDnBar  := bosDn or eBosDn ? bar_index : _lastBosDnBar
        _lastMssUpBar  := mssUp ? bar_index : _lastMssUpBar
        _lastMssDnBar  := mssDn ? bar_index : _lastMssDnBar
        _lastCisdUpBar := cisdUp ? bar_index : _lastCisdUpBar
        _lastCisdDnBar := cisdDn ? bar_index : _lastCisdDnBar
        _lastDispUpBar := bullBar and body >= dispFactor * atr ? bar_index : _lastDispUpBar
        _lastDispDnBar := bearBar and body >= dispFactor * atr ? bar_index : _lastDispDnBar
    [_lastChUpBar, _lastChDnBar, _lastBosUpBar, _lastBosDnBar, _lastMssUpBar, _lastMssDnBar, _lastCisdUpBar, _lastCisdDnBar, _lastDispUpBar, _lastDispDnBar]
[lastChUpBar, lastChDnBar, lastBosUpBar, lastBosDnBar, lastMssUpBar, lastMssDnBar, lastCisdUpBar, lastCisdDnBar, lastDispUpBar, lastDispDnBar] = f_trigMem()
recentChUp   = f_recent(lastChUpBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentChDn   = f_recent(lastChDnBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentBosUp  = f_recent(lastBosUpBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentBosDn  = f_recent(lastBosDnBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentMssUp  = f_recent(lastMssUpBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentMssDn  = f_recent(lastMssDnBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentCisdUp = f_recent(lastCisdUpBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentCisdDn = f_recent(lastCisdDnBar, TRIG_LOOK)
recentDispUp = f_recent(lastDispUpBar, 3)
recentDispDn = f_recent(lastDispDnBar, 3)
recentStructUp = recentChUp or recentBosUp
recentStructDn = recentChDn or recentBosDn

// structure marks: MSS > CHoCH > BOS; internal marks muted
// CE10295 refactor: the BOS / CHoCH / MSS chips are display only.
// SCOPE: the two eight-branch chains became one call per side; the priority
// (MSS > external CHoCH > external BOS > internal) is unchanged, it is just
// expressed as a ternary instead of eight local scopes.
f_structMarks() =>
    bool _up  = mssUp or eChochUp or eBosUp or chochUp or bosUp
    bool _dn  = mssDn or eChochDn or eBosDn or chochDn or bosDn
    bool _iUp = not (mssUp or eChochUp or eBosUp)
    bool _iDn = not (mssDn or eChochDn or eBosDn)
    if showStruct and confirmed and _up
        f_chip(true, mssUp ? "MSS" : eChochUp ? "CHoCH" : eBosUp ? "BOS" : chochUp ? "iCHoCH" : "ibos",
             _iUp ? color.new(color.gray, 60) : color.new(COL_BULL, mssUp ? 0 : 15),
             _iUp ? color.new(COL_BULL, 0) : color.white, _iUp ? size.tiny : sigSize)
    if showStruct and confirmed and _dn
        f_chip(false, mssDn ? "MSS" : eChochDn ? "CHoCH" : eBosDn ? "BOS" : chochDn ? "iCHoCH" : "ibos",
             _iDn ? color.new(color.gray, 60) : color.new(COL_BEAR, mssDn ? 0 : 15),
             _iDn ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 0) : color.white, _iDn ? size.tiny : sigSize)
f_structMarks()

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 7
// HTF DATA — MTF bias (last CLOSED candle only, lookahead off)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
f_bias(simple string tf) =>
    request.security(syminfo.tickerid, tf, nz((close > ta.ema(close, biasLen) ? 1 : -1)[1], 0),
         lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)

// TOKEN: v3.2.1 ran FIVE bias security calls to fill a dashboard row. Only the
// SELECTED timeframe can gate anything, so that one plus the daily (context)
// are computed; the dashboard prints exactly what the engine uses.
biasTf  = biasFilter == "Off" ? "60" : biasFilter
emaBias = f_bias(biasTf)
bD      = f_bias("D")

// ◆ FIX-F1 HTF STRUCTURE — the protected-swing engine run on the bias timeframe.
// Self-contained (its own pivots, break latch and leg tracking) because a
// request.security expression may only read the HTF series and its own state.
// Returns the CLOSED HTF bar's direction: 1 bullish structure · -1 bearish ·
// 0 none yet. Same `[1]` + lookahead_off idiom as the other HTF functions.
f_htfStruct(int piv, float dispMult) =>
    var float _res    = na
    var float _sup    = na
    var bool  _resBrk = false
    var bool  _supBrk = false
    var int   _dir    = 0
    var float _protLo = na
    var float _protHi = na
    var float _legLo  = na
    var float _legHi  = na
    float _ph = ta.pivothigh(high, piv, piv)
    float _pl = ta.pivotlow(low, piv, piv)
    if not na(_ph)
        _res    := _ph
        _resBrk := false
    if not na(_pl)
        _sup    := _pl
        _supBrk := false
    float _atr  = ta.atr(atrLen)
    float _body = math.abs(close - open)
    bool  _upBrk = not na(_res) and not _resBrk and close > _res
    bool  _dnBrk = not na(_sup) and not _supBrk and close < _sup
    if _upBrk
        _resBrk := true
    if _dnBrk
        _supBrk := true
    _legLo := na(_legLo) ? low : math.min(_legLo, low)
    _legHi := na(_legHi) ? high : math.max(_legHi, high)
    bool _ev = false
    if _body >= dispMult * _atr
        if _dir == 1
            if _upBrk
                _protLo := _legLo
                _ev     := true
            else if not na(_protLo) and close < _protLo
                _dir    := -1
                _protHi := _legHi
                _protLo := na
                _ev     := true
        else if _dir == -1
            if _dnBrk
                _protHi := _legHi
                _ev     := true
            else if not na(_protHi) and close > _protHi
                _dir    := 1
                _protLo := _legLo
                _protHi := na
                _ev     := true
        else if _upBrk
            _dir    := 1
            _protLo := _legLo
            _ev     := true
        else if _dnBrk
            _dir    := -1
            _protHi := _legHi
            _ev     := true
        if _ev
            _legLo := low
            _legHi := high
    [_dir[1], _protLo[1], _protHi[1]]

[hsDirRaw, hsProtLo, hsProtHi] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, biasTf, f_htfStruct(htfStructPiv, structDisp),
     lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
hsDir = nz(hsDirRaw, 0)

// the bias every consumer reads: EMA · structure · or both in agreement
// (structure with no opinion yet defers to the EMA; disagreement is neutral)
htfB = biasMode == "EMA only"       ? emaBias :
       biasMode == "Structure only" ? (hsDir != 0 ? hsDir : emaBias) :
       (hsDir == 0 ? emaBias : hsDir == emaBias ? hsDir : 0)
// ◆ FIX-V1: the ONE bias permission every gate reads. A neutral bias is a
// decision (block / follow chart structure), not a permission slip for both.
biasOkL = biasFilter == "Off" or htfB > 0 or (htfB == 0 and (neutralMode == "Allow both (v3.2.2)" or (neutralMode == "Follow chart structure" and extDir >= 0)))
biasOkS = biasFilter == "Off" or htfB < 0 or (htfB == 0 and (neutralMode == "Allow both (v3.2.2)" or (neutralMode == "Follow chart structure" and extDir <= 0)))

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 8
// SESSION LIQUIDITY MEMORY + OPENING PRICES
// v3.1: the killzone / volatility-window flags now live in MODULE 4B and the
// per-session H/L tracker in MODULE 5 (V31-FIX-1). What remains here is the
// engineered-liquidity SCORE memory and the 00:00 / 08:30 opening lines.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// engineered-liquidity memory (EQ pools + ANY session raid) — score only
var int lastEngLoBar = na
var int lastEngHiBar = na
if confirmed                       // SCOPE
    lastEngLoBar := eqlSwept or sesLoSwept or pdlSweep ? bar_index : lastEngLoBar
    lastEngHiBar := eqhSwept or sesHiSwept or pdhSweep ? bar_index : lastEngHiBar
recentEngLo = f_recent(lastEngLoBar, sweepLook)
recentEngHi = f_recent(lastEngHiBar, sweepLook)

// TOKEN: the 00:00 / 08:30 opening lines were display-only and were removed
// to fit the compiled-token budget. Nothing read them.

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 9
// HTF ENGINES — CRT + Turtle soup + FVG POI (closed HTF candles only)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
f_autoHtf() =>
    switch timeframe.period
        "1"   => "15"
        "2"   => "30"
        "3"   => "30"
        "5"   => "60"
        "10"  => "120"
        "15"  => "240"
        "30"  => "240"
        "60"  => "D"
        "120" => "D"
        "240" => "D"
        => "D"
htfTf   = htfSel == "Auto" ? f_autoHtf() : htfSel
htfLive = htfTf != timeframe.period

// turtle-soup age note (audited, V2 logic KEPT): ta.lowestbars/highestbars
// return a NON-POSITIVE offset (0 = this bar, -k = k bars back). Taking the
// value on the [1] series and negating: -offset[1] + 1 = positive age of the
// extreme in bars as seen from the CURRENT bar (≥ 1). No sign bug. v3 adds
// the MAX age gate: fading a months-old level is not a turtle soup.
f_tbsAgeOk(int age) => age >= tbsAgeMin and (tbsAgeMax == 0 or age <= tbsAgeMax)

f_crtTbsHtf() =>
    _atr  = ta.atr(atrLen)
    _rng  = high - low
    _body = math.abs(close - open)
    _bull = close > open
    _bear = close < open
    _c1big = _rng[2] >= crtRangeAtr * _atr
    _crtB  = _c1big and low[1] < low[2] and close[1] > low[2] and close[1] < high[2] and low[2] - low[1] >= minWickAtr * _atr and _bull and close > high[1] and _body >= crtDispAtr * _atr
    _crtS  = _c1big and high[1] > high[2] and close[1] < high[2] and close[1] > low[2] and high[1] - high[2] >= minWickAtr * _atr and _bear and close < low[1] and _body >= crtDispAtr * _atr
    _tl    = ta.lowest(low, tbsLen)[1]
    _th    = ta.highest(high, tbsLen)[1]
    _loAge = -ta.lowestbars(low, tbsLen)[1] + 1
    _hiAge = -ta.highestbars(high, tbsLen)[1] + 1
    _tbsB  = low < _tl and close > _tl and _bull and f_tbsAgeOk(_loAge) and close >= low + 0.5 * _rng
    _tbsS  = high > _th and close < _th and _bear and f_tbsAgeOk(_hiAge) and close <= high - 0.5 * _rng
    [_crtB[1], _crtS[1], _tbsB[1], _tbsS[1], time[1]]

[hCrtB, hCrtS, hTbsB, hTbsS, hT] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, htfTf, f_crtTbsHtf(),
     lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)

newHtfBar    = htfLive and not na(hT) and hT != nz(hT[1])
sigHCrtLong  = useHtfCrt and confirmed and newHtfBar and hCrtB
sigHCrtShort = useHtfCrt and confirmed and newHtfBar and hCrtS
sigHTbsLong  = useHtfCrt and confirmed and newHtfBar and hTbsB
sigHTbsShort = useHtfCrt and confirmed and newHtfBar and hTbsS

// ◆ FIX-F5 + FIX-N6 HTF FVG POI REGISTRY — up to THREE live POIs per side,
// freshness counted in SEPARATE VISITS (V2 FIX-15 lineage).
// v3.2 kept ONE slot per side: every new HTF FVG overwrote the previous one,
// so the morning's untested POI vanished when a lesser gap printed at noon.
// And `bIn` counted HTF candles spent inside, so a three-candle consolidation
// "spent" a POI it never left — while the chart-TF zone engine counts
// leave-and-return visits. Both definitions now agree.
// Internal state lives in arrays (allowed inside a security expression); the
// EXPORT is a flat tuple of the three newest slots per side, `[1]`-shifted
// under lookahead_off — the same non-repainting idiom as f_crtTbsHtf.
f_htfPoi() =>
    var bT  = array.new<float>()
    var bB  = array.new<float>()
    var bBr = array.new<int>()
    var bIn = array.new<int>()
    var bWs = array.new<bool>()
    var sT  = array.new<float>()
    var sB  = array.new<float>()
    var sBr = array.new<int>()
    var sIn = array.new<int>()
    var sWs = array.new<bool>()
    // 1 · lifecycle of the live slots: visit counting, then invalidation / age / spent
    if array.size(bT) > 0
        for i = array.size(bT) - 1 to 0
            float _tB = array.get(bT, i)
            float _bB = array.get(bB, i)
            if bar_index > array.get(bBr, i)
                bool _inB = low <= _tB and high >= _bB
                if _inB and not array.get(bWs, i)
                    array.set(bIn, i, array.get(bIn, i) + 1)
                array.set(bWs, i, _inB)
            if close < _bB or bar_index - array.get(bBr, i) > hPoiMaxAge or array.get(bIn, i) > hPoiMaxTouch
                array.remove(bT, i)
                array.remove(bB, i)
                array.remove(bBr, i)
                array.remove(bIn, i)
                array.remove(bWs, i)
    if array.size(sT) > 0
        for i = array.size(sT) - 1 to 0
            float _tS = array.get(sT, i)
            float _bS = array.get(sB, i)
            if bar_index > array.get(sBr, i)
                bool _inS = high >= _bS and low <= _tS
                if _inS and not array.get(sWs, i)
                    array.set(sIn, i, array.get(sIn, i) + 1)
                array.set(sWs, i, _inS)
            if close > _tS or bar_index - array.get(sBr, i) > hPoiMaxAge or array.get(sIn, i) > hPoiMaxTouch
                array.remove(sT, i)
                array.remove(sB, i)
                array.remove(sBr, i)
                array.remove(sIn, i)
                array.remove(sWs, i)
    // 2 · new imbalances go on the end; past three the OLDEST slot is dropped
    if low > high[2]
        array.push(bT, low)
        array.push(bB, high[2])
        array.push(bBr, bar_index)
        array.push(bIn, 0)
        array.push(bWs, false)
        if array.size(bT) > 3
            array.remove(bT, 0)
            array.remove(bB, 0)
            array.remove(bBr, 0)
            array.remove(bIn, 0)
            array.remove(bWs, 0)
    if high < low[2]
        array.push(sT, low[2])
        array.push(sB, high)
        array.push(sBr, bar_index)
        array.push(sIn, 0)
        array.push(sWs, false)
        if array.size(sT) > 3
            array.remove(sT, 0)
            array.remove(sB, 0)
            array.remove(sBr, 0)
            array.remove(sIn, 0)
            array.remove(sWs, 0)
    // 3 · export — newest first, na where a slot is empty
    int nb = array.size(bT)
    int ns = array.size(sT)
    float b1T = nb > 0 ? array.get(bT, nb - 1) : na
    float b1B = nb > 0 ? array.get(bB, nb - 1) : na
    int   b1I = nb > 0 ? array.get(bIn, nb - 1) : na
    float b2T = nb > 1 ? array.get(bT, nb - 2) : na
    float b2B = nb > 1 ? array.get(bB, nb - 2) : na
    int   b2I = nb > 1 ? array.get(bIn, nb - 2) : na
    float b3T = nb > 2 ? array.get(bT, nb - 3) : na
    float b3B = nb > 2 ? array.get(bB, nb - 3) : na
    int   b3I = nb > 2 ? array.get(bIn, nb - 3) : na
    float s1T = ns > 0 ? array.get(sT, ns - 1) : na
    float s1B = ns > 0 ? array.get(sB, ns - 1) : na
    int   s1I = ns > 0 ? array.get(sIn, ns - 1) : na
    float s2T = ns > 1 ? array.get(sT, ns - 2) : na
    float s2B = ns > 1 ? array.get(sB, ns - 2) : na
    int   s2I = ns > 1 ? array.get(sIn, ns - 2) : na
    float s3T = ns > 2 ? array.get(sT, ns - 3) : na
    float s3B = ns > 2 ? array.get(sB, ns - 3) : na
    int   s3I = ns > 2 ? array.get(sIn, ns - 3) : na
    [b1T[1], b1B[1], b1I[1], b2T[1], b2B[1], b2I[1], b3T[1], b3B[1], b3I[1],
     s1T[1], s1B[1], s1I[1], s2T[1], s2B[1], s2I[1], s3T[1], s3B[1], s3I[1]]

[hb1T, hb1B, hb1I, hb2T, hb2B, hb2I, hb3T, hb3B, hb3I,
 hs1T, hs1B, hs1I, hs2T, hs2B, hs2I, hs3T, hs3B, hs3I] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, htfTf, f_htfPoi(),
     lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)

// the WORKING POI per side: the nearest UNTESTED slot first, else the nearest
// tested one. A slot is a candidate when it is not entirely on the far side of
// price (a demand POI must have its bottom at or below the close). Every
// downstream consumer (FEM, the machine's tapHtf, NEXT level, the drawing)
// keeps reading `hPoiBTop / hPoiBBot / hPoiBIn` unchanged.
// FIX-T11: an untested POI eight ATR away used to outrank a tested one right
// under price, and that choice drives NEXT BUY / NEXT SELL, the POI score and
// the machine's HTF tap. Freshness only wins inside a distance window.
f_pickPoi(bool demand, float t1, float b1, int i1, float t2, float b2, int i2, float t3, float b3, int i3) =>
    float pT = na
    float pB = na
    int   pI = na
    float _win = POI_NEAR_ATR * 2 * atr
    if not na(t1) and (demand ? b1 <= close : t1 >= close)
        pT := t1
        pB := b1
        pI := nz(i1)
    if not na(t2) and (demand ? b2 <= close : t2 >= close)
        bool near2    = math.abs(close - (demand ? t2 : b2)) <= _win
        bool fresher2 = nz(i2) == 0 and pI != 0 and near2
        bool same2    = (nz(i2) == 0) == (pI == 0)
        bool nearer2  = demand ? t2 > pT : b2 < pB
        if na(pT) or fresher2 or (same2 and nearer2)
            pT := t2
            pB := b2
            pI := nz(i2)
    if not na(t3) and (demand ? b3 <= close : t3 >= close)
        bool near3    = math.abs(close - (demand ? t3 : b3)) <= _win
        bool fresher3 = nz(i3) == 0 and pI != 0 and near3
        bool same3    = (nz(i3) == 0) == (pI == 0)
        bool nearer3  = demand ? t3 > pT : b3 < pB
        if na(pT) or fresher3 or (same3 and nearer3)
            pT := t3
            pB := b3
            pI := nz(i3)
    [pT, pB, pI]

[hPoiBTop, hPoiBBot, hPoiBIn] = f_pickPoi(true,  hb1T, hb1B, hb1I, hb2T, hb2B, hb2I, hb3T, hb3B, hb3I)
[hPoiSTop, hPoiSBot, hPoiSIn] = f_pickPoi(false, hs1T, hs1B, hs1I, hs2T, hs2B, hs2I, hs3T, hs3B, hs3I)

// ◆ FIX-F4 ADR EXHAUSTION. Average daily range from the CLOSED daily bars
// (`[1]` + lookahead_off, non-repainting); today's range is tracked on the
// chart itself, reset at the daily boundary — never read from the developing
// daily bar, which would leak the day's final extremes into history.
adrVal = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "D", ta.sma(high - low, adrLen)[1], lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_off)
newDay = timeframe.isintraday and ta.change(time("D")) != 0
var float dayHi = na
var float dayLo = na
if newDay or na(dayHi)
    dayHi := high
    dayLo := low
else
    dayHi := math.max(dayHi, high)
    dayLo := math.min(dayLo, low)
dayRng    = dayHi - dayLo
adrPct    = timeframe.isintraday and not na(adrVal) and adrVal > 0 ? dayRng / adrVal : na
adrExh    = useAdr and not na(adrPct) and adrPct >= adrMax
adrPosD   = dayRng > 0 ? (close - dayLo) / dayRng : na
// chasing = entering WITH the exhausted move at its far end
adrChaseL = adrExh and not na(adrPosD) and adrPosD >= 0.7
adrChaseS = adrExh and not na(adrPosD) and adrPosD <= 0.3

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 10
// DEALING RANGE — premium / discount + OTE (external swings by default)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ◆ FIX-P1-9 THE DEALING RANGE MUST CONTAIN PRICE.
// Live charts printed "Discount -30%" and "Premium 463%". `pdPos` is
// (close - rngLo) / rngW with NO bound, so the moment price left the external
// swing range the reading ran outside 0–100% and stopped meaning anything.
// Premium / discount is a position INSIDE a range; outside one there is no
// range to be in. Worse, that unbounded number gates the A+ tier, the context
// score and the SB entry rule, so a runaway trend fed them a nonsense input.
// (The first attempt clamped the two swing anchors to contain price. It stopped
//  the bad numbers but pinned every chart at 0% / 100% — superseded below.)
//
// ◆ FIX-P1-12 THE RANGE IS THE CURRENT LEG'S EXTREMES, NOT TWO STALE PIVOTS.
// FIX-P1-9 stopped the nonsense readings but every live chart then printed
// "·at edge" — 0% or 100% — permanently. Root cause: a pivot needs `extPiv`
// bars of RIGHT-side confirmation, so the last CONFIRMED swing high always sits
// behind price. In any move the range's own high is already below the market
// and containment can never hold. The swing pair told us WHEN the leg started,
// never WHERE it currently reaches.
// The dealing range is now the high-to-low span measured from the OLDER of the
// two anchoring swings up to the current bar: the running extremes of the leg
// being delivered. Price is inside by construction, `pdPos` is a real position
// in a real range again, and the anchors still decide where the range BEGINS.
// KNOWN LIMITATION (audit LOGIC-N2, accepted): when a new external pivot
// confirms — `extPiv` bars after the fact — `anchBar` jumps forward and the
// range re-anchors in one bar, so `pdPos` can step (e.g. 85% → 25%) without
// price moving. That step is a REAL structural change (a new swing pair), only
// reported late because pivots need right-side confirmation. Smoothing it
// would hide the change rather than fix the delay, so it is left as is; the
// dashboard's "·tight" flag covers the degenerate case of a very short leg.
anchBarExt = math.min(nz(extResBar, bar_index), nz(extSupBar, bar_index))
anchBarInt = math.min(nz(lastResBar, bar_index), nz(lastSupBar, bar_index))
anchBar    = rangeSrc == "External" ? anchBarExt : anchBarInt
drLen      = math.max(5, math.min(500, bar_index - anchBar + 1))
rngHi      = ta.highest(high, drLen)
rngLo      = ta.lowest(low, drLen)
rngHiBar   = bar_index + ta.highestbars(high, drLen)
rngLoBar   = bar_index + ta.lowestbars(low, drLen)
rangeOK    = not na(rngHi) and not na(rngLo) and rngHi > rngLo
// the reading is only worth acting on once the leg is wide enough to HAVE a
// premium and a discount; a one-ATR range has neither.
rangeHolds = rangeOK and atr > 0 and (rngHi - rngLo) >= 2 * atr
bullRange = rangeOK and not na(rngHiBar) and not na(rngLoBar) and rngHiBar > rngLoBar
bearRange = rangeOK and not na(rngHiBar) and not na(rngLoBar) and rngLoBar > rngHiBar
rngW    = rangeOK ? rngHi - rngLo : na
// clamped by construction (rngLo ≤ close ≤ rngHi), the math.min/max is a belt
// against a zero-width range on the first bars
pdPos   = rangeOK ? math.min(1, math.max(0, (close - rngLo) / rngW)) : na
oteBuyTop  = rangeOK ? rngHi - OTE_1 * rngW : na
oteBuyBot  = rangeOK ? rngHi - OTE_2 * rngW : na
oteSellBot = rangeOK ? rngLo + OTE_1 * rngW : na
oteSellTop = rangeOK ? rngLo + OTE_2 * rngW : na
inOTEbuy   = bullRange and close <= oteBuyTop  and close >= oteBuyBot
inOTEsell  = bearRange and close >= oteSellBot and close <= oteSellTop

// ---- ◆ SB-12 DEALING-RANGE LOCK ------------------------------------------------
// The premium / discount read for an SB setup must be taken from the range as
// it stood when the WINDOW OPENED. Without the lock the raid itself extends
// `rngHi` / `rngLo`, which flips the setup to the wrong side of equilibrium and
// silently kills it through `sbReqPD`. The live range is untouched: every
// non-SB consumer (score, tier bump, OTE, flow boxes, dashboard) reads `pdPos`.
// `sbSetLive` is written at the END of MODULE 19 and therefore read here one
// bar later — deliberate: the lock must survive the window close while an SB
// setup or an SB trade is still alive, and only then clear.
var float sbRngHi   = na
var float sbRngLo   = na
var int   sbRngWin  = 0
var bool  sbSetLive = false
// ◆ FIX-P1-2: the SB window TAGS are declared here rather than in MODULE 17 so
// the tier grader (MODULE 16) can see them. MODULE 17 still owns every write;
// MODULE 16 reads the tag the live setup is carrying from the previous bar,
// which is exactly the setup being graded.
var int  sbTagL    = 0      // 0 none · 1 London · 2 NY AM · 3 NY PM
var int  sbTagS    = 0
if sbOn
    if sbWinNow != 0 and nz(sbWinNow[1]) != sbWinNow
        sbRngHi  := rngHi            // freeze at the open of a new SB window
        sbRngLo  := rngLo
        sbRngWin := sbWinNow
    else if sbWinNow == 0 and sbRngWin != 0 and not sbSetLive
        sbRngHi  := na               // window over and nothing SB-tagged live
        sbRngLo  := na
        sbRngWin := 0
sbRngOK = not na(sbRngHi) and not na(sbRngLo) and sbRngHi > sbRngLo
sbPdOK  = sbRngOK or rangeOK
// ◆ FIX-T9: `pdPos` is clamped to 0–1 (FIX-P1-9) but its SB twin was not, so
// once price left the FROZEN window range the locked reading ran negative or
// past 100% — and that number gates `sbReqPD` and the A+ tier. Clamped now, and
// a price well outside the frozen range falls back to the live reading rather
// than extrapolating a range that no longer contains the market.
sbPdRaw = sbRngOK ? (close - sbRngLo) / (sbRngHi - sbRngLo) : pdPos
sbPdOut = sbRngOK and (sbPdRaw < -0.15 or sbPdRaw > 1.15)
sbPdPos = sbPdOut ? pdPos : sbRngOK ? math.min(1, math.max(0, sbPdRaw)) : pdPos

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 11
// MARKET REGIME — TRENDING / TRANSITION / RANGING / CHOP (ARCH-5)
// Deterministic, closed-bar only:
//   TRENDING   = external dir set + external BOS within REGIME_LOOK bars and
//                no fresh opposing external CHoCH
//   TRANSITION = external CHoCH within REGIME_LOOK bars (structure flipping)
//   CHOP       = no recent external event AND external range < CHOP_RANGE_ATR
//   RANGING    = everything else (wide range, no directional events)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CE10295 refactor: external-event bar memory kept inside a function.
f_regimeMem() =>
    var int _lastEBosUpBar = na
    var int _lastEBosDnBar = na
    var int _lastEChUpBar  = na
    var int _lastEChDnBar  = na
    if confirmed                       // SCOPE: four stamps, zero branches
        _lastEBosUpBar := eBosUp ? bar_index : _lastEBosUpBar
        _lastEBosDnBar := eBosDn ? bar_index : _lastEBosDnBar
        _lastEChUpBar  := eChochUp ? bar_index : _lastEChUpBar
        _lastEChDnBar  := eChochDn ? bar_index : _lastEChDnBar
    [_lastEBosUpBar, _lastEBosDnBar, _lastEChUpBar, _lastEChDnBar]
[lastEBosUpBar, lastEBosDnBar, lastEChUpBar, lastEChDnBar] = f_regimeMem()
regEBosUp = f_recent(lastEBosUpBar, REGIME_LOOK)
regEBosDn = f_recent(lastEBosDnBar, REGIME_LOOK)
regEChUp  = f_recent(lastEChUpBar,  REGIME_LOOK)
regEChDn  = f_recent(lastEChDnBar,  REGIME_LOOK)
// ◆ FIX-N1b (LOGIC-N1) THE REGIME MEASURES ITS OWN RANGE.
// FIX-P1-12 turned `rngW` into the running high-to-low of the current leg,
// which is deliberately WIDER than the swing-to-swing distance. The regime
// classifier kept reading `rngW`, so `extRngAtr` inflated and CHOP was
// under-detected — more entries fired inside chop. ARCH-5 defines the regime
// on the EXTERNAL swing range (pivot to pivot); that is restored here and the
// dealing range stays what FIX-P1-12 made it.
regRngW   = not na(extRes) and not na(extSup) ? math.abs(extRes - extSup) : na
extRngAtr = not na(regRngW) and atr > 0 ? regRngW / atr : na

regTrending = (extDir == 1 and regEBosUp and not regEChDn) or (extDir == -1 and regEBosDn and not regEChUp)
regTransition = not regTrending and (regEChUp or regEChDn)
// ◆ FIX-P1-8(b): an UNKNOWN external range is not evidence of chop. v3.1.1
// treated `na(extRngAtr)` as CHOP, so the whole engine was suppressed on every
// chart until two external pivots had printed.
// ◆ FIX-T12 CHOP NEEDS THREE AGREEING FACTS, NOT ONE.
// A live XAUUSD M5 chart delivering 4,250 → 4,400 printed "CHOP" and killed an
// A-tier long: the last two EXTERNAL pivots happened to sit in a tight
// consolidation (narrow `extRngAtr`) while the leg itself was enormous, and the
// last external BOS was older than REGIME_LOOK. Chop now also requires the
// CURRENT LEG to be narrow and price to still be INSIDE the external pivot
// range — price beyond it is structure being taken, which is never chop.
legRngAtr = not na(rngW) and atr > 0 ? rngW / atr : na
inExtRng  = not na(extRes) and not na(extSup) and close <= math.max(extRes, extSup) and close >= math.min(extRes, extSup)
// ◆ FIX-T16 A THRUST IS NEVER CHOP. Even after FIX-T12, BTCUSDT and USTEC M5
// still read CHOP while printing new highs: `regTrending` needs an EXTERNAL BOS
// inside REGIME_LOOK, but on M5 an external pivot needs `extPiv` bars of
// right-side confirmation, so a single vertical leg creates NO new pivot to
// break — and the ATR the leg itself inflates then makes both ranges look
// "narrow". Structure is too slow a witness here, so price gets a vote: a net
// move of CHOP_RANGE_ATR × ATR across the memory window is directional by
// definition, whatever the pivots have managed to confirm.
regThrust = atr > 0 and math.abs(ta.mom(close, REGIME_LOOK)) >= CHOP_RANGE_ATR * atr
regChop = not regTrending and not regTransition and not regThrust and not na(extRngAtr) and extRngAtr < CHOP_RANGE_ATR
     and (na(legRngAtr) or legRngAtr < CHOP_RANGE_ATR) and inExtRng
regime = regTrending ? "TRENDING" : regTransition ? "TRANSITION" : regChop ? "CHOP" : "RANGING"

// ◆ FIX-T17 ONE CHOP EXEMPTION, READ IN BOTH PLACES. MODULE 17 armed a setup
// inside chop on the EFFECTIVE sweep grade (which an MSS sets to 4 by itself)
// while MODULE 18 refused the entry unless the RAW grade plus an MSS agreed.
// A live BTC / USTEC chart therefore showed "SWEPT" setups in chop that could
// never fire. Same test at both ends: a strong RAW raid AND a real shift.
instSweepL = lastSslQualRaw >= 3 and recentMssUp
instSweepS = lastBslQualRaw >= 3 and recentMssDn

// structure filter direction: external first, internal fallback
structDir = extDir != 0 ? extDir : msDir

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 12
// FVG ENGINE (chart TF registry, ARCH-7)
// Creation → freshness → partial fill → full fill / inversion / age-out.
// The registry feeds visuals + POI checks; FEM keeps its own locked copy.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
type Fvg
    box   bx
    float top
    float bot
    int   barCreated
    bool  isBull
    bool  partial
    bool  inverted   // FIX-F3: this gap was closed through once and now acts the other way

var bullFvgs = array.new<Fvg>()
var bearFvgs = array.new<Fvg>()

bullFVG = low > high[2]
bearFVG = high < low[2]

f_newFvg(bool isBull, float top, float bot) =>
    c = isBull ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR
    box bx = na
    if showFvg
        bx := box.new(bar_index - 2, top, bar_index + 1, bot,
             border_color = color.new(c, 70), bgcolor = color.new(c, 96),
             border_style = line.style_dotted,
             text = "FVG", text_color = color.new(c, 30), text_size = size.tiny,
             text_halign = text.align_right)
    Fvg.new(bx, top, bot, bar_index, isBull, false, false)

// CE10295 refactor: the registry pushes to / mutates the global bullFvgs and
// bearFvgs arrays, which a function may do. FIX-F3 adds ONE return: the levels
// of a gap that INVERTED on this bar, so MODULE 13 can hand it to the zone
// engine as a POI (the `zones` array is declared after this module and cannot
// be pushed to from here).
// ◆ FIX-F3 INVERTED FVG. A bull FVG that price CLOSES through is not dead — it
// is an IFVG: the same imbalance now acting as supply. v3.2 deleted it.
//   · close through the far edge → INVERT (recolour, "IFVG", fresh clock,
//     moves to the opposite array so the opposite loop manages it)
//   · wick through without a close → the gap is merely FILLED → delete
//   · a gap that is already inverted and gets closed through again → delete
f_fvgRegistry() =>
    float _invSupTop = na      // a bull FVG inverted → supply IFVG
    float _invSupBot = na
    float _invDemTop = na      // a bear FVG inverted → demand IFVG
    float _invDemBot = na
    if confirmed and bullFVG
        array.push(bullFvgs, f_newFvg(true, low, high[2]))
        if array.size(bullFvgs) > fvgMaxKeep
            f0 = array.get(bullFvgs, 0)
            box.delete(f0.bx)
            array.remove(bullFvgs, 0)
    if confirmed and bearFVG
        array.push(bearFvgs, f_newFvg(false, low[2], high))
        if array.size(bearFvgs) > fvgMaxKeep
            f0 = array.get(bearFvgs, 0)
            box.delete(f0.bx)
            array.remove(bearFvgs, 0)

    // update: partial fill (traded inside) · inversion · full fill / age → remove
    if confirmed and array.size(bullFvgs) > 0
        for i = array.size(bullFvgs) - 1 to 0
            f = array.get(bullFvgs, i)
            if bar_index > f.barCreated
                if low <= f.top and not f.partial
                    f.partial := true
                    if not na(f.bx)
                        box.set_text(f.bx, "FVG ·tested")
                if useIfvg and close < f.bot and not f.inverted
                    // INVERT → supply. Fresh clock, hands off to the bear loop.
                    f.inverted   := true
                    f.isBull     := false
                    f.partial    := false
                    f.barCreated := bar_index
                    if not na(f.bx)
                        box.set_border_color(f.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 45))
                        box.set_bgcolor(f.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 92))
                        box.set_text(f.bx, "IFVG ▼")
                        box.set_text_color(f.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 0))
                    _invSupTop := f.top
                    _invSupBot := f.bot
                    array.remove(bullFvgs, i)
                    array.push(bearFvgs, f)
                    if array.size(bearFvgs) > fvgMaxKeep
                        f0 = array.get(bearFvgs, 0)
                        box.delete(f0.bx)
                        array.remove(bearFvgs, 0)
                else if close < f.bot or low <= f.bot or bar_index - f.barCreated > fvgMaxAge
                    box.delete(f.bx)
                    array.remove(bullFvgs, i)
    if confirmed and array.size(bearFvgs) > 0
        for i = array.size(bearFvgs) - 1 to 0
            f = array.get(bearFvgs, i)
            if bar_index > f.barCreated
                if high >= f.bot and not f.partial
                    f.partial := true
                    if not na(f.bx)
                        box.set_text(f.bx, f.inverted ? "IFVG ▼ ·tested" : "FVG ·tested")
                if useIfvg and close > f.top and not f.inverted
                    // INVERT → demand. Fresh clock, hands off to the bull loop.
                    f.inverted   := true
                    f.isBull     := true
                    f.partial    := false
                    f.barCreated := bar_index
                    if not na(f.bx)
                        box.set_border_color(f.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 45))
                        box.set_bgcolor(f.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 92))
                        box.set_text(f.bx, "IFVG ▲")
                        box.set_text_color(f.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 0))
                    _invDemTop := f.top
                    _invDemBot := f.bot
                    array.remove(bearFvgs, i)
                    array.push(bullFvgs, f)
                    if array.size(bullFvgs) > fvgMaxKeep
                        f0 = array.get(bullFvgs, 0)
                        box.delete(f0.bx)
                        array.remove(bullFvgs, 0)
                else if close > f.top or high >= f.top or bar_index - f.barCreated > fvgMaxAge
                    box.delete(f.bx)
                    array.remove(bearFvgs, i)
    [_invSupTop, _invSupBot, _invDemTop, _invDemBot]
[ifvgSupTop, ifvgSupBot, ifvgDemTop, ifvgDemBot] = f_fvgRegistry()

// latest respected FVG per side (FEM execution + machine retest anchor)
// CE10295 refactor: the latest-FVG tracker keeps its state inside and returns
// the eight fields FEM, the machine and the SB anchor rule read.
f_latestFvg() =>
    var float _cFvgBTop = na
    var float _cFvgBBot = na
    var int   _cFvgBBar = na
    var float _cFvgSTop = na
    var float _cFvgSBot = na
    var int   _cFvgSBar = na
    // ◆ SB-4: was the latest FVG created by a DISPLACEMENT leg? The Silver Bullet
    // anchor may only be a clean displacement gap, never an incidental one.
    var bool  _cFvgBDisp = false
    var bool  _cFvgSDisp = false
    if confirmed
        if bullFVG
            _cFvgBTop  := low
            _cFvgBBot  := high[2]
            _cFvgBBar  := bar_index
            _cFvgBDisp := bullBar[1] and body[1] >= math.min(dispFactor, sbDispAtr) * atr   // FIX-V9: only sbFvgOk reads this
        if not na(_cFvgBBot) and close < _cFvgBBot
            _cFvgBTop := na
            _cFvgBBot := na
            _cFvgBBar := na
        if bearFVG
            _cFvgSBot  := high
            _cFvgSTop  := low[2]
            _cFvgSBar  := bar_index
            _cFvgSDisp := bearBar[1] and body[1] >= math.min(dispFactor, sbDispAtr) * atr   // FIX-V9
        if not na(_cFvgSTop) and close > _cFvgSTop
            _cFvgSTop := na
            _cFvgSBot := na
            _cFvgSBar := na
    [_cFvgBTop, _cFvgBBot, _cFvgBBar, _cFvgBDisp, _cFvgSTop, _cFvgSBot, _cFvgSBar, _cFvgSDisp]
[cFvgBTop, cFvgBBot, cFvgBBar, cFvgBDisp, cFvgSTop, cFvgSBot, cFvgSBar, cFvgSDisp] = f_latestFvg()

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 13
// ZONE ENGINE — S/D (imbalance geometry) · OB (origin candle) · RB (wick) ·
// BREAKER / MITIGATION flips. Each zone carries its KIND (ARCH-6) and a
// freshness test-counter (spec §20/§24: heavily mitigated zone = low quality).
// Lifecycle: fresh → tested (×N) → 50% tapped → signaled / spent → violated
// (flip once → breaker/mitigation, second violation deletes).
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
type Zone
    box    bx
    bool   isSupply
    bool   touched
    bool   tapped50
    bool   signaled
    int    score
    int    tap50Bar
    bool   flipped
    int    touchBar
    string kind      // "SD" | "OB" | "RB" | "BRK" | "MIT"
    int    tests     // separate visits (leave + return) consumed
    bool   wasIn     // price overlapped the zone on the previous bar

var zones = array.new<Zone>()

f_pushZone(Zone z) =>
    array.push(zones, z)

// ◆ FIX-T20 OPPOSING ZONES MAY NOT OVERLAP. The duplicate scan only ever
// compared a new zone with zones of its OWN side, so a demand and a supply
// could share a band (a live XAU chart had demand top == supply bottom, which
// made `inDemand` AND `inSupply` true at once). The NEWER zone wins the band;
// the older keeps its own side and is dropped when too little is left to be a
// POI (FIX-T19's height).
// ◆ FIX-V3 (v3.3, audit F-3): the rule was inlined for the S/D branch only —
// OB companions, rejection blocks, IFVGs and breaker / mitigation FLIPS all
// bypassed it (a live BTC chart: demand top == supply-OB bottom == 83990.8).
// One function now, called at every creation site and after every flip.
f_claimBand(bool isSupply, float top, float bot) =>
    if array.size(zones) > 0
        for k = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
            zo = array.get(zones, k)
            if zo.isSupply != isSupply
                float ot = box.get_top(zo.bx)
                float ob = box.get_bottom(zo.bx)
                if not (ob > top or ot < bot)
                    ot := isSupply ? math.min(ot, bot) : ot
                    ob := isSupply ? ob : math.max(ob, top)
                    if ot - ob < minZoneAtr * atr
                        box.delete(zo.bx)
                        array.remove(zones, k)
                    else if isSupply
                        box.set_top(zo.bx, ot)
                    else
                        box.set_bottom(zo.bx, ob)
    array.size(zones)      // CE10235: the branches above return Zone / void — give the function one explicit return

f_newZone(bool isSupply, float top, float bot, bool star, string kind, string txt, int lft) =>
    c  = isSupply ? COL_BEAR : COL_BULL
    x1 = lft != 0 ? lft : bar_index - 2
    thin = kind == "OB"
    bx = box.new(x1, top, bar_index + 1, bot,
         border_color = color.new(c, thin ? 75 : 55), bgcolor = color.new(c, thin ? 96 : 93),
         text = txt, text_color = color.new(c, thin ? 35 : 0), text_size = drawSize,
         text_halign = text.align_right,
         text_valign = isSupply ? text.align_top : text.align_bottom)
    Zone.new(bx, isSupply, false, false, false, star ? 3 : 2, -1, false, -1, kind, 0, false)

// ---- update zones, 50% tap engine (edge-triggered, V2 FIX-10), flips ----------
// CE10295 refactor: the zone lifecycle loop runs in a function and hands back
// the six values the rest of the script consumes. sigSLLong / sigSLShort stay
// ordinary globals afterwards, so MODULE 14 / 15 / 17 can still overwrite them.
f_zoneUpdate() =>
    sigSDLong  = false
    sigSDShort = false
    float sigSLLong  = na
    float sigSLShort = na
    bool zoneInvalidatedUp = false   // demand-side POI violated this bar (machine invalidation)
    bool zoneInvalidatedDn = false
    // FIX-V3: a flipped block claims its band AFTER the loop (mutating `zones`
    // inside a descending index loop could run the index past the new size)
    float flipTop = na
    float flipBot = na
    bool  flipSup = false

    if array.size(zones) > 0
        for i = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
            z    = array.get(zones, i)
            top  = box.get_top(z.bx)
            bot  = box.get_bottom(z.bx)
            midP = (top + bot) / 2
            if z.isSupply
                if close > top and confirmed
                    zoneInvalidatedDn := true
                    if showFlip and not z.flipped
                        // BREAKER needs sweep + structure break before failure (FIX-14)
                        isBrkS = recentSsl and recentStructUp
                        z.isSupply := false
                        z.flipped  := true
                        z.touched  := false
                        z.touchBar := -1
                        z.tapped50 := false
                        z.signaled := false
                        z.tap50Bar := -1
                        z.tests    := 0
                        z.wasIn    := false
                        z.kind     := isBrkS ? "BRK" : "MIT"
                        z.score    := isBrkS ? 3 : 2
                        box.set_border_color(z.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 55))
                        box.set_bgcolor(z.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 93))
                        box.set_text(z.bx, z.kind)
                        box.set_text_color(z.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 0))
                        box.set_text_valign(z.bx, text.align_bottom)
                        flipTop := top                                  // FIX-V3
                        flipBot := bot
                        flipSup := false
                    else
                        box.delete(z.bx)
                        array.remove(zones, i)
                    continue
                // SCOPE: same lifecycle, written as assignments
                inZ = high >= bot
                z.touchBar := inZ and not z.touched ? bar_index : z.touchBar
                z.touched  := inZ or z.touched
                bool _newTest = inZ and not z.wasIn and confirmed
                z.tests    := _newTest ? z.tests + 1 : z.tests
                if _newTest and z.tests > zoneMaxTests and not z.signaled
                    z.signaled := true                               // spent by repeated tests
                    box.set_bgcolor(z.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 96))
                    box.set_text(z.bx, z.kind + " ·spent")
                z.wasIn    := confirmed ? inZ : z.wasIn
                z.tapped50 := high < bot ? false : z.tapped50
                bool _tap  = high >= midP and not z.tapped50
                z.tapped50 := _tap ? true : z.tapped50
                z.tap50Bar := _tap ? bar_index : z.tap50Bar
                if not z.signaled and z.tapped50 and bar_index - z.tap50Bar <= confirmWin and zoneBearConf and high >= bot and confirmed
                    sigSDShort := true
                    sigSLShort := math.max(high, top) + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                    z.signaled := true
                    box.set_bgcolor(z.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 94))
            else
                if close < bot and confirmed
                    zoneInvalidatedUp := true
                    if showFlip and not z.flipped
                        isBrkD = recentBsl and recentStructDn
                        z.isSupply := true
                        z.flipped  := true
                        z.touched  := false
                        z.touchBar := -1
                        z.tapped50 := false
                        z.signaled := false
                        z.tap50Bar := -1
                        z.tests    := 0
                        z.wasIn    := false
                        z.kind     := isBrkD ? "BRK" : "MIT"
                        z.score    := isBrkD ? 3 : 2
                        box.set_border_color(z.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 55))
                        box.set_bgcolor(z.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 93))
                        box.set_text(z.bx, z.kind)
                        box.set_text_color(z.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 0))
                        box.set_text_valign(z.bx, text.align_top)
                        flipTop := top                                  // FIX-V3
                        flipBot := bot
                        flipSup := true
                    else
                        box.delete(z.bx)
                        array.remove(zones, i)
                    continue
                inZ = low <= top                                 // SCOPE (see above)
                z.touchBar := inZ and not z.touched ? bar_index : z.touchBar
                z.touched  := inZ or z.touched
                bool _newTest = inZ and not z.wasIn and confirmed
                z.tests    := _newTest ? z.tests + 1 : z.tests
                if _newTest and z.tests > zoneMaxTests and not z.signaled
                    z.signaled := true
                    box.set_bgcolor(z.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 96))
                    box.set_text(z.bx, z.kind + " ·spent")
                z.wasIn    := confirmed ? inZ : z.wasIn
                z.tapped50 := low > top ? false : z.tapped50
                bool _tap  = low <= midP and not z.tapped50
                z.tapped50 := _tap ? true : z.tapped50
                z.tap50Bar := _tap ? bar_index : z.tap50Bar
                if not z.signaled and z.tapped50 and bar_index - z.tap50Bar <= confirmWin and zoneBullConf and low <= top and confirmed
                    sigSDLong := true
                    sigSLLong := math.min(low, bot) - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                    z.signaled := true
                    box.set_bgcolor(z.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 94))
    if not na(flipTop)
        f_claimBand(flipSup, flipTop, flipBot)               // FIX-V3
    [sigSDLong, sigSDShort, sigSLLong, sigSLShort, zoneInvalidatedUp, zoneInvalidatedDn]
[zuSDL, zuSDS, zuSLL, zuSLS, zuInvU, zuInvD] = f_zoneUpdate()
sigSDLong  = zuSDL
sigSDShort = zuSDS
bool zoneInvalidatedUp = zuInvU
bool zoneInvalidatedDn = zuInvD

// ◆ FIX-P0-5 ONE STOP PER ENGINE.
// v3.1.1 had a single `sigSLLong` / `sigSLShort` pair that the zone engine,
// CRT, turtle soup, the trendline, FEM and the machine all wrote to in source
// order — last writer wins. MODULE 18 then muted engines on tier / cooldown,
// but it never unwound their stop. So a CRT candidate that the tier filter had
// already thrown away could still hand its stop to the demand-zone signal that
// actually fired. Each engine keeps its own now, and MODULE 19 picks the stop
// belonging to the signal that SURVIVED the filters.
float slSDLong  = zuSLL
float slSDShort = zuSLS
float slCRTLong  = na
float slCRTShort = na
float slTBSLong  = na
float slTBSShort = na
float slTLLong   = na
float slTLShort  = na
float slFEMLong  = na
float slFEMShort = na
float slMCLong   = na
float slMCShort  = na
float slLTLong   = na      // FIX-V6 (v3.3): the trap engine's own stop
float slLTShort  = na

// ---- create S/D zones (FVG-imbalance geometry, V2 FIX-9) + OB companions ------
// CE10295 refactor: zone creation only pushes to the global `zones` array,
// which a function may mutate, so nothing needs returning.
f_zoneCreate() =>
    // FIX-F2: the displacement candle (bar[1]) must also carry volume when the
    // volume gate is on. `volOK[1]` — the candle that MADE the imbalance.
    dispUp  = bullBar[1] and body[1] >= dispFactor * atr and volOK[1]
    dispDn  = bearBar[1] and body[1] >= dispFactor * atr and volOK[1]
    newDemand = showZones and bullFVG and dispUp
    newSupply = showZones and bearFVG and dispDn

    if newDemand and confirmed
        float zTop = low
        float zBot = math.max(high[2], low - maxZoneAtr * atr)
        bs = not na(lastRes) and close[1] > lastRes
        newScore = bs ? 3 : 2
        dup = false
        if array.size(zones) > 0
            for i = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
                z = array.get(zones, i)
                if not z.isSupply and not (zBot > box.get_top(z.bx) or zTop < box.get_bottom(z.bx))
                    bool _keep = z.score >= newScore and not z.signaled      // SCOPE
                    dup := _keep or dup
                    if not _keep
                        box.delete(z.bx)
                        array.remove(zones, i)
        if not dup and zTop - zBot >= minZoneAtr * atr      // FIX-T19
            f_claimBand(false, zTop, zBot)                   // FIX-T20 / FIX-V3
            f_pushZone(f_newZone(false, zTop, zBot, bs, "SD", bs ? "DEMAND ★HP" : "", 0))
            // ORDER BLOCK companion: the opposing (bearish) origin candle before
            // the displacement leg — its own geometry, below the imbalance
            if showOB and bearBar[2]
                float obTop = high[2]
                float obBot = math.max(low[2], high[2] - maxZoneAtr * atr)
                if obTop - obBot >= minZoneAtr * atr and obTop <= zBot + atr * 0.01
                    f_claimBand(false, obTop, obBot)         // FIX-V3
                    f_pushZone(f_newZone(false, obTop, obBot, false, "OB", "OB", bar_index - 2))

    if newSupply and confirmed
        float zBot = high
        float zTop = math.min(low[2], high + maxZoneAtr * atr)
        bs = not na(lastSup) and close[1] < lastSup
        newScore = bs ? 3 : 2
        dup = false
        if array.size(zones) > 0
            for i = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
                z = array.get(zones, i)
                if z.isSupply and not (zBot > box.get_top(z.bx) or zTop < box.get_bottom(z.bx))
                    bool _keep = z.score >= newScore and not z.signaled      // SCOPE
                    dup := _keep or dup
                    if not _keep
                        box.delete(z.bx)
                        array.remove(zones, i)
        if not dup and zTop - zBot >= minZoneAtr * atr      // FIX-T19
            f_claimBand(true, zTop, zBot)                    // FIX-T20 / FIX-V3
            f_pushZone(f_newZone(true, zTop, zBot, bs, "SD", bs ? "SUPPLY ★HP" : "", 0))
            if showOB and bullBar[2]
                float obBot = low[2]
                float obTop = math.min(high[2], low[2] + maxZoneAtr * atr)
                if obTop - obBot >= minZoneAtr * atr and obBot >= zTop - atr * 0.01
                    f_claimBand(true, obTop, obBot)          // FIX-V3
                    f_pushZone(f_newZone(true, obTop, obBot, false, "OB", "OB", bar_index - 2))

    // ---- rejection blocks (swept pivot wick + displacement away, V2 FIX-24) -------
    rbSwpH = high[pivLen] > ta.highest(high, pivLen * 2)[pivLen + 1]
    rbDspH = ta.lowest(low, pivLen) <= high[pivLen] - dispFactor * atr
    rbSwpL = low[pivLen] < ta.lowest(low, pivLen * 2)[pivLen + 1]
    rbDspL = ta.highest(high, pivLen) >= low[pivLen] + dispFactor * atr
    rbNew = false
    if showRB and confirmed
        if not na(ph)
            float rbTop = high[pivLen]
            float rbBot = math.max(open[pivLen], close[pivLen])
            if rbTop - rbBot >= minWickAtr * atr and rbSwpH and rbDspH
                rbDup = false
                for z in zones                 // SCOPE: `for in`, no size guard
                    if z.isSupply and not (rbBot > box.get_top(z.bx) or rbTop < box.get_bottom(z.bx))
                        rbDup := true
                        break
                if not rbDup
                    f_claimBand(true, rbTop, rbBot)          // FIX-V3
                    f_pushZone(f_newZone(true, rbTop, rbBot, false, "RB", "RB", bar_index - pivLen))
                    rbNew := true
        if not na(pl)
            float rbBot2 = low[pivLen]
            float rbTop2 = math.min(open[pivLen], close[pivLen])
            if rbTop2 - rbBot2 >= minWickAtr * atr and rbSwpL and rbDspL
                rbDup2 = false
                for z in zones
                    if not z.isSupply and not (rbBot2 > box.get_top(z.bx) or rbTop2 < box.get_bottom(z.bx))
                        rbDup2 := true
                        break
                if not rbDup2
                    f_claimBand(false, rbTop2, rbBot2)       // FIX-V3
                    f_pushZone(f_newZone(false, rbTop2, rbBot2, false, "RB", "RB", bar_index - pivLen))
                    rbNew := true

    // ---- cap zone count per side (drop oldest) -------------------------------------
    // ◆ FIX-P1-8(c): v3.1.1 dropped at most ONE zone per bar per side. A burst
    // (a new S/D plus its OB companion plus a rejection block on one bar) left
    // the side permanently over `maxZones`. Drain until the cap is actually met.
    // ---- FIX-F3 IFVG → POI zone ----------------------------------------------------
    // The FVG registry reports a gap that inverted on this bar; it becomes a
    // zone of kind "IFVG" so NEXT level, the machine's POI tap, retest scoring
    // and the flip lifecycle all treat it like any other POI. Score 2 (the
    // gap was violated once — same standing as a MITIGATION block).
    ifvgNew = false
    if useIfvg and confirmed
        if not na(ifvgSupTop)
            ifDup = false
            for z in zones                     // SCOPE
                if z.isSupply and not (ifvgSupBot > box.get_top(z.bx) or ifvgSupTop < box.get_bottom(z.bx))
                    ifDup := true
                    break
            if not ifDup and ifvgSupTop - ifvgSupBot >= minZoneAtr * atr
                f_claimBand(true, ifvgSupTop, ifvgSupBot)    // FIX-V3
                f_pushZone(f_newZone(true, ifvgSupTop, ifvgSupBot, false, "IFVG", "IFVG", bar_index - 2))
                ifvgNew := true
        if not na(ifvgDemTop)
            ifDup2 = false
            for z in zones
                if not z.isSupply and not (ifvgDemBot > box.get_top(z.bx) or ifvgDemTop < box.get_bottom(z.bx))
                    ifDup2 := true
                    break
            if not ifDup2 and ifvgDemTop - ifvgDemBot >= minZoneAtr * atr
                f_claimBand(false, ifvgDemTop, ifvgDemBot)   // FIX-V3
                f_pushZone(f_newZone(false, ifvgDemTop, ifvgDemBot, false, "IFVG", "IFVG", bar_index - 2))
                ifvgNew := true

    // SCOPE: one descending pass keeps the NEWEST `maxZones` per side and drops
