    // the rest — the same result as the old census plus two drain loops.
    // FIX-V12 (F-15): rejection blocks are capped at two per side — they were
    // the bulk of the box pile-up around price on a live BTC chart
    if (newDemand or newSupply or rbNew or ifvgNew) and array.size(zones) > 0
        int dKeep = 0
        int sKeep = 0
        int dRb   = 0
        int sRb   = 0
        for i = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
            z = array.get(zones, i)
            sKeep := z.isSupply ? sKeep + 1 : sKeep
            dKeep := z.isSupply ? dKeep : dKeep + 1
            sRb   := z.isSupply and z.kind == "RB" ? sRb + 1 : sRb
            dRb   := not z.isSupply and z.kind == "RB" ? dRb + 1 : dRb
            if (z.isSupply ? sKeep : dKeep) > maxZones or (z.isSupply ? sRb : dRb) > 2
                box.delete(z.bx)
                array.remove(zones, i)
f_zoneCreate()

// ---- NEXT buy / sell zone (nearest un-spent POI per side) ----------------------
// CE10295 refactor: nearest un-spent POI per side, scanned in a function.
f_nextZones() =>
    float nbTop = na
    float nbBot = na
    float nsTop = na
    float nsBot = na
    bool  nbFresh = false
    bool  nsFresh = false
    // grade + kind of the chosen zone, for the NEXT BUY / NEXT SELL labels
    int    nbScore = 0
    int    nsScore = 0
    string nbKind  = ""
    string nsKind  = ""
    for z in zones                     // SCOPE: `for in` is empty-safe
        t = box.get_top(z.bx)
        b = box.get_bottom(z.bx)
        if not z.isSupply and not z.signaled and b <= close and (na(nbTop) or t > nbTop)
            nbTop := t
            nbBot := b
            nbFresh := not z.touched or z.touchBar == bar_index
            nbScore := z.score
            nbKind  := z.kind
        if z.isSupply and not z.signaled and t >= close and (na(nsBot) or b < nsBot)
            nsTop := t
            nsBot := b
            nsFresh := not z.touched or z.touchBar == bar_index
            nsScore := z.score
            nsKind  := z.kind
    // ◆ FIX-F6: the working HTF FVG POI competes for the NEXT level. Same
    // candidacy rule as a chart zone (not entirely on the far side of price),
    // same "nearest wins" rule. Grade T3 — an HTF imbalance outranks a chart-TF
    // one; fresh = no separate visit yet.
    if nextInclHtf and not na(hPoiBTop) and hPoiBBot <= close and hPoiBTop - hPoiBBot <= hPoiNextMaxAtr * atr and (na(nbTop) or hPoiBTop > nbTop)   // FIX-V13
        nbTop   := hPoiBTop
        nbBot   := hPoiBBot
        nbFresh := nz(hPoiBIn) == 0
        nbScore := 3
        nbKind  := "HTF"
    if nextInclHtf and not na(hPoiSTop) and hPoiSTop >= close and hPoiSTop - hPoiSBot <= hPoiNextMaxAtr * atr and (na(nsBot) or hPoiSBot < nsBot)   // FIX-V13
        nsTop   := hPoiSTop
        nsBot   := hPoiSBot
        nsFresh := nz(hPoiSIn) == 0
        nsScore := 3
        nsKind  := "HTF"
    [nbTop, nbBot, nsTop, nsBot, nbFresh, nsFresh, nbScore, nsScore, nbKind, nsKind]
[nbTop, nbBot, nsTop, nsBot, nbFresh, nsFresh, nbScore, nsScore, nbKind, nsKind] = f_nextZones()
touchNextBuy  = confirmed and not na(nbTop) and not na(nbTop[1]) and low <= nbTop and low[1] > nbTop[1]
touchNextSell = confirmed and not na(nsBot) and not na(nsBot[1]) and high >= nsBot and high[1] < nsBot[1]

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 14
// TRIGGER-CANDIDATE ENGINES — CRT · Turtle soup · Trendline sweep ·
// Inside bar · Doji. All context-gated, cooldown-limited, closed-bar only.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var int lastCrtLongBar  = na
var int lastCrtShortBar = na
var int lastTbsLongBar  = na
var int lastTbsShortBar = na
crtLongCd  = na(lastCrtLongBar)  or bar_index - lastCrtLongBar  > sigCooldown
crtShortCd = na(lastCrtShortBar) or bar_index - lastCrtShortBar > sigCooldown
tbsLongCd  = na(lastTbsLongBar)  or bar_index - lastTbsLongBar  > sigCooldown
tbsShortCd = na(lastTbsShortBar) or bar_index - lastTbsShortBar > sigCooldown

// CRT: Range → Raid → Reclaim → Directional delivery (all four required)
crtC1Big     = rng[2] >= crtRangeAtr * atr
crtBullSetup = crtC1Big and low[1] < low[2] and close[1] > low[2] and close[1] < high[2] and low[2] - low[1] >= minWickAtr * atr
crtBearSetup = crtC1Big and high[1] > high[2] and close[1] < high[2] and close[1] > low[2] and high[1] - high[2] >= minWickAtr * atr
sigCRTLong  = showCRT and confirmed and crtBullSetup and bullBar and close > high[1] and body >= crtDispAtr * atr and crtLongCd
sigCRTShort = showCRT and confirmed and crtBearSetup and bearBar and close < low[1] and body >= crtDispAtr * atr and crtShortCd
slCRTLong  := sigCRTLong  ? low[1] - SL_PAD_ATR * atr  : slCRTLong     // SCOPE
slCRTShort := sigCRTShort ? high[1] + SL_PAD_ATR * atr : slCRTShort

// Turtle soup: OLD (min AND max age) extreme + raid + decisive reclaim.
// Age math audited — see MODULE 9 note. No sign bug.
tbsLow  = ta.lowest(low, tbsLen)[1]
tbsHigh = ta.highest(high, tbsLen)[1]
tbsLowAge  = -ta.lowestbars(low, tbsLen)[1] + 1
tbsHighAge = -ta.highestbars(high, tbsLen)[1] + 1
sigTBSLong  = showTBS and confirmed and low < tbsLow and close > tbsLow and bullBar and f_tbsAgeOk(tbsLowAge) and close >= low + 0.5 * rng and tbsLongCd
sigTBSShort = showTBS and confirmed and high > tbsHigh and close < tbsHigh and bearBar and f_tbsAgeOk(tbsHighAge) and close <= high - 0.5 * rng and tbsShortCd
slTBSLong  := sigTBSLong  ? low - SL_PAD_ATR * atr  : slTBSLong        // SCOPE
slTBSShort := sigTBSShort ? high + SL_PAD_ATR * atr : slTBSShort

// Trendline liquidity: candidate → validated → sweep entry / break (FIX-19)
float tlUpVal = not na(tlUpSlope) ? tlUpPrice + tlUpSlope * (bar_index - tlUpBar) : na
float tlDnVal = not na(tlDnSlope) ? tlDnPrice + tlDnSlope * (bar_index - tlDnBar) : na
sigTLLong  = false
sigTLShort = false
tlUpBreak  = false
tlDnBreak  = false
// ◆ FIX-P1-4 TOUCHES ARE EDGE-TRIGGERED AND LINES EXPIRE.
// v3.1.1 did `tlUpTouch += 1` on EVERY bar that closed within NEAR_LVL_ATR of
// the line. Price loitering near a trendline for eight bars scored eight
// touches, so `tlTouchMin` was meaningless and the `>= 2` gate was satisfied
// by drift alone — the trendline engine spammed. A touch now requires price to
// LEAVE the line's neighbourhood and come BACK. A line that is never broken
// also used to live forever; it now ages out.
var bool tlUpWasNear = false
var bool tlDnWasNear = false
if not na(tlUpVal) and confirmed
    bool nearUp = low <= tlUpVal + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
    if close < tlUpVal
        tlUpBreak := true
        if not na(tlUpLn)
            line.set_extend(tlUpLn, extend.none)
            line.set_x2(tlUpLn, bar_index)
            line.set_style(tlUpLn, line.style_dashed)
        tlUpLn    := na
        tlUpSlope := na
        tlUpWasNear := false
    else
        if nearUp and not tlUpWasNear
            tlUpTouch += 1                       // one touch per approach
        if low < tlUpVal and tlSweepEnt and tlUpTouch > tlTouchMin and tlUpTouch >= 2 and (not tlNeedConf or zoneBullConf)
            sigTLLong := true
            slTLLong  := low - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
        tlUpWasNear := nearUp
    // stale-line expiry: an anchor older than tlMaxAge bars stops being tradable
    if not na(tlUpBar) and bar_index - tlUpBar > tlMaxAge
        line.delete(tlUpLn)
        tlUpLn    := na
        tlUpSlope := na
if not na(tlDnVal) and confirmed
    bool nearDn = high >= tlDnVal - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
    if close > tlDnVal
        tlDnBreak := true
        if not na(tlDnLn)
            line.set_extend(tlDnLn, extend.none)
            line.set_x2(tlDnLn, bar_index)
            line.set_style(tlDnLn, line.style_dashed)
        tlDnLn    := na
        tlDnSlope := na
        tlDnWasNear := false
    else
        if nearDn and not tlDnWasNear
            tlDnTouch += 1
        if high > tlDnVal and tlSweepEnt and tlDnTouch > tlTouchMin and tlDnTouch >= 2 and (not tlNeedConf or zoneBearConf)
            sigTLShort := true
            slTLShort  := high + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
        tlDnWasNear := nearDn
    if not na(tlDnBar) and bar_index - tlDnBar > tlMaxAge
        line.delete(tlDnLn)
        tlDnLn    := na
        tlDnSlope := na
if not na(tlUpLn) and tlUpTouch >= 1
    line.set_style(tlUpLn, line.style_solid)
if not na(tlDnLn) and tlDnTouch >= 1
    line.set_style(tlDnLn, line.style_solid)

// Inside bar breakout (context-gated: structure + HTF + strong close, FIX-22)
// CE10295 refactor: the inside-bar stage machine keeps its state inside.
f_insideBar() =>
    var int   _ibStage = 0
    var float _mHigh   = na
    var float _mLow    = na
    var float _ibHigh  = na
    var float _ibLow   = na
    var int   _ibStart = na
    _sig3Long  = false
    _sig3Short = false
    _mMid = (_mHigh + _mLow) / 2
    if confirmed
        if _ibStage == 1
            if high < _mHigh and low > _mLow and low >= _mMid
                _ibHigh := high
                _ibLow  := low
                _ibStage := 2
            else
                _ibStage := 0
        else if _ibStage == 2
            if close > _ibHigh         // SCOPE
                _sig3Long := showS3 and bullBar and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr and close >= high - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT * rng and structDir >= 0 and biasOkL   // FIX-V1
                _ibStage := 0
            else if close < _ibLow or bar_index - _ibStart > IB_WAIT_BARS
                _ibStage := 0
        else if _ibStage == -1
            if high < _mHigh and low > _mLow and high <= _mMid
                _ibHigh := high
                _ibLow  := low
                _ibStage := -2
            else
                _ibStage := 0
        else if _ibStage == -2
            if close < _ibLow          // SCOPE
                _sig3Short := showS3 and bearBar and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr and close <= low + STRONG_CLOSE_PCT * rng and structDir <= 0 and biasOkS   // FIX-V1
                _ibStage := 0
            else if close > _ibHigh or bar_index - _ibStart > IB_WAIT_BARS
                _ibStage := 0
        if showS3 and _ibStage == 0 and brokeUp and body >= ibBodyAtr * atr
            _ibStage := 1
            _mHigh := high
            _mLow  := low
            _ibStart := bar_index
        else if showS3 and _ibStage == 0 and brokeDn and body >= ibBodyAtr * atr
            _ibStage := -1
            _mHigh := high
            _mLow  := low
            _ibStart := bar_index
    [_sig3Long, _sig3Short]
[t3L, t3S] = f_insideBar()
sig3Long  = t3L
sig3Short = t3S

// Doji reversal (compression + level raid + displacement confirm, FIX-23)
dojiRecent = math.sum(doji ? 1 : 0, DOJI_WINDOW)
ctxBias = structDir != 0 ? structDir : htfB
dojiCompressed = ta.sma(rng, DOJI_WINDOW)[1] <= atr
dojiRaidRes    = not na(lastRes) and ta.highest(high, DOJI_WINDOW) > lastRes
dojiRaidSup    = not na(lastSup) and ta.lowest(low, DOJI_WINDOW) < lastSup
// CE10295 refactor: the doji watcher runs in a function; the two flags stay
// ordinary globals afterwards so MODULE 18 can still gate them.
f_dojiRev() =>
    _sig4Short = false
    _sig4Long  = false
    if showS4 and confirmed
        atRes   = not na(lastRes) and high >= lastRes - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr and close <= lastRes + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
        atSup   = not na(lastSup) and low <= lastSup + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr and close >= lastSup - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
        driftUp = close[1] > close[DOJI_WINDOW + 1]
        driftDn = close[1] < close[DOJI_WINDOW + 1]
        dispConf = body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr
        _sig4Short := ctxBias == -1 and dojiRecent[1] >= dojiMin and atRes and driftUp and dojiCompressed and dojiRaidRes and bearConfirm and dispConf
        _sig4Long  := ctxBias == 1 and dojiRecent[1] >= dojiMin and atSup and driftDn and dojiCompressed and dojiRaidSup and bullConfirm and dispConf
    [_sig4Long, _sig4Short]
[t4L, t4S] = f_dojiRev()
sig4Long  = t4L
sig4Short = t4S

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 15
// FRACTAL ENTRY MODEL (FEM) — HTF POI → tap → LTF shift → LOCKED LTF FVG →
// retest execution. V2 FIX-11 preserved: the first valid FVG is locked, later
// FVGs never overwrite it, a close through it kills the chain.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var int   femL    = 0
var int   femLBar = na
var float femLTop = na
var float femLBot = na
var int   femLFvg = na
var int   femS    = 0
var int   femSBar = na
var float femSTop = na
var float femSBot = na
var int   femSFvg = na
sigFEMLong  = false
sigFEMShort = false
if useFEM and confirmed
    if femL >= 1 and (na(hPoiBTop) or bar_index - femLBar > femWait)
        femL := 0
    if femL == 0 and htfLive and not na(hPoiBTop) and low <= hPoiBTop and close > hPoiBBot
        femL    := 1
        femLBar := bar_index
        femLTop := na
        femLBot := na
        femLFvg := na
    if femL == 1 and (cisdUp or chochUp or bosUp or mssUp)
        femL := 2
        if not na(cFvgBTop) and cFvgBBar > femLBar
            femLTop := cFvgBTop
            femLBot := cFvgBBot
            femLFvg := cFvgBBar
            femL    := 3
    if femL == 2 and bullFVG
        femLTop := low
        femLBot := high[2]
        femLFvg := bar_index
        femL    := 3
    if femL == 3
        if close < femLBot
            femL := 0
        else if bar_index > femLFvg and low <= femLTop and close > femLBot and zoneBullConf
            sigFEMLong := true
            slFEMLong  := math.min(low, femLBot) - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
            femL := 0
    if femS >= 1 and (na(hPoiSTop) or bar_index - femSBar > femWait)
        femS := 0
    if femS == 0 and htfLive and not na(hPoiSTop) and high >= hPoiSBot and close < hPoiSTop
        femS    := 1
        femSBar := bar_index
        femSTop := na
        femSBot := na
        femSFvg := na
    if femS == 1 and (cisdDn or chochDn or bosDn or mssDn)
        femS := 2
        if not na(cFvgSTop) and cFvgSBar > femSBar
            femSTop := cFvgSTop
            femSBot := cFvgSBot
            femSFvg := cFvgSBar
            femS    := 3
    if femS == 2 and bearFVG
        femSBot := high
        femSTop := low[2]
        femSFvg := bar_index
        femS    := 3
    if femS == 3
        if close > femSTop
            femS := 0
        else if bar_index > femSFvg and high >= femSBot and close < femSTop and zoneBearConf
            sigFEMShort := true
            slFEMShort  := math.max(high, femSTop) + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
            femS := 0

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 15A
// ◆ FIX-V2 (v3.3) MASTER DIRECTION LOCK. The machine state and the tracked
// trade are declared HERE (moved up from MODULES 17 / 19, values unchanged) so
// that the trap engine, the machine arm and the final gate can all read the
// previous bar's narrative: an open trade, or a setup AT POI or beyond, owns
// the chart. The opposite side may not arm, fire or confirm a trap.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var int    suL       = 0
var int    suLBar    = na
var float  suLAnchorT = na    // retest anchor top (locked FVG or POI zone)
var float  suLAnchorB = na
var int    suLFvgBar  = na    // anchor creation bar (na = zone anchor)
var float  suLSweepLvl = na
var float  suLSweepExt = na
var string suLReason  = ""    // last invalidation / expiry reason
var int    suS       = 0
var int    suSBar    = na
var float  suSAnchorT = na
var float  suSAnchorB = na
var int    suSFvgBar  = na
var float  suSSweepLvl = na
var float  suSSweepExt = na
var string suSReason  = ""
var float trdEntry = na
var float trdSL    = na
var float trdTP1   = na
var float trdTP2   = na
var float trdTP3   = na
var int   trdDir   = 0
var bool  trdTp1Hit = false
// ◆ SB-6 trade state: only an SB-tagged trade uses the extended exit engine
var bool  trdTp2Hit = false
var bool  trdIsSb   = false
var int   trdSbWin  = 0     // the window whose close is this trade's time stop
var int   trdShiftG = 0     // grade of the shift that produced the entry
var float trdLiqTgt = na    // the planned liquidity target (TP3)
dirLock = not oneDir ? 0 : trdDir != 0 ? trdDir : suL >= 2 ? 1 : suS >= 2 ? -1 : 0
lockOkL = dirLock >= 0
lockOkS = dirLock <= 0
// ◆ FIX-W2 (v3.4, OP-2): the block moved ABOVE the score module. MODULE 16
// now grades the POI the LIVE SETUP is standing on, which it can only do if
// the machine state is already declared. Every write still happens in
// MODULE 17 / 19, so the score reads the setup carried from the previous
// bar — the same idiom FIX-P1-2 uses for `sbTagL`.

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 16
// CONFLUENCE SCORE (FIX-21 base, ARCH dedup) — ALIGNMENT, NOT WIN PROBABILITY
//   Context 30   HTF 10 · external structure 10 · session 5 · P/D 5
//   Liquidity 20 sweep in play 10 · engineered pool/session/PDL 5 · quality≥3 5
//   POI 20       in zone 10 (near 5) · fresh 5 · OTE 5
//   Trigger 20   MSS 12 / CHoCH 9 / BOS 5 · CISD +8 only when it is a
//                SEPARATE event (different bar than the shift) · capped 20.
//                Displacement is NOT scored separately — it is embedded in
//                MSS / structural-break definitions (no double counting).
//   Execution 10 retest 5 · confirmation candle 5
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ◆ FIX-P1-8(a): a non-intraday chart has no killzone, so it should not be
// handed the full killzone bonus. v3.1.1 gave daily bars a flat 5, inflating
// every daily context score by the amount an actual London/NY session earns.
sesPts = not timeframe.isintraday ? 3 : inSB or inNY or inLon ? 5 : inAsia ? 2 : 0
// the A+ session test: intraday must be in a real killzone; higher timeframes
// are exempt rather than permanently disqualified by the reduced sesPts above
sesTierOK = not timeframe.isintraday or sesPts >= 5

// ◆ FIX-W2 (v3.4, OP-2) THE SCORE GRADES THE POI THE SETUP IS STANDING ON.
// `poiLong` / `retestLong` read `nbTop / nbBot` — the NEXT UNSPENT ZONE marker.
// But a live setup's anchor is `suLAnchorT / B`, and after FIX-V14 (fastShift)
// that anchor is the SHIFT-LEG FVG, which is not in the `zones` registry at
// all. So on exactly the entries v3.3.1 was built to enable, the entry bar
// scored `inDemand = false` and `retestLong = false` and lost up to 20 points
// — enough to fail `minScore` and be handed back to SHIFTED by FIX-V8, where
// it died on the retest clock. Live XAU / BTC / USOIL M5 charts all read
// "score 22 / 34 / 42 < 50" with a locked anchor sitting under price.
// From stage 2 (AT POI) the score reads the anchor; idle setups keep the
// NEXT-zone marker, so nothing about the idle-bar reading changes.
useAnchL = suL >= 2 and not na(suLAnchorT) and not na(suLAnchorB)
useAnchS = suS >= 2 and not na(suSAnchorT) and not na(suSAnchorB)
dmdTop = useAnchL ? suLAnchorT : nbTop
dmdBot = useAnchL ? suLAnchorB : nbBot
supTop = useAnchS ? suSAnchorT : nsTop
supBot = useAnchS ? suSAnchorB : nsBot
// an anchor the setup locked and has not yet entered from is fresh by
// construction; a NEXT-zone marker keeps the registry's own freshness flag
dmdFresh = useAnchL ? suL <= 3 : nbFresh
supFresh = useAnchS ? suS <= 3 : nsFresh
// A retest of a locked anchor normally WICKS into it and closes back out on the
// right side — that is the whole point of the retest — so the anchor test is
// "traded into it and held", not "closed inside it". The NEXT-zone branch keeps
// v3.3's exact wording so an idle bar reads exactly as it did before.
inDemand   = useAnchL ? (low <= dmdTop and close >= dmdBot) : (not na(nbTop) and close <= nbTop)
nearDemand = not na(dmdTop) and not inDemand and math.abs(close - dmdTop) <= POI_NEAR_ATR * atr
inSupply   = useAnchS ? (high >= supBot and close <= supTop) : (not na(nsBot) and close >= nsBot)
nearSupply = not na(supBot) and not inSupply and math.abs(supBot - close) <= POI_NEAR_ATR * atr
// ◆ FIX-V4 (v3.3, audit F-4): the next demand and the next supply within one
// ATR of each other is indecision, not a POI (a live USTEC chart had them 0.05%
// apart and both sides scored "at POI"). Neither side earns POI points, the
// machine may not tap, and the verdict says why.
// FIX-V13: only CHART-TF zones can conflict — an HTF gap legitimately contains
// smaller opposing LTF zones (it is not in the `zones` registry, so FIX-V3's
// band claim never separates them).
// ◆ FIX-W3 (v3.4, OP-3) A CONFLICT IS RESOLVED, NOT MUTUALLY KILLED.
// FIX-V4 zeroed POI points on BOTH sides whenever the next demand and the next
// supply were within one ATR. Inside any balanced range that is the NORMAL
// geometry, not indecision: a live NAS100 M5 chart had demand 30325.8-30349.2
// against supply 30367.8-30381.3 — about one ATR apart — so the chart could
// never reach `minScore` in either direction, permanently.
// The side that agrees with the narrative (HTF bias, else the direction lock,
// else external structure) keeps its points; the other loses them. Only when
// there is no tie-break left at all is it still a mutual kill.
poiConflictRaw = not na(nbTop) and not na(nsBot) and nbKind != "HTF" and nsKind != "HTF" and nsBot - nbTop <= atr
poiSideBias = htfB != 0 ? htfB : dirLock != 0 ? dirLock : extDir
// a setup standing on its OWN locked anchor is not muted by geometry between
// two NEXT-zone markers it is not using
poiBlockL   = poiConflictRaw and not useAnchL and poiSideBias <= 0
poiBlockS   = poiConflictRaw and not useAnchS and poiSideBias >= 0
poiConflict = poiConflictRaw and poiSideBias == 0      // no tie-break = both muted

// FIX-F4: chasing an exhausted day costs 10 context points (floored at 0)
// FIX-T18: a NEUTRAL bias (structure vs EMA disagree) is less certainty, not
// zero context — it scores half instead of nothing.
ctxLong  = math.max(0, (htfB > 0 ? 10 : htfB == 0 ? 5 : 0) + (extDir > 0 ? 10 : 0) + sesPts + (rangeOK and pdPos < 0.5 ? 5 : 0) - (adrChaseL ? 10 : 0))
ctxShort = math.max(0, (htfB < 0 ? 10 : htfB == 0 ? 5 : 0) + (extDir < 0 ? 10 : 0) + sesPts + (rangeOK and pdPos >= 0.5 ? 5 : 0) - (adrChaseS ? 10 : 0))
// ◆ FIX-P1-1: the "strong sweep" bonus reads the RAW grade. On the EFF grade
// every MSS handed itself these 5 points for free.
liqLong  = (sslInPlay ? 10 : 0) + (recentEngLo ? 5 : 0) + (sslInPlay and lastSslQualRaw >= 3 ? 5 : 0)
liqShort = (bslInPlay ? 10 : 0) + (recentEngHi ? 5 : 0) + (bslInPlay and lastBslQualRaw >= 3 ? 5 : 0)
poiLong  = poiBlockL ? 0 : (inDemand ? 10 : nearDemand ? 5 : 0) + ((inDemand or nearDemand) and dmdFresh ? 5 : 0) + (inOTEbuy ? 5 : 0)     // FIX-V4 / FIX-W2 / FIX-W3
poiShort = poiBlockS ? 0 : (inSupply ? 10 : nearSupply ? 5 : 0) + ((inSupply or nearSupply) and supFresh ? 5 : 0) + (inOTEsell ? 5 : 0)
cisdSepUp = recentCisdUp and (na(lastChUpBar) or lastCisdUpBar != lastChUpBar)
cisdSepDn = recentCisdDn and (na(lastChDnBar) or lastCisdDnBar != lastChDnBar)
// ◆ FIX-F8 RECENCY DECAY. A shift used to be worth its full points for 10 bars
// and zero on the 11th — a cliff. A 9-bar-old MSS is not as good as a fresh
// one. Points now fade linearly to 1/(TRIG_LOOK+1) of full at the edge of the
// window. Tiers stay boolean (a shift either happened or it did not); only the
// score, which is a measure of ALIGNMENT, ages.
f_decay(int evBar, float pts) =>
    not na(evBar) and bar_index - evBar <= TRIG_LOOK ? pts * (1.0 - (bar_index - evBar) / (TRIG_LOOK + 1.0)) : 0.0
trigLong  = math.min(20, math.round(math.max(f_decay(lastMssUpBar, 12), f_decay(lastChUpBar, 9), f_decay(lastBosUpBar, 5)) + (cisdSepUp ? f_decay(lastCisdUpBar, 8) : 0)))
trigShort = math.min(20, math.round(math.max(f_decay(lastMssDnBar, 12), f_decay(lastChDnBar, 9), f_decay(lastBosDnBar, 5)) + (cisdSepDn ? f_decay(lastCisdDnBar, 8) : 0)))
// FIX-W2: the retest is of the setup's OWN anchor once it has one
retestLong  = not na(dmdTop) and low <= dmdTop and close >= dmdBot
retestShort = not na(supBot) and high >= supBot and close <= supTop
execLong  = (retestLong ? 5 : 0) + (zoneBullConf ? 5 : 0)
execShort = (retestShort ? 5 : 0) + (zoneBearConf ? 5 : 0)
// ◆ FIX-X8 THE BASE ALIGNMENT IS KEPT SEPARATELY.
// The SB context component (up to 15) is added to the same `scoreLong` that
// `minScore` is then tested against, so an SB bar cleared a 50-point gate on
// 35 points of actual alignment while a non-SB bar needed the full 50. Two
// populations, one threshold. The displayed score is unchanged; the GATE reads
// the base (see MODULE 18).
scoreBaseL = ctxLong  + liqLong  + poiLong  + trigLong  + execLong
scoreBaseS = ctxShort + liqShort + poiShort + trigShort + execShort
scoreLong  = scoreBaseL
scoreShort = scoreBaseS

// ---- ◆ SB CONTEXT component (0–15, SB-7) ---------------------------------------
//   window weight     NY AM 6 · NY PM 5 · London 4   (NY AM is the priority slot)
//   preferred pool    +3  (prior session / EQH-EQL / PDH-PDL, not a bare pivot)
//   sweep quality     +3 institutional · +2 strong
//   shift grade       +2 MSS · +1 CHoCH
//   window gate met   +1  (quality ≥ this window's own minimum)
// Zero outside an SB context window, and the total is clamped at 100, so a
// non-SB bar scores exactly what it scored in v3.
sbPoolOkL = not sbPoolPref or lastSslKind >= 2
sbPoolOkS = not sbPoolPref or lastBslKind >= 2
// ◆ FIX-P1-1: the sweep-quality component reads RAW (the MSS already scores
// separately in the shift-grade component right after it — on EFF the same
// event was paid twice). The window-gate point still reads EFF, because that
// is the grade the arming path actually uses.
sbCtxLong  = sbCtx ? sbWinPts + (sslInPlay and lastSslKind >= 2 ? 3 : 0) + (lastSslQualRaw >= 4 ? 3 : lastSslQualRaw >= 3 ? 2 : 0) + (recentMssUp ? 2 : recentChUp ? 1 : 0) + (sslInPlay and lastSslQual >= sbMinQual and sbPoolOkL ? 1 : 0) : 0
sbCtxShort = sbCtx ? sbWinPts + (bslInPlay and lastBslKind >= 2 ? 3 : 0) + (lastBslQualRaw >= 4 ? 3 : lastBslQualRaw >= 3 ? 2 : 0) + (recentMssDn ? 2 : recentChDn ? 1 : 0) + (bslInPlay and lastBslQual >= sbMinQual and sbPoolOkS ? 1 : 0) : 0
scoreLong  := math.min(100, scoreBaseL + sbCtxLong)
scoreShort := math.min(100, scoreBaseS + sbCtxShort)

// ---- tier grading (ARCH-2) ------------------------------------------------------
// C = bare trigger · B = trigger + (liquidity OR poi) · A = liquidity + POI +
// structure trigger + execution · A+ = A + HTF + session + strong sweep + P/D.
f_tierLong() =>
    bool hasLiq  = liqLong >= 10
    bool hasPoi  = poiLong >= 5
    bool hasTrig = recentMssUp or recentChUp or recentCisdUp
    bool hasExec = execLong >= 5
    int tier = 1
    if hasTrig and (hasLiq or hasPoi)
        tier := 2
    if hasLiq and poiLong >= 10 and hasTrig and hasExec
        tier := 3
    // ◆ FIX-P1-1 RAW grade · FIX-P1-2 the SB-locked range when the setup is
    // SB-tagged, so the tier test and the entry rule can no longer contradict
    // each other · FIX-P1-8(a) session exemption off intraday
    // FIX-T11: a leg under 2 ATR wide has no meaningful premium / discount, so
    // the test is waived there instead of being decided by noise.
    bool pdOkL = sbTagL != 0 ? (sbPdOK and sbPdPos < 0.5) : (rangeOK and (not rangeHolds or pdPos < 0.5))
    if tier == 3 and htfB > 0 and sesTierOK and lastSslQualRaw >= 3 and pdOkL
        tier := 4
    tier
f_tierShort() =>
    bool hasLiq  = liqShort >= 10
    bool hasPoi  = poiShort >= 5
    bool hasTrig = recentMssDn or recentChDn or recentCisdDn
    bool hasExec = execShort >= 5
    int tier = 1
    if hasTrig and (hasLiq or hasPoi)
        tier := 2
    if hasLiq and poiShort >= 10 and hasTrig and hasExec
        tier := 3
    bool pdOkS = sbTagS != 0 ? (sbPdOK and sbPdPos >= 0.5) : (rangeOK and (not rangeHolds or pdPos >= 0.5))
    if tier == 3 and htfB < 0 and sesTierOK and lastBslQualRaw >= 3 and pdOkS
        tier := 4
    tier
tierLong  = f_tierLong()
tierShort = f_tierShort()
minRank = tierMode == "A+ only" ? 4 : tierMode == "A and above" ? 3 : tierMode == "B and above" ? 2 : 1
f_tierTxt(int t) => t == 4 ? "A+" : t == 3 ? "A" : t == 2 ? "B" : "C"


// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 16B
// ◬ LIQUIDITY TRAP ENGINE — 7 breakout-trap patterns as an EXTRA layer
// Fully additive: engine OFF ⇒ every other module behaves exactly as before.
// REUSES the existing engines instead of duplicating them: sweep memory +
// quality (M5), MSS/CHoCH/BOS + displacement memory (M6), HTF bias (M7),
// regime (M11), FVG registry (M12), zone/OB/supply-demand engine (M13),
// candle anatomy + CISD/IFC confirmations (M3). Closed-bar detection only.
//   LT-1 Compression→Expansion breakout — weak sellers compress under a key
//        level, strong displacement breaks it → GENUINE-breakout candidate.
//        NO instant buy: wait retest + bullish confirmation → LONG.
//   LT-2 Expansion+Compression trap — strong sellers displaced away from the
//        level, weak buyers crawl back and poke above it → wait for bearish
//        confirmation → SHORT.
//   LT-3 Lower-wick liquidity trap — long-lower-wick cluster builds a
//        sell-side pool below; an upside breakout while that pool survives is
//        suspect. Wait for bearish confirmation → SHORT. Wick structure alone
//        NEVER signals — it only arms the watcher.
//   LT-4 Shadow-high liquidity — after a strong breakout, upper wicks cluster
//        under the prior high (buy-side pool above). Long upper wicks are NOT
//        read as bearish: wait support retest + bullish confirmation → LONG.
//   LT-5 Weak breakout trap — breakout candles carry long upper wicks and
//        weak bodies (no displacement) → weak buyers → wait bearish
//        confirmation → SHORT.
//   LT-6 Structural breakout trap — strong prior bear leg + weak compressed
//        crawl back + weak poke above the level. HARD rule: price must CLOSE
//        back below the level before bearish confirmation can fire → SHORT.
//   LT-7 Supply rejection breakout — breakout runs straight into an existing
//        M13 SUPPLY zone above the level and gets rejected there → SHORT.
// Genuine-vs-trap split (spec §24): displacement-grade break = LT-1 genuine
// candidate; wick-heavy / weak-body / pool-contradicted break = trap watch.
// State per direction: IDLE → WAITING CONFIRMATION → CONFIRMED → ACTIVE
// (cooldown), with expiry + explicit invalidation reasons. Patterns arming
// the same direction merge into ONE label — no label spam.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
grpLT = "◬ Liquidity Trap Engine"
ltEnable = input.bool(true,  "Enable Liquidity Trap Engine", group = grpLT)
// ◆ FIX-P1-5 THE TRAP ENGINE IS NOT A SECOND, UNFILTERED SIGNAL SYSTEM.
// In v3.1.1 `ltLongSig` / `ltShortSig` never entered `anyLong` / `anyShort`, so
// a trap signal bypassed EVERY filter the rest of the script applies: tier,
// minScore, the global cooldown, killzones, the volatility windows, the HTF
// bias filter, chop suppression and the SB conflict hierarchy. The chart
// carried two kinds of mark — one strictly graded, one nearly raw — and the
// raw one was the noisy one. The context gate below applies the filters that
// are available at this point in the script, and the class floor keeps only
// signals the engine itself rates HIGH or better.
ltCtxGate = input.bool(true, "Apply session / volatility / regime / HTF gates to trap signals", group = grpLT,
     tooltip = "ON: a trap must clear the same killzone, volatility, chop and bias conditions as every other engine.")
ltMinCls  = input.string("HIGH and above", "Minimum trap confidence to signal",
     options = ["MEDIUM and above", "HIGH and above", "VERY HIGH only"], group = grpLT)
ltVeto   = input.bool(true,  "Trap veto — mute opposing engine signals while a trap waits", group = grpLT,
     tooltip = "An armed trap mutes opposing ENGINE candidates until it resolves. Machine / SB entries are never vetoed.")
ltP1 = input.bool(true, "LT-1 Compression → Expansion breakout (long)", group = grpLT)
ltP2 = input.bool(true, "LT-2 Expansion + Compression trap (short)",    group = grpLT)
ltP3 = input.bool(true, "LT-3 Lower-wick liquidity trap (short)",       group = grpLT)
ltP4 = input.bool(true, "LT-4 Shadow-high liquidity (long)",            group = grpLT)
ltP5 = input.bool(true, "LT-5 Weak breakout trap (short)",              group = grpLT)
ltP6 = input.bool(true, "LT-6 Structural breakout trap (short)",        group = grpLT)
ltP7 = input.bool(true, "LT-7 Supply rejection breakout (short)",       group = grpLT)

grpLTs = "◬ LT — sensitivity"
ltWickAtr    = input.float(0.4, "Wick sensitivity (ATR ×)",               step = 0.1,  minval = 0.1,  group = grpLTs)
ltWickRatio  = input.float(1.5, "Significant wick ≥ body ×",              step = 0.25, minval = 1,    group = grpLTs)
ltWickMin    = input.int(3,     "Wick cluster — min candles",             minval = 2, maxval = 6,     group = grpLTs)
ltWickWin    = input.int(8,     "Wick cluster — window (bars)",           minval = 4, maxval = 12,    group = grpLTs)
ltClusterTol = input.float(0.6, "Wick cluster — level tolerance (ATR ×)", step = 0.1,  minval = 0.1,  group = grpLTs)
ltCmpLen     = input.int(5,     "Compression window (bars)",              minval = 3, maxval = 10,    group = grpLTs)
ltCmpSens    = input.float(0.5, "Compression — avg body ≤ ATR ×",         step = 0.1,  minval = 0.1,  group = grpLTs)
ltCmpRngAtr  = input.float(2.5, "Compression — window range ≤ ATR ×",     step = 0.5,  minval = 1,    group = grpLTs)
ltExpSens    = input.float(1.2, "Expansion body ≥ ATR × (genuine break)", step = 0.1,  minval = 0.5,  group = grpLTs)
ltBrkStr     = input.float(0.8, "Weak breakout body < ATR ×",             step = 0.1,  minval = 0.2,  group = grpLTs)
ltNearAtr    = input.float(2.0, "Compression near level (ATR ×)",         step = 0.5,  minval = 0.5,  group = grpLTs)
ltRetestAtr  = input.float(0.5, "Retest distance from level (ATR ×)",     step = 0.1,  minval = 0.1,  group = grpLTs)
ltDispLook   = input.int(20,    "Seller-displacement memory (bars)",      minval = 5,  group = grpLTs)
ltPoolLook   = input.int(25,    "Liquidity pool stays valid (bars)",      minval = 5,  group = grpLTs)
ltWaitBars   = input.int(25,    "Setup expiry — bars to confirm",         minval = 5,  group = grpLTs)
ltInvAtr     = input.float(1.0, "Invalidation beyond level (ATR ×)",      step = 0.25, minval = 0.25, group = grpLTs)
ltCd         = input.int(10,    "Cooldown after a trap signal (bars)",    minval = 0,  group = grpLTs)

grpLTc = "◬ LT — confirmation"
// TOKEN: the Intrabar (repainting) confirmation mode was removed with its
// preview alerts — every LT state and signal commits on the CLOSED bar.
ltReqRetest = input.bool(true,  "Require retest (long: level retap · short: close back below)", group = grpLTc)
ltReqMss    = input.bool(false, "Require recent MSS",              group = grpLTc)
ltReqCh     = input.bool(false, "Require recent CHoCH",            group = grpLTc)
ltReqSweep  = input.bool(false, "Require sweep in play",           group = grpLTc)
ltReqFvg    = input.bool(false, "Require live FVG (signal side)",  group = grpLTc)
ltReqSD     = input.bool(false, "Require S/D zone interaction",    group = grpLTc)
ltReqOB     = input.bool(false, "Require order-block interaction", group = grpLTc)
ltReqMtf    = input.bool(false, "Require HTF bias agreement",      group = grpLTc)

grpLTv = "◬ LT — score / display"
ltScoreMed   = input.int(50, "MEDIUM score ≥",    minval = 1, maxval = 100, group = grpLTv)
ltScoreHigh  = input.int(70, "HIGH score ≥",      minval = 1, maxval = 100, group = grpLTv)
ltScoreVHigh = input.int(85, "VERY HIGH score ≥", minval = 1, maxval = 100, group = grpLTv)
ltShowSig    = input.bool(true, "Show trap signal labels",           group = grpLTv)
ltShowSetup  = input.bool(false, "Show setup (armed) chips",          group = grpLTv,
     tooltip = "OFF by default: the armed chips are a waiting state, not a signal. Confirmed TRAP labels still print.")
ltShowMarks  = input.bool(true, "Show retest / failed-break marks",  group = grpLTv)
ltShowScore  = input.bool(true, "Show confidence score in label",    group = grpLTv)
ltDynAlert   = input.bool(true, "Dynamic alert() messages (pattern + score + level)", group = grpLTv)

// ---- LT helpers ------------------------------------------------------------------
f_addPat(string acc, bool c, string nm) => c ? (acc == "" ? nm : acc + "+" + nm) : acc
f_ltCls(int s) => s >= ltScoreVHigh ? "VERY HIGH" : s >= ltScoreHigh ? "HIGH" : s >= ltScoreMed ? "MEDIUM" : "LOW"
f_ltStTxt(int s) => s == 0 ? "idle" : s == 1 ? "WAITING" : "ACTIVE"

ltAtrAvg = ta.sma(atr, 50)
ltLowVol = ltAtrAvg > 0 and atr < 0.7 * ltAtrAvg

// ◆ FIX-P1-5 the context gate + class floor the two trap machines must clear.
// Only conditions that already exist at MODULE 16B are used (the machine-tier
// and SB-conflict gates live further down and cannot be read from here); the
// trap engine keeps its own `ltCd` cooldown.
ltClsFloor  = ltMinCls == "VERY HIGH only" ? ltScoreVHigh : ltMinCls == "MEDIUM and above" ? ltScoreMed : ltScoreHigh
ltCtxBase   = not ltCtxGate or (kzOK and newsOK and (not useRegime or regime != "CHOP"))
// FIX-V1 the shared bias permission · FIX-V5 (F-8) narrative gate: a trap against
// a TRENDING external structure needs a RAW strong raid, or it is inducement
ltCtxOKLong  = ltCtxBase and (not ltCtxGate or biasOkL) and not (regime == "TRENDING" and extDir == -1 and lastSslQualRaw < 3)
ltCtxOKShort = ltCtxBase and (not ltCtxGate or biasOkS) and not (regime == "TRENDING" and extDir == 1 and lastBslQualRaw < 3)

// significant-wick candles — context is judged later, NEVER a raw signal (§11)
ltLoWickBar = rng > 0 and dnWick >= ltWickAtr * atr and dnWick >= ltWickRatio * body and dnWick > upWick
ltHiWickBar = rng > 0 and upWick >= ltWickAtr * atr and upWick >= ltWickRatio * body and upWick > dnWick

// compression: small average body + tight window + mixed colors + no displacement
ltCmpBodyAvg = ta.sma(body, ltCmpLen)
ltCmpWinRng  = ta.highest(high, ltCmpLen) - ta.lowest(low, ltCmpLen)
ltCmpBullCnt = math.sum(bullBar ? 1 : 0, ltCmpLen)
ltCmpMaxBody = ta.highest(body, ltCmpLen)
ltCmp = ltCmpBodyAvg <= ltCmpSens * atr and ltCmpWinRng <= ltCmpRngAtr * atr and ltCmpBullCnt >= 1 and ltCmpBullCnt <= ltCmpLen - 1 and ltCmpMaxBody < dispFactor * atr

// ---- lower-wick cluster → sell-side pool BELOW price (LT-3) ----------------------
f_wickClusterLow(bool en) =>
    int   cnt = 0
    float mn  = na
    float mx  = na
    if en
        for i = 1 to ltWickWin
            if ltLoWickBar[i]
                cnt += 1
                mn := na(mn) ? low[i] : math.min(mn, low[i])
                mx := na(mx) ? low[i] : math.max(mx, low[i])
    bool ok = cnt >= ltWickMin and not na(mn) and mx - mn <= ltClusterTol * atr
    [ok, mn]

// CE10295 refactor: the wick-cluster pools, the breakout memory and the LT-4
// shadow-high watcher keep their state inside one function and hand back the
// eight values the arm expressions, the visuals and the score need.
f_ltPools() =>
    [_p3ClusterOk, _p3ClusterLow] = f_wickClusterLow(ltEnable and ltP3)

    var float _p3PoolLow = na
    var int   _p3PoolBar = na
    var float _p3Swept   = na    // last consumed pool level — blocks instant re-arming
    if ltEnable and ltP3 and confirmed
        if not na(_p3PoolLow) and low < _p3PoolLow
            _p3Swept   := _p3PoolLow
            _p3PoolLow := na      // pool consumed
        if not na(_p3PoolLow) and bar_index - _p3PoolBar > ltPoolLook
            _p3PoolLow := na      // pool stale
        bool _p3FreshOk = na(_p3Swept) or math.abs(_p3ClusterLow - _p3Swept) > ltClusterTol * atr
        if _p3ClusterOk and low > _p3ClusterLow and _p3FreshOk
            _p3PoolLow := _p3ClusterLow
            _p3PoolBar := bar_index

    // ---- breakout memory (M5 once-per-level latch reused) ----------------------------
    var int   _ltBrkUpBar = na
    var float _ltBrkUpLvl = na
    if ltEnable and confirmed and brokeUp
        _ltBrkUpBar := bar_index
        _ltBrkUpLvl := lastRes

    // ---- LT-4 shadow-high watcher: strong breakout → upper-wick cluster --------------
    var int   _p4Stage   = 0
    var float _p4Lvl     = na
    var int   _p4Bar     = na
    var int   _p4WickCnt = 0
    var float _p4HiMax   = na
    var float _p4HiMin   = na
    var float _p4PoolHi  = na
    var int   _p4PoolBar = na
    bool _p4Arm = false
    if ltEnable and ltP4 and confirmed
        if not na(_p4PoolHi) and (high > _p4PoolHi or bar_index - _p4PoolBar > ltPoolLook)
            _p4PoolHi := na       // buy-side pool taken or stale
        if _p4Stage == 1
            if close < _p4Lvl or bar_index - _p4Bar > ltPoolLook
                _p4Stage := 0
            else
                if ltHiWickBar and high <= high[1]
                    _p4WickCnt += 1
                    _p4HiMax := na(_p4HiMax) ? high : math.max(_p4HiMax, high)
                    _p4HiMin := na(_p4HiMin) ? high : math.min(_p4HiMin, high)
                if _p4WickCnt >= ltWickMin and not na(_p4HiMax) and _p4HiMax - _p4HiMin <= ltClusterTol * atr
                    _p4PoolHi  := _p4HiMax
                    _p4PoolBar := bar_index
                    _p4Stage   := 0
                    _p4Arm     := true
        if _p4Stage == 0 and not _p4Arm and brokeUp and bullBar and body >= dispFactor * atr
            _p4Stage   := 1
            _p4Lvl     := lastRes
            _p4Bar     := bar_index
            _p4WickCnt := 0
            _p4HiMax   := na
            _p4HiMin   := na
    [_p3PoolLow, _p3PoolBar, _p4PoolHi, _p4PoolBar, _p4Lvl, _ltBrkUpLvl, _ltBrkUpBar, _p4Arm]
[p3PoolLow, p3PoolBar, p4PoolHi, p4PoolBar, p4Lvl, ltBrkUpLvl, ltBrkUpBar, p4Arm] = f_ltPools()

// ---- pattern setup events (edge-triggered, closed bars, once-per-level latch) ----
var int p1PrimeBar = na
if ltEnable and ltP1 and confirmed and ltCmp and not na(lastRes) and close < lastRes and lastRes - close <= ltNearAtr * atr
    p1PrimeBar := bar_index

ltWeakPoke   = brokeUp and body <= ltBrkStr * atr
ltWeakWickUp = ltHiWickBar and body <= ltBrkStr * atr

p1Arm = ltEnable and ltP1 and confirmed and brokeUp and bullBar and body >= ltExpSens * atr and f_recent(p1PrimeBar, ltCmpLen * 2)
p2Arm = ltEnable and ltP2 and confirmed and ltWeakPoke and f_recent(lastDispDnBar, ltDispLook) and (ltCmp or ltCmp[1]) and close > close[ltCmpLen]
p3Arm = ltEnable and ltP3 and confirmed and brokeUp and not na(p3PoolLow) and close - p3PoolLow <= SWEEP_CTX_ATR * atr
p5Arm = ltEnable and ltP5 and confirmed and ((brokeUp and ltHiWickBar) or (f_recent(ltBrkUpBar, 3) and not na(ltBrkUpLvl) and close > ltBrkUpLvl and ltWeakWickUp and ltWeakWickUp[1]))
p6Arm = ltEnable and ltP6 and confirmed and ltWeakPoke and not na(extSup) and (extDir == -1 or f_recent(lastDispDnBar, ltDispLook * 2)) and (na(lastDispUpBar) or bar_index - lastDispUpBar > ltCmpLen) and (ltCmp or ltCmp[1])
p7Arm = ltEnable and ltP7 and confirmed and not na(nsBot) and nsKind != "HTF" and high >= nsBot and close <= nsTop and (brokeUp or f_recent(ltBrkUpBar, 3)) and not na(ltBrkUpLvl) and nsBot >= ltBrkUpLvl

// ---- order-block interaction (M13 kind "OB" reused — score / gate only) ----------
// CE10295 refactor: the OB-interaction scan runs in a function.
f_ltObNear() =>
    bool _obNearL = false
    bool _obNearS = false
    if ltEnable
        for z in zones                 // SCOPE: `for in`, ternary flags
            if z.kind == "OB" and not z.signaled
                float zt = box.get_top(z.bx)
                float zb = box.get_bottom(z.bx)
                _obNearL := not z.isSupply and low <= zt and close >= zb ? true : _obNearL
                _obNearS := z.isSupply and high >= zb and close <= zt ? true : _obNearS
    [_obNearL, _obNearS]
[obNearL, obNearS] = f_ltObNear()

// ---- trap state machines (per direction) ------------------------------------------
// CE10295 refactor: f_ltScore is lifted to global scope (Pine has no nested
// function definitions) and now takes the two machine states as parameters.
// confidence score (§12) — clamped 0–100, thresholds configurable (§13)
f_ltScore(bool isLong, bool retOk, int lState, int sState) =>
    int s = 10                                                                              // key-level interaction (anchored by construction)
    s += (isLong ? sslInPlay : bslInPlay) ? 20 : 0                                          // liquidity sweep in play
    bool strongRej = isLong ? (bullPin or (rng > 0 and dnWick >= 2 * body and dnWick >= minWickAtr * atr)) : (bearPin or (rng > 0 and upWick >= 2 * body and upWick >= minWickAtr * atr))
    s += strongRej ? 15 : 0                                                                 // strong rejection candle
    s += (isLong ? (recentMssUp or recentChUp) : (recentMssDn or recentChDn)) ? 20 : 0      // MSS / CHoCH
    s += (isLong ? (inDemand or nearDemand) : (inSupply or nearSupply)) ? 10 : 0            // S/D confirmation
    s += (isLong ? not na(cFvgBTop) : not na(cFvgSTop)) ? 10 : 0                            // live FVG on signal side
    s += (isLong ? obNearL : obNearS) ? 10 : 0                                              // order-block interaction
    s += (isLong ? recentDispUp : recentDispDn) ? 10 : 0                                    // fresh displacement
    s += retOk ? 10 : 0                                                                     // retest / failed-break done
    s += (isLong ? htfB > 0 : htfB < 0) ? 15 : 0                                            // MTF agreement
    s -= regime == "CHOP" ? 10 : 0                                                          // weak / unclear structure
    s -= (isLong ? extDir == -1 : extDir == 1) ? 20 : 0                                     // counter-trend (FIX-V5: was 10)
    s -= (isLong ? inOTEsell : inOTEbuy) ? 15 : 0                                           // FIX-V5: fighting the OTE of the other side
    s -= ltLowVol ? 5 : 0                                                                   // low volatility
    s -= not (isLong ? (sslInPlay or recentEngLo) : (bslInPlay or recentEngHi)) ? 10 : 0    // insufficient liquidity
    s -= lState >= 1 and sState >= 1 ? 15 : 0                                           // ambiguous — both directions armed
    math.max(0, math.min(100, s))

// CE10295 refactor: the two trap state machines, their labels and their alert()
// feed all live in one function; the eleven values below are what MODULE 18,
// the debug table and MODULE 23 consume.
f_ltMachines() =>
    var int    _ltLState  = 0     // 0 idle · 1 waiting confirmation · 2 active/cooldown
    var int    _ltLBar    = na
    var float  _ltLLevel  = na
    var string _ltLPats   = ""
    var bool   _ltLRetOk  = false
    var string _ltLReason = ""
    var int    _ltSState  = 0
    var int    _ltSBar    = na
    var float  _ltSLevel  = na
    var string _ltSPats   = ""
    var bool   _ltSRetOk  = false
    var string _ltSReason = ""
    // FIX-V6a (F-6): the retest bar may not also be the confirmation bar
    var int    _ltLRetBar = na
    var int    _ltSRetBar = na
    // FIX-V6b (F-7): one signal per level — the cooldown alone let the same
    // supply fire LT-7 twice ten bars apart
    var float  _ltLDoneLvl = na
    var float  _ltSDoneLvl = na
    bool _ltLongSig    = false
    bool _ltShortSig   = false
    int  _ltLongScore  = 0
    int  _ltShortScore = 0
    float _ltSlL = na
    float _ltSlS = na
    bool _ltArmLEvt = false
    bool _ltArmSEvt = false
    bool _ltRetLEvt = false
    bool _ltRetSEvt = false
    string _ltLArmTxt = f_addPat(f_addPat("", p1Arm, "LT-1"), p4Arm, "LT-4")
    string _ltSArmTxt = f_addPat(f_addPat(f_addPat(f_addPat(f_addPat("", p2Arm, "LT-2"), p3Arm, "LT-3"), p5Arm, "LT-5"), p6Arm, "LT-6"), p7Arm, "LT-7")
    float  _ltBrkLvlNow = not na(ltBrkUpLvl) and f_recent(ltBrkUpBar, 5) ? ltBrkUpLvl : lastRes

    if ltEnable and confirmed
        // ---- LONG trap machine (LT-1 / LT-4) --------------------------------------
        if _ltLState == 1
            if bar_index - _ltLBar > ltWaitBars
                _ltLState  := 0
                _ltLReason := "expired (no confirmation)"
            else if close < _ltLLevel - ltInvAtr * atr and bearBar and body >= dispFactor * atr
                _ltLState  := 0
                _ltLReason := "breakout failed (bear displacement below level)"
        if _ltLState == 2 and bar_index - _ltLBar > ltCd
            _ltLState := 0
        float _ltLLvlNow = p1Arm ? _ltBrkLvlNow : p4Lvl
        // FIX-W8 (OP-8): a `na` level arms a machine that can never register its
        // retest (`close < _ltSLevel` is never true) and just burns `ltWaitBars`.
        if _ltLArmTxt != "" and not na(_ltLLvlNow) and (na(_ltLDoneLvl) or math.abs(_ltLLvlNow - _ltLDoneLvl) > ltClusterTol * atr)   // FIX-V6b / FIX-W8
            if _ltLState == 0
                _ltLState  := 1
                _ltLBar    := bar_index
                _ltLLevel  := _ltLLvlNow
                _ltLPats   := _ltLArmTxt
                _ltLRetOk  := false
                _ltLRetBar := na
                _ltArmLEvt := true
                if ltShowSetup
                    f_chip(true, "◬ " + _ltLPats + " setup", color.new(color.orange, 45), color.white, mkSize)
            else if _ltLState == 1      // SCOPE: appends as ternaries
                _ltLPats := p1Arm and not str.contains(_ltLPats, "LT-1") ? _ltLPats + "+LT-1" : _ltLPats
                _ltLPats := p4Arm and not str.contains(_ltLPats, "LT-4") ? _ltLPats + "+LT-4" : _ltLPats
        if _ltLState == 1 and bar_index > _ltLBar and not na(_ltLLevel) and low <= _ltLLevel + ltRetestAtr * atr and not _ltLRetOk
            _ltLRetOk  := true
            _ltLRetBar := bar_index
            _ltRetLEvt := true
        // FIX-V6a: a raw `sslSweep` is no longer a confirmation — it was the retest
        // bar confirming itself. Confirmation = rejection / IFC / CISD / shift, on a
        // bar AFTER the retest bar.
        bool ltBullConfNow = zoneBullConf or mssUp or chochUp or eChochUp
        bool ltGatesL = (not ltReqMss or recentMssUp) and (not ltReqCh or recentChUp or recentMssUp) and (not ltReqSweep or sslInPlay) and (not ltReqFvg or not na(cFvgBTop)) and (not ltReqSD or inDemand or nearDemand) and (not ltReqOB or obNearL) and (not ltReqMtf or biasOkL)
        if _ltLState == 1 and ltBullConfNow and ltGatesL and ltCtxOKLong and lockOkL and (not ltReqRetest or (_ltLRetOk and bar_index > _ltLRetBar)) and close > _ltLLevel - ltInvAtr * atr
            int scL = f_ltScore(true, _ltLRetOk, _ltLState, _ltSState)
            if scL >= ltClsFloor
                _ltLongSig   := true
                _ltLongScore := scL
                _ltSlL       := math.min(low, _ltLLevel) - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                _ltLDoneLvl  := _ltLLevel
                _ltLState    := 2
                _ltLBar      := bar_index
        // ---- SHORT trap machine (LT-2 / LT-3 / LT-5 / LT-6 / LT-7) ------------------
        if _ltSState == 1
            if bar_index - _ltSBar > ltWaitBars
                _ltSState  := 0
                _ltSReason := "expired (no confirmation)"
            else if close > _ltSLevel + ltInvAtr * atr and bullBar and body >= dispFactor * atr
                _ltSState  := 0
                _ltSReason := "breakout accepted (bull displacement above level)"
        if _ltSState == 2 and bar_index - _ltSBar > ltCd
            _ltSState := 0
        if _ltSArmTxt != "" and not na(_ltBrkLvlNow) and (na(_ltSDoneLvl) or math.abs(_ltBrkLvlNow - _ltSDoneLvl) > ltClusterTol * atr)   // FIX-V6b / FIX-W8
            if _ltSState == 0
                _ltSState  := 1
                _ltSBar    := bar_index
                _ltSLevel  := _ltBrkLvlNow
                _ltSPats   := _ltSArmTxt
                _ltSRetOk  := p7Arm      // LT-7's supply tap IS its retest
                _ltSRetBar := p7Arm ? bar_index : na
                _ltArmSEvt := true
                if ltShowSetup
                    f_chip(false, "◬ " + _ltSPats + " trap?", color.new(color.orange, 45), color.white, mkSize)
            else if _ltSState == 1      // SCOPE: appends as ternaries
                _ltSPats  := p2Arm and not str.contains(_ltSPats, "LT-2") ? _ltSPats + "+LT-2" : _ltSPats
                _ltSPats  := p3Arm and not str.contains(_ltSPats, "LT-3") ? _ltSPats + "+LT-3" : _ltSPats
                _ltSPats  := p5Arm and not str.contains(_ltSPats, "LT-5") ? _ltSPats + "+LT-5" : _ltSPats
                _ltSPats  := p6Arm and not str.contains(_ltSPats, "LT-6") ? _ltSPats + "+LT-6" : _ltSPats
                _ltSPats  := p7Arm and not str.contains(_ltSPats, "LT-7") ? _ltSPats + "+LT-7" : _ltSPats
                _ltSRetOk  := p7Arm ? true : _ltSRetOk
                _ltSRetBar := p7Arm and na(_ltSRetBar) ? bar_index : _ltSRetBar
        if _ltSState == 1 and bar_index > _ltSBar and not na(_ltSLevel) and close < _ltSLevel and not _ltSRetOk
            _ltSRetOk  := true
            _ltSRetBar := bar_index
            _ltRetSEvt := true
        bool ltBearConfNow = zoneBearConf or mssDn or chochDn or eChochDn           // FIX-V6a (no raw bslSweep)
        bool ltGatesS = (not ltReqMss or recentMssDn) and (not ltReqCh or recentChDn or recentMssDn) and (not ltReqSweep or bslInPlay) and (not ltReqFvg or not na(cFvgSTop)) and (not ltReqSD or inSupply or nearSupply) and (not ltReqOB or obNearS) and (not ltReqMtf or biasOkS)
        bool ltSNeedBelow = str.contains(_ltSPats, "LT-6")    // LT-6 spec: close back below is mandatory
        if _ltSState == 1 and ltBearConfNow and ltGatesS and ltCtxOKShort and lockOkS and (not ltReqRetest or (_ltSRetOk and bar_index > _ltSRetBar)) and (not ltSNeedBelow or close < _ltSLevel)
            int scS = f_ltScore(false, _ltSRetOk, _ltLState, _ltSState)
            if scS >= ltClsFloor
                _ltShortSig   := true
                _ltShortScore := scS
                _ltSlS        := math.max(high, _ltSLevel) + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                _ltSDoneLvl   := _ltSLevel
                _ltSState     := 2
                _ltSBar       := bar_index
    // FIX-V6 (F-5): the trap label and the alert() feed moved to MODULE 18 / 20 —
    // a trap signal is now an ENGINE CANDIDATE (tier · score · cooldown · direction
    // lock · viability gate · tracking), not a second unfiltered signal system.
    // the debug rows are built here, where the machine state lives
    string _ltDbgMach = "L " + f_ltStTxt(_ltLState) + (_ltLState >= 1 and _ltLPats != "" ? " [" + _ltLPats + "]" : "") + " · S " + f_ltStTxt(_ltSState) + (_ltSState >= 1 and _ltSPats != "" ? " [" + _ltSPats + "]" : "")
    string _ltDbgLast = "L: " + (_ltLReason != "" ? _ltLReason : "—") + " · S: " + (_ltSReason != "" ? _ltSReason : "—")
    [_ltLongSig, _ltShortSig, _ltLongScore, _ltShortScore, _ltLState, _ltSState, _ltDbgMach, _ltDbgLast, _ltArmLEvt or _ltArmSEvt, _ltRetLEvt, _ltRetSEvt,
     _ltSlL, _ltSlS, _ltLPats, _ltSPats, _ltLLevel, _ltSLevel, _ltLRetOk, _ltSRetOk]
[ltLongSig, ltShortSig, ltLongScore, ltShortScore, ltLState, ltSState, ltDbgMach, ltDbgLast, ltArmEvt, ltRetLEvt, ltRetSEvt,
 ltSlL, ltSlS, ltLPats, ltSPats, ltLLevel, ltSLevel, ltLRetOk, ltSRetOk] = f_ltMachines()
slLTLong  := ltSlL
slLTShort := ltSlS
sigLTLong  = ltLongSig      // graded in MODULE 18 like every other engine candidate
sigLTShort = ltShortSig

plotchar(ltEnable and ltShowMarks and ltRetLEvt, "LT retest (long)",        "◦", location.belowbar, color.new(COL_BULL, 20), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(ltEnable and ltShowMarks and ltRetSEvt, "LT failed break (short)", "◦", location.abovebar, color.new(COL_BEAR, 20), size = size.tiny)

// TOKEN (v3.4): the two wick-cluster pool LINES were removed for the compiled-
// token budget (CE10117). The pools themselves are untouched — `p3PoolLow` and
// `p4PoolHi` still arm LT-3 and LT-4 and still feed the trap score.

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 17
// MASTER SETUP STATE MACHINE (ARCH-1) — the primary signal path
//   0 IDLE → 1 SWEPT (quality ≥ minArmQual) → 2 AT POI → 3 SHIFTED
//   (MSS/CHoCH/CISD, retest anchor locked) → ENTRY → 4 ACTIVE → 0.
// Edge-triggered, stage expiry, explicit invalidation with a reason string.
// CHOP suppression with the institutional-sweep exception (ARCH-5).
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// (suL / suS state is declared in MODULE 15A — FIX-V2 / FIX-W2 read it there)
sigMCLong  = false
sigMCShort = false
// ◆ FIX-P0-4: the machine records the PRICE ITS PLAN IS BUILT ON — the CE of
// the locked FVG for a CE fill, the touched anchor edge otherwise. MODULE 19
// uses this instead of the bar close, so RR, the TP ladder, the break-even
// trigger and every counter are measured from where the order actually sits.
float mcPlanLong  = na
float mcPlanShort = na

// ---- ◆ SB OVERLAY STATE (SB-4) ------------------------------------------------
// A dedicated Silver Bullet ARMING PATH that can only fire inside the three
// windows: it stamps a WINDOW TAG onto the setup it arms. The tag then drives
// the shortened stage clocks, the mandatory locked-FVG anchor, the CE /
// proximal entry rule, the window-close kill and the MODULE 19 exit clock.
// A setup armed outside a window carries tag 0 and behaves exactly as in v3.
// (sbTagL / sbTagS are declared in MODULE 10 — see FIX-P1-2)
var int  sbShiftGL = 0      // grade of the shift that opened stage 3: 2 MSS · 1 CHoCH/CISD
var int  sbShiftGS = 0
bool sbSigLong  = false     // this bar's entry came from the SB path
bool sbSigShort = false
bool sbCeFillL  = false     // filled at the CE (vs the proximal edge)
bool sbCeFillS  = false
// ◆ FIX-X3: the ORDINARY path filled its limit at the CE of the locked FVG
bool mcCeFillL  = false
bool mcCeFillS  = false
mcCeOn = mcEntryMode != "Proximal edge + confirmation (v3.4)"

// one SB arm per sweep: the bar of the sweep the SB path last armed from, so a
// grace re-arm cannot churn on the same pool after an invalidation (SB-9)
var int sbArmedSslBar = na
var int sbArmedBslBar = na
// ◆ FIX-T3: the same once-per-sweep memory for the ORDINARY path
var int armedSslBar = na
var int armedBslBar = na

// ◆ FIX-V10 (F-13 companion): one SB entry per window per direction
var int sbDoneWinL = 0
var int sbDoneWinS = 0
sbDoneWinL := sbCtxWin == 0 ? 0 : sbDoneWinL
sbDoneWinS := sbCtxWin == 0 ? 0 : sbDoneWinS

// arming gate: SB window + that window's own min sweep quality + preferred pool
sbArmOkL = sbCtx and lastSslQual >= sbMinQual and sbPoolOkL and sbCtxWin != sbDoneWinL   // FIX-V10
sbArmOkS = sbCtx and lastBslQual >= sbMinQual and sbPoolOkS and sbCtxWin != sbDoneWinS

// ◆ SB-9 relaxed same-bar arming — SB PATH ONLY. The non-SB path still needs
// the sweep stamped on THIS bar (`lastSslBar == bar_index`). A setup that
// clears the full SB gate (window + window quality + preferred pool) may also
// arm while the sweep is still `inPlay`, up to SB_ARM_GRACE bars after it, and
// only once per sweep. Every other gate — quality, preferred pool, chop bypass
// — is untouched below.
sbGraceL  = sbArmOkL and sslInPlay and f_recent(lastSslBar, SB_ARM_GRACE) and nz(sbArmedSslBar, -1) != lastSslBar
sbGraceS  = sbArmOkS and bslInPlay and f_recent(lastBslBar, SB_ARM_GRACE) and nz(sbArmedBslBar, -1) != lastBslBar
// ◆ FIX-T3 NON-SB ARM GRACE. The sweep must still be in play (its reclaim
// holds) and each sweep may arm one setup only, so this cannot churn.
stdGraceL = armGrace > 0 and sslInPlay and f_recent(lastSslBar, armGrace) and nz(armedSslBar, -1) != lastSslBar
stdGraceS = armGrace > 0 and bslInPlay and f_recent(lastBslBar, armGrace) and nz(armedBslBar, -1) != lastBslBar
sbArmBarL = lastSslBar == bar_index or sbGraceL or stdGraceL
sbArmBarS = lastBslBar == bar_index or sbGraceS or stdGraceS

// ◆ FIX-T4 WHAT COUNTS AS THE STRUCTURAL SHIFT. A CISD is a change in the way
// price is being delivered inside a leg — it breaks no swing, so on its own it
// is not a shift. It remains an execution confirmation (`zoneBullConf`).
shiftUpOk = shiftSrc == "MSS only"             ? mssUp :
     shiftSrc == "MSS / external CHoCH" ? (mssUp or eChochUp) :
     shiftSrc == "+ internal CHoCH"     ? (mssUp or eChochUp or chochUp) :
     (mssUp or eChochUp or chochUp or cisdUp)
shiftDnOk = shiftSrc == "MSS only"             ? mssDn :
     shiftSrc == "MSS / external CHoCH" ? (mssDn or eChochDn) :
     shiftSrc == "+ internal CHoCH"     ? (mssDn or eChochDn or chochDn) :
     (mssDn or eChochDn or chochDn or cisdDn)

// ◆ FIX-V9: the SB shift (LTF model) — the swing model keeps `shiftUpOk`
sbDispNow = body >= sbDispAtr * atr
sbShiftUp = sbShiftSrc == "Same as machine (v3.2.2)" ? shiftUpOk : (mssUp or eChochUp or ((chochUp or cisdUp) and bullBar and sbDispNow))
sbShiftDn = sbShiftSrc == "Same as machine (v3.2.2)" ? shiftDnOk : (mssDn or eChochDn or ((chochDn or cisdDn) and bearBar and sbDispNow))

// ◆ FIX-T8: a resting limit at the CE fills on the TOUCH
ceFillReal = sbFillMode == "Limit touch (realistic)"

// stage clocks — shorter for a tagged SB setup
sbLiqWL = sbTagL != 0 ? sbLiqWait : liqWait
sbPoiWL = sbTagL != 0 ? sbPoiWait : poiWait
sbRetWL = sbTagL != 0 ? sbRetWait : retWait
sbLiqWS = sbTagS != 0 ? sbLiqWait : liqWait
sbPoiWS = sbTagS != 0 ? sbPoiWait : poiWait
sbRetWS = sbTagS != 0 ? sbRetWait : retWait

// window-close kill: an SB narrative is only valid inside its OWN window
sbKillL = sbTagL != 0 and ((sbTagL == 1 and sbEndLOEvt) or (sbTagL == 2 and sbEndAMEvt) or (sbTagL == 3 and sbEndPMEvt))
sbKillS = sbTagS != 0 and ((sbTagS == 1 and sbEndLOEvt) or (sbTagS == 2 and sbEndAMEvt) or (sbTagS == 3 and sbEndPMEvt))

// a CLEAN SB FVG: cut by a displacement leg and of meaningful height
sbFvgOkL = not na(cFvgBTop) and cFvgBDisp and cFvgBTop - cFvgBBot >= sbFvgMinAtr * atr
sbFvgOkS = not na(cFvgSTop) and cFvgSDisp and cFvgSTop - cFvgSBot >= sbFvgMinAtr * atr

// LONG machine
if confirmed
    sbTagL    := suL == 0 ? 0 : sbTagL     // SCOPE: idle ⇒ no SB tag to carry
    sbShiftGL := suL == 0 ? 0 : sbShiftGL
    // invalidation checks first (any armed stage)
    if suL >= 1
        if not na(suLSweepExt) and close < suLSweepExt
            suL := 0
            suLReason := "sweep undone"
        // ◆ FIX-N7 (LOGIC-N7): arming demanded the RECLAIM (close above the
        // swept level), but the only continuation test was the wick extreme.
        // Between level and wick a setup survived with its premise gone. Before
        // the shift (stages 1–2) the reclaim must still hold, with a tolerance
        // so a POI that straddles the swept low does not kill its own tap.
        // Once structure has shifted (stage 3) the sweep has done its job and
        // only the wick check applies.
        else if suL <= 2 and not na(suLSweepLvl) and close < suLSweepLvl - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
            suL := 0
            suLReason := "sweep reclaim lost"
        // ◆ FIX-X6 A RETEST IS DELIVERED BY AN OPPOSING CANDLE.
        // Once structure has shifted, price comes BACK to the anchor — and on
        // XAU / NAS that return leg is routinely a 1.2 ATR bearish body. v3.4
        // read it as "opposing displacement" and killed the setup on the very
        // bar it was waiting for. At stage 3 the candle only invalidates if it
        // CLOSES through the anchor's distal edge; that close is already the
        // "anchor FVG violated" test below, so the premise is intact whenever
        // the close holds above it.
        else if bearBar and body >= dispFactor * atr and suL <= 3
             and not (suL == 3 and not na(suLAnchorB) and close > suLAnchorB)
            suL := 0
            suLReason := "opposing displacement"
        // ◆ FIX-V2b (F-2): a quality raid of the OTHER side while still waiting
        // for a POI means the range is being swept both ways — no narrative
        else if suL == 1 and bslSweep and lastBslQual >= 2
            suL := 0
            suLReason := "range swept both ways"
    // ◆ FIX-T1 ONLY THE SETUP'S OWN ANCHOR MAY INVALIDATE IT. `zoneInvalidatedUp`
    // is a registry-wide flag: it fires when ANY demand zone is closed through,
    // including one far below price that this setup never touched. Valid setups
    // died on unrelated housekeeping, and the reason string could not say which
    // zone it meant. A setup whose anchor is an FVG (suLFvgBar set) already has
    // its own violation test further down; an OTE/HTF tap with no anchor is
    // covered by the sweep and displacement checks above.
    if suL >= 2 and na(suLFvgBar) and not na(suLAnchorB) and close < suLAnchorB
        suL := 0
        suLReason := "POI violated (own anchor)"
    // ◆ FIX-T6 the HTF protected swing (MODULE 7) finally does something: a
    // close through it means the higher-timeframe narrative is gone.
    if htfProtInval and suL >= 1 and suL <= 3 and not na(hsProtLo) and close < hsProtLo
        suL := 0
        suLReason := "HTF protected low broken"
    if sbKillAtEnd and sbKillL and suL >= 1 and suL <= 3
        suL := 0
        suLReason := "SB window closed"
    // stage expiry — SCOPE: one branch, the stage picks the clock and the text
    bool expL = (suL == 1 and bar_index - suLBar > sbLiqWL) or (suL == 2 and bar_index - suLBar > sbPoiWL) or (suL == 3 and bar_index - suLBar > sbRetWL)
    if expL
        suLReason := suL == 1 ? "sweep expired (no POI tap)" : suL == 2 ? "POI tap expired (no shift)" : "retest window expired"
        suL := 0
    // transitions
    // ◆ SB arming path: `or sbArmOkL` lets the SB gate arm a setup that the
    // generic minArmQual would reject, and the chop block may be waived for it.
    if suL == 0 and sslInPlay and sbArmBarL and (lastSslQual >= minArmQual or sbArmOkL)
         and (not armNeedHtf or biasOkL) and lockOkL                                   // FIX-V1 · FIX-V2
        bool chopBlock = useRegime and regime == "CHOP" and not instSweepL and not (sbArmOkL and sbChopBypass)   // FIX-T17
        if not chopBlock
            suL := 1
            suLBar := bar_index
            suLSweepLvl := lastSslLvl
            suLSweepExt := lastSslExt
            suLAnchorT := na
            suLAnchorB := na
            suLFvgBar  := na
            sbTagL     := sbArmOkL ? sbCtxWin : 0
            sbShiftGL  := 0
            armedSslBar := lastSslBar           // FIX-T3: this sweep is spent
            if sbArmOkL
                sbArmedSslBar := lastSslBar     // SB-9: this sweep is spent
    // ◆ FIX-V9b (F-10): in the SB model the raid IS the POI — a displaced shift
    // while the sweep is in play goes straight to stage 3 (anchor = shift-leg FVG)
    if suL == 1 and sslInPlay and (sbTagL != 0 ? (sbReqMss ? mssUp : sbShiftUp) : (fastShift and shiftUpOk))   // FIX-V14
        suL := 3
        suLBar := bar_index
        sbShiftGL := mssUp ? 2 : 1
        suLAnchorT := na
        suLAnchorB := na
        suLFvgBar  := na
    if suL == 1
        bool tapZone = inDemand and retestLong and not poiBlockL      // FIX-V4 / FIX-W3
        bool tapOte  = poiSrc != "Zone / HTF POI only" and inOTEbuy      // FIX-T5
        bool tapHtf  = htfLive and not na(hPoiBTop) and low <= hPoiBTop and close > hPoiBBot
        if tapZone or tapOte or tapHtf
            suL := 2
            suLBar := bar_index
            suLAnchorT := tapZone ? nbTop : suLAnchorT      // SCOPE
            suLAnchorB := tapZone ? nbBot : suLAnchorB
    // ◆ SB: MSS preferred. With sbReqMss on, a tagged SB setup only shifts on a
    // true MSS; otherwise an external CHoCH or a CISD still qualifies (its lower
    // grade is recorded in sbShiftGL and costs a point in the SB score).
    if suL == 2 and (sbTagL != 0 ? (sbReqMss ? mssUp : sbShiftUp) : shiftUpOk)     // FIX-V9
        suL := 3
        suLBar := bar_index
        sbShiftGL := mssUp ? 2 : 1
        // a TAGGED SB setup may only lock a CLEAN displacement FVG
        // ◆ FIX-P1-3: the anchor must be the SHIFT LEG's imbalance. v3.1.1 used
        // `cFvgBBar >= suLBar - TRIG_LOOK`, and `suLBar` had just been set to
        // `bar_index` — so the test read "any FVG from the last 10 bars",
        // happily locking a gap cut BEFORE the sweep and unrelated to the
        // displacement. If the shift left no FVG, stage 3 waits for the next
        // one (the block below), which is the correct behaviour.
        if not na(cFvgBTop) and cFvgBBar >= suLBar and (sbTagL == 0 or sbFvgOkL)
            suLAnchorT := cFvgBTop      // lock the shift-leg FVG as the anchor
            suLAnchorB := cFvgBBot
            suLFvgBar  := cFvgBBar
    if suL == 3
        if na(suLFvgBar) and not na(cFvgBTop) and cFvgBBar >= suLBar and (sbTagL == 0 or sbFvgOkL)   // FIX-V9b: the shift bar's own gap counts
            suLAnchorT := cFvgBTop      // first FVG formed after the shift
            suLAnchorB := cFvgBBot
            suLFvgBar  := cFvgBBar
        if not na(suLAnchorT)
            float ceL       = (suLAnchorT + suLAnchorB) / 2      // consequent encroachment
            // ◆ FIX-P1-6 "AWAIT RETEST" IS A REAL STAGE. With a zone anchor
            // (`suLFvgBar` na) v3.1.1 set `ripeL` true immediately, so an entry
            // could land on the very bar the POI was tapped — a reaction, not a
            // retest. The entry bar must now come after the SHIFT bar whatever
            // the anchor type is.
            bool  ripeL     = bar_index > suLBar and (na(suLFvgBar) or bar_index > suLFvgBar)
            // SB needs a LOCKED FVG anchor and (by default) an in-window entry
            bool  sbSetL    = sbTagL != 0 and not na(suLFvgBar) and (sbWinNow == sbTagL or not sbKillAtEnd)
            // ◆ SB-11: a CE fill is a TOUCH plus CONFIRMATION. Touching the CE
            // with a wick and closing back below it is price REJECTING the
            // level, not accepting it — that fill is refused unless the bar
            // itself is a genuine rejection (pin / engulf / IFC / CISD).
            bool  sbRejL    = bullPin or bullEngulf or ifcBull or cisdUp
            // ◆ FIX-X1 A RESTING LIMIT FILLS ONLY ON A REAL TOUCH. `sbCeTol`
            // let the fill count while the low stopped up to 0.10 ATR ABOVE the
            // CE — and the plan price was then written as the EXACT CE, a price
            // the bar never traded. MODULE 18B skips its slippage guard for a
            // CE limit fill, so nothing downstream caught it: every CE entry was
            // optimistic by up to the tolerance, on every counter and every RR.
            // The tolerance now belongs to the REJECTION path only, where the
            // plan price is the traded price rather than the CE.
            bool  sbCeNearL = low <= suLAnchorT and low <= ceL + sbCeTol * atr
            bool  sbCeHitL  = low <= ceL                                               // the limit actually traded
            bool  sbCeTapL  = ceFillReal ? sbCeHitL : (sbCeNearL and (close >= ceL or sbRejL))   // FIX-T8 · FIX-X1
            bool  sbPrxTapL = low <= suLAnchorT and sbRejL      // FIX-X1: near-CE rejections land here, priced at the close
            // ◆ SB-12: premium / discount off the LOCKED window range
            bool  sbLocOkL  = (not sbReqHtf or htfB > 0 or (htfB == 0 and biasOkL)) and (not sbReqPD or (sbPdOK and sbPdPos < 0.5))   // FIX-V1: arm and entry agree on neutral
            bool  sbEnterL  = sbSetL and ripeL and sbLocOkL and (sbCeTapL or (sbEntryMode != "CE (50%) only" and sbPrxTapL))
            // ◆ FIX-X3 the ordinary path's own limit at the CE, with the
            // confirmation candle at the proximal edge as the fallback. Only an
            // FVG anchor has a meaningful CE, so a zone / OTE / HTF tap keeps
            // the v3.4 behaviour exactly.
            bool  mcCeTapL  = mcCeOn and sbTagL == 0 and not na(suLFvgBar) and low <= ceL
            // ◆ FIX-X9 THE CONFIRMATION CANDLE HAS TO CONFIRM THIS ANCHOR.
            // `zoneBullConf` is a generic bar pattern — `bullPin` needs only a
            // lower wick twice its own body — and nothing tied it to the POI
            // being entered. A small pin that never reached the CE and closed
            // in the lower half of its own range counted as acceptance. The
            // entry candle must now trade into the anchor, close above its CE
            // (price accepted the level) and close in its own outer 30%.
            bool  mcCnfOkL  = not mcStrictCnf or (close >= ceL and close >= low + (1 - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT) * rng)
            bool  mcCnfTapL = mcEntryMode != "CE (50%) only" and low <= suLAnchorT and zoneBullConf and mcCnfOkL
            bool  stdEnterL = sbTagL == 0 and ripeL and (mcCeTapL or mcCnfTapL)
            if close < suLAnchorB
                suL := 0
                suLReason := sbTagL != 0 ? "SB FVG violated" : "anchor FVG violated"
            else if (sbEnterL or stdEnterL) and close > suLAnchorB
                sigMCLong := true
                sbSigLong := sbEnterL
                sbCeFillL := sbEnterL and sbCeTapL
                mcCeFillL := stdEnterL and mcCeTapL      // FIX-X3
                // ◆ FIX-P0-4 the plan price: the CE when that is what filled,
                // otherwise the anchor edge price was actually traded at.
                mcPlanLong := sbCeFillL or mcCeFillL ? ceL : math.min(close, suLAnchorT)   // FIX-X3
                // ◆ FIX-P0-5 the machine's OWN stop variable
                // ◆ FIX-X4 raid stop first, anchor stop when it will not fit
                float slAnchL = math.min(low, suLAnchorB) - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                float slRaidL = math.min(math.min(low, suLAnchorB), nz(suLSweepExt, low)) - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                slMCLong := not stopTier or maxRiskAtr == 0 or mcPlanLong - slRaidL <= maxRiskAtr * atr ? slRaidL : slAnchL
                suL := 4
                suLBar := bar_index

// SHORT machine
if confirmed
    sbTagS    := suS == 0 ? 0 : sbTagS     // SCOPE
    sbShiftGS := suS == 0 ? 0 : sbShiftGS
    if suS >= 1
        if not na(suSSweepExt) and close > suSSweepExt
            suS := 0
            suSReason := "sweep undone"
        else if suS <= 2 and not na(suSSweepLvl) and close > suSSweepLvl + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr     // FIX-N7
            suS := 0
            suSReason := "sweep reclaim lost"
        // ◆ FIX-X6 (see the long side)
        else if bullBar and body >= dispFactor * atr and suS <= 3
             and not (suS == 3 and not na(suSAnchorT) and close < suSAnchorT)
            suS := 0
            suSReason := "opposing displacement"
        else if suS == 1 and sslSweep and lastSslQual >= 2                          // FIX-V2b
            suS := 0
            suSReason := "range swept both ways"
    if suS >= 2 and na(suSFvgBar) and not na(suSAnchorT) and close > suSAnchorT      // FIX-T1
        suS := 0
        suSReason := "POI violated (own anchor)"
    if htfProtInval and suS >= 1 and suS <= 3 and not na(hsProtHi) and close > hsProtHi  // FIX-T6
        suS := 0
        suSReason := "HTF protected high broken"
    if sbKillAtEnd and sbKillS and suS >= 1 and suS <= 3
        suS := 0
        suSReason := "SB window closed"
    bool expS = (suS == 1 and bar_index - suSBar > sbLiqWS) or (suS == 2 and bar_index - suSBar > sbPoiWS) or (suS == 3 and bar_index - suSBar > sbRetWS)
    if expS                            // SCOPE (see the long side)
        suSReason := suS == 1 ? "sweep expired (no POI tap)" : suS == 2 ? "POI tap expired (no shift)" : "retest window expired"
        suS := 0
    if suS == 0 and bslInPlay and sbArmBarS and (lastBslQual >= minArmQual or sbArmOkS)
         and (not armNeedHtf or biasOkS) and lockOkS                                   // FIX-V1 · FIX-V2
        bool chopBlock = useRegime and regime == "CHOP" and not instSweepS and not (sbArmOkS and sbChopBypass)   // FIX-T17
        if not chopBlock
            suS := 1
            suSBar := bar_index
            suSSweepLvl := lastBslLvl
            suSSweepExt := lastBslExt
            suSAnchorT := na
            suSAnchorB := na
            suSFvgBar  := na
            sbTagS     := sbArmOkS ? sbCtxWin : 0
            sbShiftGS  := 0
            armedBslBar := lastBslBar           // FIX-T3: this sweep is spent
            if sbArmOkS
                sbArmedBslBar := lastBslBar     // SB-9: this sweep is spent
    if suS == 1 and bslInPlay and (sbTagS != 0 ? (sbReqMss ? mssDn : sbShiftDn) : (fastShift and shiftDnOk))   // FIX-V9b · FIX-V14
        suS := 3
        suSBar := bar_index
        sbShiftGS := mssDn ? 2 : 1
        suSAnchorT := na
        suSAnchorB := na
        suSFvgBar  := na
    if suS == 1
        bool tapZone = inSupply and retestShort and not poiBlockS     // FIX-V4 / FIX-W3
        bool tapOte  = poiSrc != "Zone / HTF POI only" and inOTEsell     // FIX-T5
        bool tapHtf  = htfLive and not na(hPoiSTop) and high >= hPoiSBot and close < hPoiSTop
        if tapZone or tapOte or tapHtf
            suS := 2
            suSBar := bar_index
            suSAnchorT := tapZone ? nsTop : suSAnchorT      // SCOPE
            suSAnchorB := tapZone ? nsBot : suSAnchorB
    if suS == 2 and (sbTagS != 0 ? (sbReqMss ? mssDn : sbShiftDn) : shiftDnOk)     // FIX-V9
        suS := 3
        suSBar := bar_index
        sbShiftGS := mssDn ? 2 : 1
        // ◆ FIX-P1-3 anchor must belong to the shift leg (see the long side)
        if not na(cFvgSTop) and cFvgSBar >= suSBar and (sbTagS == 0 or sbFvgOkS)
            suSAnchorT := cFvgSTop
            suSAnchorB := cFvgSBot
            suSFvgBar  := cFvgSBar
    if suS == 3
        if na(suSFvgBar) and not na(cFvgSTop) and cFvgSBar >= suSBar and (sbTagS == 0 or sbFvgOkS)   // FIX-V9b
            suSAnchorT := cFvgSTop
            suSAnchorB := cFvgSBot
            suSFvgBar  := cFvgSBar
        if not na(suSAnchorB)
            float ceS       = (suSAnchorT + suSAnchorB) / 2
            // ◆ FIX-P1-6 the entry bar must follow the shift bar (see long side)
            bool  ripeS     = bar_index > suSBar and (na(suSFvgBar) or bar_index > suSFvgBar)
            bool  sbSetS    = sbTagS != 0 and not na(suSFvgBar) and (sbWinNow == sbTagS or not sbKillAtEnd)
            // ◆ SB-11: touch + close through the CE, or a genuine rejection
            bool  sbRejS    = bearPin or bearEngulf or ifcBear or cisdDn
            // ◆ FIX-X1 the limit must be traded (see the long side)
            bool  sbCeNearS = high >= suSAnchorB and high >= ceS - sbCeTol * atr
            bool  sbCeHitS  = high >= ceS
            bool  sbCeTapS  = ceFillReal ? sbCeHitS : (sbCeNearS and (close <= ceS or sbRejS))   // FIX-T8 · FIX-X1
            bool  sbPrxTapS = high >= suSAnchorB and sbRejS      // FIX-X1
            // ◆ SB-12: premium / discount off the LOCKED window range
            bool  sbLocOkS  = (not sbReqHtf or htfB < 0 or (htfB == 0 and biasOkS)) and (not sbReqPD or (sbPdOK and sbPdPos >= 0.5))   // FIX-V1
            bool  sbEnterS  = sbSetS and ripeS and sbLocOkS and (sbCeTapS or (sbEntryMode != "CE (50%) only" and sbPrxTapS))
            // ◆ FIX-X3 the ordinary path's CE limit (see the long side)
            bool  mcCeTapS  = mcCeOn and sbTagS == 0 and not na(suSFvgBar) and high >= ceS
            // ◆ FIX-X9 (see the long side)
            bool  mcCnfOkS  = not mcStrictCnf or (close <= ceS and close <= high - (1 - STRONG_CLOSE_PCT) * rng)
            bool  mcCnfTapS = mcEntryMode != "CE (50%) only" and high >= suSAnchorB and zoneBearConf and mcCnfOkS
            bool  stdEnterS = sbTagS == 0 and ripeS and (mcCeTapS or mcCnfTapS)
            if close > suSAnchorT
                suS := 0
                suSReason := sbTagS != 0 ? "SB FVG violated" : "anchor FVG violated"
            else if (sbEnterS or stdEnterS) and close < suSAnchorT
                sigMCShort := true
                sbSigShort := sbEnterS
                sbCeFillS  := sbEnterS and sbCeTapS
                mcCeFillS  := stdEnterS and mcCeTapS      // FIX-X3
