                // ◆ FIX-P0-4 plan price · FIX-P0-5 the machine's own stop
                mcPlanShort := sbCeFillS or mcCeFillS ? ceS : math.max(close, suSAnchorB)   // FIX-X3
                // ◆ FIX-X4 (see the long side)
                float slAnchS = math.max(high, suSAnchorT) + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                float slRaidS = math.max(math.max(high, suSAnchorT), nz(suSSweepExt, high)) + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                slMCShort   := not stopTier or maxRiskAtr == 0 or slRaidS - mcPlanShort <= maxRiskAtr * atr ? slRaidS : slAnchS
                suS := 4
                suSBar := bar_index

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 18
// SIGNAL FINALIZE — context gates, tier filter, cooldowns, conflict rule
// Precedence on conflict (spec §45): the machine narrative wins; if both
// directions fire on one bar → NO TRADE (both suppressed).
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var int lastLongSigBar  = na
var int lastShortSigBar = na
longCdOK  = masterCd == 0 or na(lastLongSigBar)  or bar_index - lastLongSigBar  > masterCd
shortCdOK = masterCd == 0 or na(lastShortSigBar) or bar_index - lastShortSigBar > masterCd

// ◆ FIX-P0-7 THE SB CHOP BYPASS REACHES THE FINAL GATE TOO.
// MODULE 17 honours `sbChopBypass` when ARMING, but v3.1.1's `chopOKL` did not
// know about it. So inside chop an SB setup armed, tapped its POI, shifted,
// locked its FVG, filled at the CE — and was then killed right here, silently,
// with no reason recorded anywhere. Either the bypass is the rule or it is not.
// ◆ FIX-T10: the exemption read the EFFECTIVE grade, which an MSS sets to 4
// itself (MODULE 6) — so any MSS inside chop unlocked the filter that was meant
// to judge it. ARCH-5's exception is an INSTITUTIONAL RAID: a raw strong sweep
// AND a real structure shift, two independent pieces of evidence.
chopOKL = not useRegime or regime != "CHOP" or instSweepL
     or (sbEnable and sbChopBypass and sbSigLong)
chopOKS = not useRegime or regime != "CHOP" or instSweepS
     or (sbEnable and sbChopBypass and sbSigShort)
// ◆ SB-only mode: nothing fires outside an SB context window
sbOnlyOK = not (sbEnable and sbOnly and timeframe.isintraday) or sbCtx
// ◆ FIX-W16 the day's book. Incremented in MODULE 19 (entries) and by the stop
// handler there, so this gate reads the state as it stood at the previous bar
// — which is what a trader knows when the next setup appears.
var int trdToday  = 0
var int lossToday = 0
if newDay
    trdToday  := 0
    lossToday := 0
govTrdOK  = not useGov or not timeframe.isintraday or maxTrdDay == 0 or trdToday < maxTrdDay
govLossOK = not useGov or not timeframe.isintraday or maxLossDay == 0 or lossToday < maxLossDay
govOK     = govTrdOK and govLossOK
// FIX-F1: the block reads the COMBINED bias (`htfB`), not the raw EMA read
// FIX-V1 the shared bias permission · FIX-V2 the direction lock
// ◆ FIX-X5 the dealing-range side gate (see the input's note). An SB entry has
// already answered this question through `sbReqPD` against its own LOCKED
// range, so the SB override below deliberately does not re-ask it.
gateScoreL = scoreOnBase ? scoreBaseL : scoreLong      // FIX-X8
gateScoreS = scoreOnBase ? scoreBaseS : scoreShort
mcPdOKL = not mcReqPD or not rangeOK or not rangeHolds or pdPos < 0.5 or (mcPdWaive and htfB > 0 and extDir > 0)
mcPdOKS = not mcReqPD or not rangeOK or not rangeHolds or pdPos >= 0.5 or (mcPdWaive and htfB < 0 and extDir < 0)
longOK  = biasOkL and lockOkL and kzOK and newsOK and longCdOK and chopOKL and sbOnlyOK and govOK and mcPdOKL and (minScore == 0 or gateScoreL >= minScore)
shortOK = biasOkS and lockOkS and kzOK and newsOK and shortCdOK and chopOKS and sbOnlyOK and govOK and mcPdOKS and (minScore == 0 or gateScoreS >= minScore)
// ◆ FIX-V7 (F-12): an SB entry outranks the same-direction COOLDOWN (SB-13 rank 3
// is the highest claim on the bar) — every other gate still applies.
longOK  := longOK  or (sbSigLong  and biasOkL and lockOkL and kzOK and newsOK and chopOKL and sbOnlyOK and govOK and (minScore == 0 or gateScoreL >= minScore))
shortOK := shortOK or (sbSigShort and biasOkS and lockOkS and kzOK and newsOK and chopOKS and sbOnlyOK and govOK and (minScore == 0 or gateScoreS >= minScore))

// ◆ FIX-P1-2 MACHINE TIER IS GRADED, NOT ASSUMED.
// The full chain (quality sweep → POI → shift → retest) genuinely is A-grade,
// so A stays the floor. But v3.1.1 wrote `math.max(tierLong, 3)` and then
// tested that against `minRank` — which can never fail for 1/2/3. The display
// filter was a no-op for machine entries and `sbMinTier = "A and above"` did
// literally nothing. A+ is now earned from the real components, using the RAW
// sweep grade and, for an SB-tagged setup, the LOCKED dealing range — the same
// range its entry rule used, so the two can no longer disagree.
f_mcTier(bool isLong) =>
    int t = 3
    bool qualOk  = (isLong ? lastSslQualRaw : lastBslQualRaw) >= 3
    bool htfOk   = isLong ? htfB > 0 : htfB < 0
    bool shiftOk = (isLong ? sbShiftGL : sbShiftGS) >= 2          // a true MSS
    bool pdOkL   = sbTagL != 0 ? (sbPdOK and sbPdPos < 0.5)  : (rangeOK and pdPos < 0.5)
    bool pdOkS   = sbTagS != 0 ? (sbPdOK and sbPdPos >= 0.5) : (rangeOK and pdPos >= 0.5)
    bool pdOk    = isLong ? pdOkL : pdOkS
    bool poolOk  = (isLong ? lastSslKind : lastBslKind) >= 2      // engineered pool
    // FIX-F4: no A+ for an entry that chases an exhausted daily range
    bool adrOk   = not (isLong ? adrChaseL : adrChaseS)
    if qualOk and htfOk and shiftOk and pdOk and poolOk and sesTierOK and adrOk
        t := 4
    t
mcTierL = f_mcTier(true)
mcTierS = f_mcTier(false)
// ◆ SB-7: a pure SB entry must clear a HIGHER tier floor than the generic
// machine entry (A by default, A+ by input) on top of the display filter.
sbTierMin = sbMinTier == "A+ only" ? 4 : 3
sigMCLong  := sigMCLong  and longOK  and mcTierL >= (sbSigLong  ? math.max(minRank, sbTierMin) : minRank)
sigMCShort := sigMCShort and shortOK and mcTierS >= (sbSigShort ? math.max(minRank, sbTierMin) : minRank)
if not sigMCLong
    sbSigLong := false
    mcCeFillL := false      // FIX-X3: no machine signal, no machine limit fill
if not sigMCShort
    sbSigShort := false
    mcCeFillS := false
// ◆ FIX-V8 (v3.3, audit F-12) A FINAL-GATE MUTE IS A REASON AND A RETRY, NOT A
// SILENT DEATH. MODULE 17 had already written stage 4; when `longOK` / the tier
// floor refused the entry nothing was recorded, the stage fell to IDLE at the
// end of the bar and the sweep was spent — an SB CE fill that scored 48 < 50
// simply vanished. The stage now returns to SHIFTED (the anchor is still valid)
// with the blocking gate named, and the retest window gets another chance.
string gateKillL = suL == 4 and suLBar == bar_index and not sigMCLong ? (not longOK ? (minScore > 0 and gateScoreL < minScore ? "gate: score " + str.tostring(gateScoreL) + " < " + str.tostring(minScore) : not govOK ? (not govTrdOK ? "gate: daily signal limit" : "gate: daily loss limit") : not longCdOK ? "gate: cooldown" : not newsOK ? "gate: volatility window" : not chopOKL ? "gate: chop" : not lockOkL ? "gate: short narrative live" : not mcPdOKL ? "gate: premium — wrong side of the dealing range" : "gate: bias / session") : "gate: tier " + f_tierTxt(mcTierL) + " below floor") : ""
string gateKillS = suS == 4 and suSBar == bar_index and not sigMCShort ? (not shortOK ? (minScore > 0 and gateScoreS < minScore ? "gate: score " + str.tostring(gateScoreS) + " < " + str.tostring(minScore) : not govOK ? (not govTrdOK ? "gate: daily signal limit" : "gate: daily loss limit") : not shortCdOK ? "gate: cooldown" : not newsOK ? "gate: volatility window" : not chopOKS ? "gate: chop" : not lockOkS ? "gate: long narrative live" : not mcPdOKS ? "gate: discount — wrong side of the dealing range" : "gate: bias / session") : "gate: tier " + f_tierTxt(mcTierS) + " below floor") : ""
suL := gateKillL != "" ? 3 : suL
suS := gateKillS != "" ? 3 : suS
suLReason := gateKillL != "" ? gateKillL : suLReason
suSReason := gateKillS != "" ? gateKillS : suSReason

// engine trigger candidates: graded through the same layer model
// ◆ SB precedence: while a clean SB setup is live (tagged and past its POI)
// the ordinary engine candidates are down-weighted one tier, or muted. The
// machine path itself is never muted — it is what carries the SB narrative.
sbLiveL = sbEnable and sbTagL != 0 and suL >= 2
sbLiveS = sbEnable and sbTagS != 0 and suS >= 2
sbLive  = sbLiveL or sbLiveS
engRank = sbLive and sbSuppress == "Down-weight" ? math.min(4, minRank + 1) : minRank
engMute = sbLive and sbSuppress == "Suppress"
engTierOKL = tierLong  >= engRank and not engMute
engTierOKS = tierShort >= engRank and not engMute
sigSDLong   := sigSDLong   and longOK and engTierOKL
sig3Long    := sig3Long    and longOK and engTierOKL
sig4Long    := sig4Long    and longOK and engTierOKL
sigTLLong   := sigTLLong   and longOK and engTierOKL
sigCRTLong  := sigCRTLong  and longOK and engTierOKL
sigTBSLong  := sigTBSLong  and longOK and engTierOKL
sigHCrtLong := sigHCrtLong and longOK and engTierOKL
sigHTbsLong := sigHTbsLong and longOK and engTierOKL
sigFEMLong  := sigFEMLong  and longOK and engTierOKL
sigLTLong   := sigLTLong   and longOK and engTierOKL      // FIX-V6: the trap is an engine candidate
sigSDShort   := sigSDShort   and shortOK and engTierOKS
sig3Short    := sig3Short    and shortOK and engTierOKS
sig4Short    := sig4Short    and shortOK and engTierOKS
sigTLShort   := sigTLShort   and shortOK and engTierOKS
sigCRTShort  := sigCRTShort  and shortOK and engTierOKS
sigTBSShort  := sigTBSShort  and shortOK and engTierOKS
sigHCrtShort := sigHCrtShort and shortOK and engTierOKS
sigHTbsShort := sigHTbsShort and shortOK and engTierOKS
sigFEMShort  := sigFEMShort  and shortOK and engTierOKS
sigLTShort   := sigLTShort   and shortOK and engTierOKS

anyLong  = sigMCLong  or sigSDLong  or sig3Long  or sig4Long  or sigTLLong  or sigCRTLong  or sigTBSLong  or sigHCrtLong  or sigHTbsLong  or sigFEMLong  or sigLTLong
anyShort = sigMCShort or sigSDShort or sig3Short or sig4Short or sigTLShort or sigCRTShort or sigTBSShort or sigHCrtShort or sigHTbsShort or sigFEMShort or sigLTShort

// ◆ SB-13 conflict rule — a HIERARCHY, not a mutual kill. v3 muted BOTH sides
// whenever the two directions fired on one bar, so a full SB narrative was
// thrown away by any stray engine candidate on the other side (the "silent
// day" problem). Rank of a bar's signal:
//     3 = SB machine entry · 2 = ordinary machine entry · 1 = pure engine
// The higher rank takes the bar and the loser is muted. Only an EQUAL-rank
// clash still mutes both — engine vs engine, machine vs machine, SB vs SB —
// because that is a genuinely split narrative with no tie-break left.
if anyLong and anyShort
    int rkL = sigMCLong  ? (sbSigLong  ? 3 : 2) : 1
    int rkS = sigMCShort ? (sbSigShort ? 3 : 2) : 1
    if rkL > rkS
        anyShort   := false
        sigMCShort := false
        sbSigShort := false
        if suS == 4 and suSBar == bar_index     // roll back the losing entry
            suS := 0
            suSReason := "conflict — higher-rank long won the bar"
    else if rkS > rkL
        anyLong   := false
        sigMCLong := false
        sbSigLong := false
        if suL == 4 and suLBar == bar_index
            suL := 0
            suLReason := "conflict — higher-rank short won the bar"
    else
        anyLong  := false
        anyShort := false

// ◬ Liquidity Trap veto: while the trap engine is WAITING for confirmation in
// one direction, the opposing breakout side is suspect — suppress those ENGINE
// candidates until it resolves.
// ◆ FIX-N3 THE VETO OBEYS THE SB-13 HIERARCHY. It used to mute `anyLong`
// outright, which killed a completed machine / SB entry (sweep → POI → shift →
// retest, all confirmed) on the say-so of a trap that had NOT confirmed yet.
// SB-13 ranks the machine above every pure engine; an unconfirmed trap is a
// weaker claim than either. Only engine-only bars are muted now.
// SCOPE: the veto as two assignments
anyLong  := ltEnable and ltVeto and ltSState == 1 and not sigMCLong  ? false : anyLong
anyShort := ltEnable and ltVeto and ltLState == 1 and not sigMCShort ? false : anyShort

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 18B
// ◆ TRADE VIABILITY GATE (FIX-P0-4 · FIX-P0-5 · FIX-P1-7)
// The last "no" in the chain, and it runs BEFORE the cooldown stamps so a
// rejected signal does not burn the direction's cooldown or leave the machine
// parked in ACTIVE with no trade behind it.
//   1. ENTRY is the plan price. v3.1.1 used the bar CLOSE for everything. A CE
//      limit fills at the CE; the close can be most of a candle away. Every RR
//      printed on the chart, every TP level and every outcome counter was
//      measured from a price no order was ever placed at.
//   2. THE STOP belongs to the engine that actually fired (see FIX-P0-5).
//   3. RISK IS CAPPED. The machine anchors its stop on the sweep wick, which
//      can sit twenty bars away — R became enormous, TP1 unreachable and the
//      break-even shift dead code. Too wide now means the setup is REJECTED
//      (you are late), or the stop is clamped, by input.
//   4. SLIPPAGE GUARD: if the bar closed far past the plan price, the fill is
//      fiction.
//   5. MIN RR: a plan that cannot pay `minRR` to TP2 is not a plan.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// stop of the SURVIVING signal, highest-conviction engine first
float selSLLong  = sigMCLong ? slMCLong : sigFEMLong ? slFEMLong : sigSDLong ? slSDLong :
     sigCRTLong ? slCRTLong : sigTBSLong ? slTBSLong : sigTLLong ? slTLLong : sigLTLong ? slLTLong : na
float selSLShort = sigMCShort ? slMCShort : sigFEMShort ? slFEMShort : sigSDShort ? slSDShort :
     sigCRTShort ? slCRTShort : sigTBSShort ? slTBSShort : sigTLShort ? slTLShort : sigLTShort ? slLTShort : na

// plan entry: the machine/SB plan price when the machine fired, else the close
float planEntry = anyLong ? nz(mcPlanLong, close) : anyShort ? nz(mcPlanShort, close) : na
float planSL    = na
float planTP2   = na
bool  planOK    = true
string planKill = ""
if (anyLong or anyShort) and confirmed
    float fallbackL = math.min(low, low[1]) - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
    float fallbackS = math.max(high, high[1]) + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
    float slL = not na(selSLLong)  and selSLLong  < planEntry ? selSLLong  : fallbackL
    float slS = not na(selSLShort) and selSLShort > planEntry ? selSLShort : fallbackS
    planSL := anyLong ? slL : slS
    // 4 — did price realistically trade the plan price on this bar?
    // FIX-T8: a CE limit fill is proven by the touch itself — the guard exists
    // for plan prices the bar may never have traded, not for a resting order.
    // FIX-X3: the ordinary path's CE fill is a resting limit too — its touch is
    // the same proof, and it is not governed by the SB fill-model input.
    bool ceLimitFill = (ceFillReal and (sbCeFillL or sbCeFillS)) or mcCeFillL or mcCeFillS
    if slipGuard > 0 and not ceLimitFill and math.abs(close - planEntry) > slipGuard * atr
        planOK   := false
        planKill := "entry slipped (close " + str.tostring(math.round(math.abs(close - planEntry) / atr * 100) / 100) + " ATR from the plan price)"
    // 3 — risk cap
    float planRisk = math.abs(planEntry - planSL)
    if planOK and maxRiskAtr > 0 and planRisk > maxRiskAtr * atr
        if riskMode == "Clamp the stop"
            planSL   := anyLong ? planEntry - maxRiskAtr * atr : planEntry + maxRiskAtr * atr
            planRisk := maxRiskAtr * atr
        else
            planOK   := false
            planKill := "risk " + str.tostring(math.round(planRisk / atr * 100) / 100) + " ATR exceeds the cap"
    // 5 — REWARD REALISM. v3.1.1 placed TP2 at `rrMult × risk` and never asked
    // whether an opposing POI sat in the way. If the next unspent supply zone
    // is closer than TP2, that zone IS the realistic target — so TP2 is clamped
    // to it, and the trade is only taken if what remains still pays `minRR`.
    if planOK
        float wantTP2 = anyLong ? planEntry + rrMult * planRisk : planEntry - rrMult * planRisk
        if anyLong and not na(nsBot) and nsBot > planEntry
            wantTP2 := math.min(wantTP2, nsBot)
        if anyShort and not na(nbTop) and nbTop < planEntry
            wantTP2 := math.max(wantTP2, nbTop)
        planTP2 := wantTP2
        // FIX-W18: net of costs, which is the only RR that can be banked
        float cost = costTicks * syminfo.mintick
        float rr = planRisk + cost > 0 ? math.max(0, math.abs(wantTP2 - planEntry) - cost) / (planRisk + cost) : 0
        if minRR > 0 and rr < minRR
            planOK   := false
            planKill := "reward blocked — only " + str.tostring(math.round(rr * 100) / 100) + "R net to the first opposing POI"
    if planOK and planRisk <= 0
        planOK   := false
        planKill := "degenerate risk"
if not planOK
    // roll the whole bar back — including a machine state that just went ACTIVE
    if anyLong and suL == 4 and suLBar == bar_index
        suL := 0
        suLReason := planKill
    if anyShort and suS == 4 and suSBar == bar_index
        suS := 0
        suSReason := planKill
    anyLong    := false
    anyShort   := false
    sigMCLong  := false
    sigMCShort := false
    sbSigLong  := false
    sbSigShort := false

// ◆ SB final fire flags — consumed by the label, the exit engine and the alert
sbFireL = anyLong  and sigMCLong  and sbSigLong
sbFireS = anyShort and sigMCShort and sbSigShort
sbDoneWinL := sbFireL ? sbTagL : sbDoneWinL        // FIX-V10: this window is spent for longs
sbDoneWinS := sbFireS ? sbTagS : sbDoneWinS

// ◆ FIX-T7: this bar's signal is ENGINE-only, so the tracked fill waits for the
// next bar's open. The label and the alert still print on this bar — that is
// when you learn about it — but no number is measured from a price no order
// could have been filled at.
// FIX-X3: a machine CE fill is a LIMIT — it fills on the touch, so it is never
// deferred to the next open. Only a confirmation-candle entry is.
mcFillNext  = mcFillMode == "Next bar open (realistic)" and (sigMCLong or sigMCShort) and not (sbSigLong or sbSigShort) and not (mcCeFillL or mcCeFillS)   // FIX-W6 · FIX-X3
engFillNext = (engFillMode == "Next bar open (realistic)" and (anyLong or anyShort) and not (sigMCLong or sigMCShort)) or mcFillNext

// ◆ FIX-N1 ONE TIER PER SIGNAL.
// FIX-P1-2 graded the machine entry properly (`mcTierL`), but four consumers
// still used the old `math.max(tierLong, sigMCLong ? 3 : 1)` — the label
// header, the A/A+ counters and the A/A+ alertconditions. So one bar could
// print "A · 55" above "◉ SETUP A+" in the SAME label, and fire the "A LONG"
// alert while the label said A+. Every consumer now reads this one value.
finalTierL = sigMCLong  ? math.max(tierLong,  mcTierL) : tierLong
finalTierS = sigMCShort ? math.max(tierShort, mcTierS) : tierShort

// stamp cooldowns from FINAL signal state only
// SCOPE: six stamps, zero branches
lastCrtLongBar  := sigCRTLong and anyLong   ? bar_index : lastCrtLongBar
lastCrtShortBar := sigCRTShort and anyShort ? bar_index : lastCrtShortBar
lastTbsLongBar  := sigTBSLong and anyLong   ? bar_index : lastTbsLongBar
lastTbsShortBar := sigTBSShort and anyShort ? bar_index : lastTbsShortBar
lastLongSigBar  := anyLong  ? bar_index : lastLongSigBar
lastShortSigBar := anyShort ? bar_index : lastShortSigBar

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 19
// TP / SL — structural stop + TP ladder + ACTIVE trade tracking (validation)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// (trd* tracker state is declared in MODULE 15A — FIX-V2 reads `trdDir` there)
bool sbTimeExitEvt   = false
bool sbStructExitEvt = false
bool sbBeEvt         = false

// nearest pool at or beyond `ref` — used to pick the SB liquidity target
f_nearAbove(float best, float cand, float ref) => not na(cand) and cand >= ref and (na(best) or cand < best) ? cand : best
f_nearBelow(float best, float cand, float ref) => not na(cand) and cand <= ref and (na(best) or cand > best) ? cand : best
// validation counters (ARCH-10 — mechanical counts, NOT statistics)
var int cntA      = 0
var int cntAp     = 0
var int cntLong   = 0
var int cntShort  = 0
var int cntTP1    = 0
var int cntTP2    = 0
var int cntSL     = 0
var int cntTP3      = 0
var int cntSbEnt    = 0
var int cntSbTime   = 0
var int cntSbStruct = 0
// ◆ FIX-T2: a trade that simply ran out of time, and the signals the tracker
// could not take because it was still busy. Counting them separately is the
// only way the A / A+ counts and the TP/SL counts can be read together.
var int cntExp       = 0
var int cntUntracked = 0
var int trdBar       = na
// ◆ FIX-T7: an engine signal waiting to fill at the next bar's open
var int   pendDir = 0
var float pendSL  = na

// resolve the ACTIVE trade before a new one can replace it
// v3.1: a NON-SB trade keeps the v3 path and the v3 counter semantics exactly
// (closed at TP2). An SB-tagged trade runs the SB-6 exit engine instead:
//   TP1  → stop to break-even + sbBeBuf ATR
//   TP2  → stay in, trail behind the external protected swing
//   TP3  → the liquidity target (nearest opposing pool that pays ≥ TP2)
//   time → window close with TP1 still unhit: full exit, or reduce and put
//          the remainder at break-even
//   struct → opposing MSS, or an opposing CHoCH/CISD when the entry shift was
//          only CHoCH/CISD grade (equal or higher quality, never lower)
// ◆ FIX-T7 DEFERRED ENGINE FILL. An engine signal is only known at the CLOSE of
// its bar, so the first price a real order can be filled at is the NEXT bar's
// open. The order fills here — before this bar's exits are evaluated, so a
// trade can still be stopped out on its entry bar — and the whole TP ladder is
// re-measured from the price actually paid, not from last bar's close.
if confirmed and pendDir != 0
    // the machine outranks a pure engine order (SB-13): a machine entry on this
    // same bar takes the tracker, so the pending engine order is cancelled
    // FIX-W7 (OP-7): whatever happens below, this order was counted as tracked
    // when it was queued. If it cannot fill, the count has to be given back or
    // the A / A+ totals and the TP/SL totals describe different populations.
    bool pendFilled = false
    if trdDir == 0 and not (sigMCLong or sigMCShort)
        float pRisk = math.abs(open - pendSL)
        // ◆ FIX-X2 THE DEFERRED ORDER IS RE-VALIDATED AT THE PRICE IT FILLS AT.
        // MODULE 18B cleared the plan against LAST bar's close; this order fills
        // at THIS bar's open, which can be a gap away. v3.4 re-derived the TP
        // ladder from the new price but re-tested nothing: the slippage guard,
        // the risk cap and the minimum RR were all bypassed, so a gap-open order
        // filled at a risk and an RR that the same setup would have been
        // rejected for one bar earlier. The three gates are applied again here,
        // against the fill price. A failed gate cancels the order (the FIX-W7
        // untracked counter below already gives the count back).
        // SCOPE: the TP2 clamp is written as expressions so the extra gates
        // cost no local scopes.
        float pTp2raw = pendDir == 1 ? open + rrMult * pRisk : open - rrMult * pRisk
        float pTp2 = pendDir == 1 and not na(nsBot) and nsBot > open ? math.min(pTp2raw, nsBot) :
             pendDir == -1 and not na(nbTop) and nbTop < open ? math.max(pTp2raw, nbTop) : pTp2raw
        float pCost = costTicks * syminfo.mintick
        float pRR   = pRisk + pCost > 0 ? math.max(0, math.abs(pTp2 - open) - pCost) / (pRisk + pCost) : 0
        bool  pSlipOK = slipGuard == 0 or math.abs(open - close[1]) <= slipGuard * atr
        bool  pRiskOK = maxRiskAtr == 0 or pRisk <= maxRiskAtr * atr
        bool  pRrOK   = minRR == 0 or pRR >= minRR
        if pRisk > 0 and (pendDir == 1 ? open > pendSL : open < pendSL) and pSlipOK and pRiskOK and pRrOK
            float pM = math.max(3.0, rrMult + 1)
            trdEntry  := open
            trdSL     := pendSL
            trdTP1    := pendDir == 1 ? open + pRisk : open - pRisk
            trdTP2    := pTp2
            trdTP3    := pendDir == 1 ? open + pM * pRisk : open - pM * pRisk
            trdDir    := pendDir
            trdBar    := bar_index
            trdTp1Hit := false
            trdTp2Hit := false
            trdIsSb   := false
            trdSbWin  := 0
            trdShiftG := 0
            trdLiqTgt := na
            pendFilled := true
    cntUntracked := pendFilled ? cntUntracked : cntUntracked + 1      // FIX-W7
    pendDir := 0        // a gap through the stop, or the tracker busy: order cancelled

if confirmed and trdDir != 0
    if not trdIsSb
        // ◆ FIX-P1-8(d): TP1 and TP2 touched on the SAME bar used to count only
        // TP1 — the `else if` chain stopped after the first match, so a strong
        // run through both levels was recorded as a half-finished trade.
        // The stop is still checked first (pessimistic: a bar that touched both
        // the stop and a target is booked as a loss, because intrabar order is
        // unknown).
        if trdDir == 1
            if low <= trdSL
                cntSL += 1
                lossToday += 1      // FIX-W16
                trdDir := 0
            else
                if not trdTp1Hit and high >= trdTP1
                    trdTp1Hit := true
                    cntTP1 += 1
                    // SB-14's adaptive break-even, applied to every trade now —
                    // there is nothing SB-specific about protecting a runner
                    // once it has paid 1R.
                    trdSL := math.max(trdSL, trdEntry + math.max(sbBeBuf * atr, SB_BE_TP1_PCT * math.abs(trdTP1 - trdEntry)))
                    sbBeEvt := true
                if trdTp1Hit and not trdTp2Hit and high >= trdTP2
                    trdTp2Hit := true
                    cntTP2 += 1
                    if nonSbTp3
                        // FIX-T2: book the partial, lock 1R, let the rest run
                        trdSL := math.max(trdSL, trdEntry + math.abs(trdTP1 - trdEntry))
                    else
                        trdDir := 0
                if nonSbTp3 and trdDir == 1 and trdTp2Hit and high >= trdTP3
                    cntTP3 += 1
                    trdDir := 0
        else
            if high >= trdSL
                cntSL += 1
                lossToday += 1      // FIX-W16
                trdDir := 0
            else
                if not trdTp1Hit and low <= trdTP1
                    trdTp1Hit := true
                    cntTP1 += 1
                    trdSL := math.min(trdSL, trdEntry - math.max(sbBeBuf * atr, SB_BE_TP1_PCT * math.abs(trdEntry - trdTP1)))
                    sbBeEvt := true
                if trdTp1Hit and not trdTp2Hit and low <= trdTP2
                    trdTp2Hit := true
                    cntTP2 += 1
                    if nonSbTp3
                        trdSL := math.min(trdSL, trdEntry - math.abs(trdEntry - trdTP1))
                    else
                        trdDir := 0
                if nonSbTp3 and trdDir == -1 and trdTp2Hit and low <= trdTP3
                    cntTP3 += 1
                    trdDir := 0
    else
        bool sbWEnd = trdSbWin == 1 ? sbEndLOEvt : trdSbWin == 2 ? sbEndAMEvt : trdSbWin == 3 ? sbEndPMEvt : false
        if trdDir == 1
            if low <= trdSL
                cntSL += 1
                lossToday += 1      // FIX-W16
                trdDir := 0
            else
                if not trdTp1Hit and high >= trdTP1
                    trdTp1Hit := true
                    cntTP1 += 1
                    // ◆ SB-14: ADAPTIVE break-even buffer — the LARGER of the
                    // fixed ATR buffer and SB_BE_TP1_PCT of the TP1 distance.
                    // On a wide-stop trade a flat 0.15 ATR left almost the
                    // whole first leg exposed; this scales with the trade.
                    float beBufL = math.max(sbBeBuf * atr, SB_BE_TP1_PCT * math.abs(trdTP1 - trdEntry))
                    trdSL := math.max(trdSL, trdEntry + beBufL)
                    sbBeEvt := true
                if trdTp1Hit and not trdTp2Hit and high >= trdTP2
                    trdTp2Hit := true
                    cntTP2 += 1
                if trdTp2Hit and sbTrail and not na(protLowE)
                    // ◆ SB-14: trail behind the protected swing, but NEVER
                    // through the last TWO bars' low — that is the immediate
                    // structure the runner needs to breathe against. Still
                    // never through current price either (an already violated
                    // stop would fake a fill on the next bar).
                    float capL = math.min(math.min(low, low[1]), close) - SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                    trdSL := math.max(trdSL, math.min(protLowE - SL_PAD_ATR * atr, capL))
                if trdDir == 1 and high >= trdTP3
                    cntTP3 += 1
                    trdDir := 0
                if trdDir == 1 and sbStructEx and (mssDn or (trdShiftG <= 1 and (cisdDn or chochDn or eChochDn)))
                    cntSbStruct += 1
                    sbStructExitEvt := true
                    trdDir := 0
                if trdDir == 1 and sbTimeExit and sbWEnd and not trdTp1Hit
                    cntSbTime += 1
                    sbTimeExitEvt := true
                    if sbTimeMode == "Full"
                        trdDir := 0
                    else
                        trdSL := math.max(trdSL, trdEntry)
        else
            if high >= trdSL
                cntSL += 1
                lossToday += 1      // FIX-W16
                trdDir := 0
            else
                if not trdTp1Hit and low <= trdTP1
                    trdTp1Hit := true
                    cntTP1 += 1
                    // ◆ SB-14: adaptive break-even buffer (see the long side)
                    float beBufS = math.max(sbBeBuf * atr, SB_BE_TP1_PCT * math.abs(trdEntry - trdTP1))
                    trdSL := math.min(trdSL, trdEntry - beBufS)
                    sbBeEvt := true
                if trdTp1Hit and not trdTp2Hit and low <= trdTP2
                    trdTp2Hit := true
                    cntTP2 += 1
                if trdTp2Hit and sbTrail and not na(protHighE)
                    // ◆ SB-14: never trail through the last two bars' high
                    float capS = math.max(math.max(high, high[1]), close) + SL_PAD_ATR * atr
                    trdSL := math.min(trdSL, math.max(protHighE + SL_PAD_ATR * atr, capS))
                if trdDir == -1 and low <= trdTP3
                    cntTP3 += 1
                    trdDir := 0
                if trdDir == -1 and sbStructEx and (mssUp or (trdShiftG <= 1 and (cisdUp or chochUp or eChochUp)))
                    cntSbStruct += 1
                    sbStructExitEvt := true
                    trdDir := 0
                if trdDir == -1 and sbTimeExit and sbWEnd and not trdTp1Hit
                    cntSbTime += 1
                    sbTimeExitEvt := true
                    if sbTimeMode == "Full"
                        trdDir := 0
                    else
                        trdSL := math.min(trdSL, trdEntry)
// ◆ FIX-T2 TRADE MAX LIFE. A non-SB trade had no time stop at all: only TP2 or
// the stop closed it. One trade drifting sideways for hundreds of bars blocked
// the tracker, and every machine entry in that window was discarded. Checked
// AFTER the exits, so a bar that reaches a target is never booked as expired.
// SCOPE: FIX-T2 trade max life, as assignments
bool trdExp = confirmed and trdDir != 0 and maxTrdBars > 0 and not na(trdBar) and bar_index - trdBar > maxTrdBars
cntExp := trdExp ? cntExp + 1 : cntExp
trdDir := trdExp ? 0 : trdDir

// ◆ SB-12: keep the MODULE 10 dealing-range lock alive while ANY SB narrative
// is still live — an armed SB setup or a running SB trade. Assigned
// unconditionally: MODULE 10 reads it at the top of the NEXT bar.
sbSetLive := (sbTagL != 0 and suL >= 1) or (sbTagS != 0 and suS >= 1) or (trdDir != 0 and trdIsSb)

// SCOPE: the ACTIVE stage ends with the tracked trade
suL := confirmed and suL == 4 and trdDir == 0 and not sigMCLong  ? 0 : suL
suS := confirmed and suS == 4 and trdDir == 0 and not sigMCShort ? 0 : suS
// ◆ FIX-T2 AN UNTRACKED ENTRY IS STILL AN ENTRY. v3.2.1 reset the stage to IDLE
// when the tracker was busy, which threw away a setup that had completed the
// whole chain — and, worse, re-armed the same direction from scratch. The stage
// now stays ACTIVE (it is released when the tracker frees, above); only the
// reason line and a counter record that no outcome will be measured for it.
suLReason := confirmed and sigMCLong and suL == 4 and suLBar == bar_index and trdDir != 0 ? "entry not tracked — tracker busy (stage stays ACTIVE)" : suLReason
suSReason := confirmed and sigMCShort and suS == 4 and suSBar == bar_index and trdDir != 0 ? "entry not tracked — tracker busy (stage stays ACTIVE)" : suSReason

// ◆ FIX-P0-6 AN OPEN TRADE IS NOT SILENTLY REPLACED.
// v3.1.1 overwrote `trdEntry / trdSL / trdDir` on any new signal even with a
// trade still running, so that trade's outcome was never counted, the SB exit
// engine lost its time and structure stops mid-flight, and the dashboard's
// "Active trade" row described something that no longer existed. One tracked
// trade at a time — which is also what a discretionary trader actually does.
trackNew = (anyLong or anyShort) and confirmed and trdDir == 0

float entryP = na
float slP    = na
float tp1P   = na
float tp2P   = na
float tp3P   = na
if (anyLong or anyShort) and confirmed
    // ◆ FIX-P0-4 / FIX-P0-5: both already resolved and validated in MODULE 18B
    entryP := planEntry
    slP    := planSL
    float risk = math.abs(entryP - slP)
    tp1P := anyLong ? entryP + risk : entryP - risk
    // FIX-P1-7: TP2 comes from the viability gate — clamped to the first
    // opposing POI when one sits inside the R multiple.
    tp2P := nz(planTP2, anyLong ? entryP + rrMult * risk : entryP - rrMult * risk)
    // ◆ FIX-N4: the 3R fallback was hardcoded, so `rrMult` above 3 put TP2 past
    // TP3 and inverted the ladder. TP3 is always at least one R beyond TP2.
    float tp3Mult = math.max(3.0, rrMult + 1)
    tp3P := anyLong ? entryP + tp3Mult * risk : entryP - tp3Mult * risk
    if tpMode == "Liquidity target"
        float tgt = na
        if anyLong
            if not na(nsBot) and nsBot > entryP
                tgt := nsBot
            else if not na(lastRes) and lastRes > entryP
                tgt := lastRes
            if not na(tgt) and tgt - entryP >= rrMult * risk
                tp3P := tgt
        else
            if not na(nbTop) and nbTop < entryP
                tgt := nbTop
            else if not na(lastSup) and lastSup < entryP
                tgt := lastSup
            if not na(tgt) and entryP - tgt >= rrMult * risk
                tp3P := tgt
    // ◆ SB-6 liquidity target: the NEAREST opposing pool that still pays at
    // least TP2 — an unspent opposite POI, a prior session high/low, or PDH/PDL.
    // If nothing qualifies, TP3 stays on the 3R (or tpMode) value.
    trdLiqTgt := trackNew ? na : trdLiqTgt      // SCOPE
    if sbLiqTgt and (sbFireL or sbFireS)
        float need = anyLong ? entryP + rrMult * risk : entryP - rrMult * risk
        float lt = na
        if anyLong
            lt := f_nearAbove(lt, nsBot,     need)
            lt := f_nearAbove(lt, asiaHiLvl, need)
            lt := f_nearAbove(lt, lonHiLvl,  need)
            lt := f_nearAbove(lt, nyHiLvl,   need)
            lt := f_nearAbove(lt, pdh,       need)
        else
            lt := f_nearBelow(lt, nbTop,     need)
            lt := f_nearBelow(lt, asiaLoLvl, need)
            lt := f_nearBelow(lt, lonLoLvl,  need)
            lt := f_nearBelow(lt, nyLoLvl,   need)
            lt := f_nearBelow(lt, pdl,       need)
        tp3P      := na(lt) ? tp3P : lt         // SCOPE
        trdLiqTgt := trackNew ? lt : trdLiqTgt
    // arm validation tracking + counters (FIX-P0-6: only when nothing is open)
    // ◆ FIX-T7: a pure ENGINE signal does not fill here — it waits for the next
    // bar's open. A machine / SB entry keeps filling at its own plan price,
    // which is a resting order and therefore already realistic.
    bool deferFill = engFillNext
    if trackNew and deferFill
        pendDir := anyLong ? 1 : -1
        pendSL  := slP
    if trackNew and not deferFill
        trdEntry := entryP
        trdSL    := slP
        trdTP1   := tp1P
        trdTP2   := tp2P
        trdTP3   := tp3P
        trdDir   := anyLong ? 1 : -1
        trdBar    := bar_index
        trdTp1Hit := false
        trdTp2Hit := false
        trdIsSb   := sbFireL or sbFireS
        trdSbWin  := sbFireL ? sbTagL : sbFireS ? sbTagS : 0
        trdShiftG := sbFireL ? sbShiftGL : sbFireS ? sbShiftGS : 0
    cntUntracked := trackNew ? cntUntracked : cntUntracked + 1   // FIX-T2 · SCOPE
    cntSbEnt     := (sbFireL or sbFireS) and trackNew ? cntSbEnt + 1 : cntSbEnt
    // SCOPE: the four tier buckets as assignments
    cntLong  := anyLong ? cntLong + 1 : cntLong
    cntShort := anyShort ? cntShort + 1 : cntShort
    trdToday := trdToday + 1        // FIX-W16: the day's signal book
    cntAp    := cntAp + (anyLong and finalTierL >= 4 ? 1 : 0) + (anyShort and finalTierS >= 4 ? 1 : 0)
    cntA     := cntA + (anyLong and finalTierL == 3 ? 1 : 0) + (anyShort and finalTierS == 3 ? 1 : 0)

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 20
// VISUALIZATION
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
plot(showLevels ? lastRes : na, "Resistance (last swing high)",
     color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 50), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(showLevels ? lastSup : na, "Support (last swing low)",
     color = color.new(COL_BULL, 50), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(showStruct and showProt ? protLowE : na, "Protected low (external)",
     color = color.new(COL_BULL, 15), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 2)
plot(showStruct and showProt ? protHighE : na, "Protected high (external)",
     color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 15), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 2)
plot(showPdwl ? pdh : na, "Prev day high",  color = color.new(color.silver, 55), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(showPdwl ? pdl : na, "Prev day low",   color = color.new(color.silver, 55), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(showPdwl ? pwh : na, "Prev week high", color = color.new(color.silver, 70), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 2)
plot(showPdwl ? pwl : na, "Prev week low",  color = color.new(color.silver, 70), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 2)

// sweep marks with quality (◆ = swing, ◈ = engineered pool)
plotchar(showSweeps and sslSweep and confirmed, "Sell-side sweep", "◆", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSweeps and bslSweep and confirmed, "Buy-side sweep", "◆", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSweeps and eqlSwept, "EQL pool swept", "◈", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSweeps and eqhSwept, "EQH pool swept", "◈", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSweeps and pdlSweep, "PDL swept", "◇", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 20), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSweeps and pdhSweep, "PDH swept", "◇", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 20), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showTL and tlUpBreak, "TL break (support)", "✕", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 20), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showTL and tlDnBreak, "TL break (resistance)", "✕", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 20), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesHiSwept, "Session high raided", "▿", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 15), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesLoSwept, "Session low raided", "▵", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 15), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesHiBroke, "Session high broken", "⇡", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 15), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesLoBroke, "Session low broken", "⇣", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 15), size = size.tiny)

bgcolor(paintKZ and timeframe.isintraday and inAsia ? color.new(color.purple, 95) : na, title = "Asia KZ")
bgcolor(paintKZ and timeframe.isintraday and inLon  ? color.new(color.blue,   94) : na, title = "London KZ")
bgcolor(paintKZ and timeframe.isintraday and inNY   ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title = "NY AM KZ")
// ◆ SB-8: each SB window gets its own band strength (NY AM brightest). This
// REUSES v3's single Silver Bullet bgcolor slot — the 64-output plot budget is
// full, so no new output may be added anywhere in the script.
sbBandCol = sbWinNow == 2 ? color.new(color.teal, 86) : sbWinNow == 3 ? color.new(color.teal, 90) : sbWinNow == 1 ? color.new(color.teal, 92) : color.new(color.teal, 94)
bgcolor((paintKZ or (sbOn and sbPaint)) and timeframe.isintraday and inSB ? sbBandCol : na, title = "Silver Bullet KZ")
bgcolor(paintNews and timeframe.isintraday and inNews ? color.new(color.red,  90) : na, title = "Scheduled volatility window")

// ---- merged signal label (one per direction per bar, tier-tagged) --------------
f_join(string acc, string part) =>
    acc == "" ? part : acc + "\n" + part

// CE10295 refactor: the merged signal labels only read the final signal state.
f_sigLabels() =>
    // SCOPE: twenty `if` blocks became two ternary chains. Same text, same
    // order, same one-label-per-direction rule.
    string longTxt = ""
    longTxt := sigMCLong   ? f_join(longTxt, sbFireL ? "◆ SB " + f_sbWinTxt(sbTagL) + " · " + (sbCeFillL ? "CE" : "edge") + " " + f_tierTxt(mcTierL) : "◉ SETUP " + f_tierTxt(mcTierL)) : longTxt
    longTxt := sigSDLong   ? f_join(longTxt, "Demand 50%" + (inOTEbuy ? " +OTE" : "")) : longTxt
    longTxt := sig3Long    ? f_join(longTxt, "IB break") : longTxt
    longTxt := sig4Long    ? f_join(longTxt, "Doji rev") : longTxt
    longTxt := sigTLLong   ? f_join(longTxt, "TL sweep") : longTxt
    longTxt := sigCRTLong  ? f_join(longTxt, "CRT") : longTxt
    longTxt := sigTBSLong  ? f_join(longTxt, "TSoup") : longTxt
    longTxt := sigHCrtLong ? f_join(longTxt, "HTF " + htfTf + " CRT") : longTxt
    longTxt := sigHTbsLong ? f_join(longTxt, "HTF " + htfTf + " TSoup") : longTxt
    longTxt := sigFEMLong  ? f_join(longTxt, "◎ FEM " + htfTf + "→LTF") : longTxt
    longTxt := sigLTLong and ltShowSig ? f_join(longTxt, "◬ TRAP " + ltLPats + " · " + f_ltCls(ltLongScore) + (ltLRetOk ? " · retest✓" : "")) : longTxt   // FIX-V6

    string shortTxt = ""
    shortTxt := sigMCShort   ? f_join(shortTxt, sbFireS ? "◆ SB " + f_sbWinTxt(sbTagS) + " · " + (sbCeFillS ? "CE" : "edge") + " " + f_tierTxt(mcTierS) : "◉ SETUP " + f_tierTxt(mcTierS)) : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigSDShort   ? f_join(shortTxt, "Supply 50%" + (inOTEsell ? " +OTE" : "")) : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sig3Short    ? f_join(shortTxt, "IB break") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sig4Short    ? f_join(shortTxt, "Doji rev") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigTLShort   ? f_join(shortTxt, "TL sweep") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigCRTShort  ? f_join(shortTxt, "CRT") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigTBSShort  ? f_join(shortTxt, "TSoup") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigHCrtShort ? f_join(shortTxt, "HTF " + htfTf + " CRT") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigHTbsShort ? f_join(shortTxt, "HTF " + htfTf + " TSoup") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigFEMShort  ? f_join(shortTxt, "◎ FEM " + htfTf + "→LTF") : shortTxt
    shortTxt := sigLTShort and ltShowSig ? f_join(shortTxt, "◬ TRAP " + ltSPats + " · " + f_ltCls(ltShortScore) + (ltSRetOk ? " · failed-break✓" : "")) : shortTxt

    if anyLong and longTxt != ""
        label.new(bar_index, na, f_tierTxt(finalTierL) + " · " + str.tostring(scoreLong) + "\n" + longTxt,
             yloc = yloc.belowbar, style = label.style_label_up,
             color = color.new(sbFireL ? color.teal : COL_BULL, uiTrans), textcolor = color.white, size = sigSize)
    if anyShort and shortTxt != ""
        label.new(bar_index, na, f_tierTxt(finalTierS) + " · " + str.tostring(scoreShort) + "\n" + shortTxt,
             yloc = yloc.abovebar, style = label.style_label_down,
             color = color.new(sbFireS ? color.teal : COL_BEAR, uiTrans), textcolor = color.white, size = sigSize)
f_sigLabels()

// ◆ SB forced-exit marks (labels — the plot/plotchar budget is full)
if sbOn and sbTimeExitEvt
    f_chip(false, "◆ SB time exit" + (sbTimeMode == "Full" ? "" : " (partial)"), color.new(color.teal, 25), color.white, mkSize)
if sbOn and sbStructExitEvt
    f_chip(false, "◆ SB structure exit", color.new(color.teal, 25), color.white, mkSize)

// ---- entry / SL / TP lines of the most recent signal ---------------------------
f_tradePlan() =>
    var line  entryLn = na
    var line  slLn    = na
    var line  tpLn    = na
    var line  tp2Ln   = na
    var line  tp3Ln   = na
    var label entryLb = na
    var label slLb    = na
    var label tpLb    = na
    var label tp2Lb   = na
    var label tp3Lb   = na
    // ◆ FIX-W11 (v3.4, OP-14) A CLOSED TRADE'S LADDER COMES OFF THE CHART.
    // The plan was drawn on the signal bar and never cleared, so a live BTC M5
    // chart still showed E / SL / TP1 / TP2 over price while the tracker read
    // "flat" — five lines and five labels describing a trade that no longer
    // existed, in the busiest part of the chart.
    if confirmed and trdDir == 0 and pendDir == 0 and not (anyLong or anyShort) and not na(entryLn)
        line.delete(entryLn)
        line.delete(slLn)
        line.delete(tpLn)
        line.delete(tp2Ln)
        line.delete(tp3Ln)
        label.delete(entryLb)
        label.delete(slLb)
        label.delete(tpLb)
        label.delete(tp2Lb)
        label.delete(tp3Lb)
        entryLn := na
        slLn    := na
        tpLn    := na
        tp2Ln   := na
        tp3Ln   := na
    if showTrade and (anyLong or anyShort) and not na(entryP)
        line.delete(entryLn)
        line.delete(slLn)
        line.delete(tpLn)
        line.delete(tp2Ln)
        line.delete(tp3Ln)
        label.delete(entryLb)
        label.delete(slLb)
        label.delete(tpLb)
        label.delete(tp2Lb)
        label.delete(tp3Lb)
        int x2 = bar_index + TRADE_BARS
        entryLn := line.new(bar_index, entryP, x2, entryP, color = color.gray,   style = line.style_dashed)
        slLn    := line.new(bar_index, slP,    x2, slP,    color = COL_BEAR,     style = line.style_dashed)
        tpLn    := line.new(bar_index, tp1P,   x2, tp1P,   color = color.new(COL_BULL, 45), style = line.style_dashed)
        tp2Ln   := line.new(bar_index, tp2P,   x2, tp2P,   color = color.new(COL_BULL, 20), style = line.style_dashed)
        tp3Ln   := line.new(bar_index, tp3P,   x2, tp3P,   color = COL_BULL,    style = line.style_dashed)
        float rrCost = costTicks * syminfo.mintick      // FIX-W18: the printed RR is net too
        rrTxt = str.tostring(math.round(math.max(0, math.abs(tp2P - entryP) - rrCost) / math.max(math.abs(entryP - slP) + rrCost, syminfo.mintick) * 10) / 10)
        entryLb := label.new(x2, entryP, "E "  + str.tostring(entryP, format.mintick) + " · RR " + rrTxt + " (plan, not promise)" +
             (engFillNext ? " · engine fill = next open" : ""),
             style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(color.gray, 20), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
        slLb    := label.new(x2, slP,    "SL " + str.tostring(slP, format.mintick),
             style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 20), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
        tpLb    := label.new(x2, tp1P,   "TP1 " + str.tostring(tp1P, format.mintick),
             style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(COL_BULL, 45), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
        tp2Lb   := label.new(x2, tp2P,   "TP2 " + str.tostring(tp2P, format.mintick),
             style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(COL_BULL, 30), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
        tp3Lb   := label.new(x2, tp3P,   "TP3 " + str.tostring(tp3P, format.mintick),
             style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(COL_BULL, 15), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
f_tradePlan()

// ---- HTF POI boxes + FEM locked FVG + machine anchor (last bar only) -----------
f_drawPoi() =>
    var box hPoiBuyBx  = na
    var box hPoiSellBx = na
    var box femFvgBxL  = na
    var box femFvgBxS  = na
    var box mcAnchorBxL = na
    var box mcAnchorBxS = na
    var line  sbCeLnL = na
    var line  sbCeLnS = na
    var line  sbTgtLn = na
    var label sbTgtLb = na
    if barstate.islast
        box.delete(hPoiBuyBx)
        box.delete(hPoiSellBx)
        box.delete(femFvgBxL)
        box.delete(femFvgBxS)
        box.delete(mcAnchorBxL)
        box.delete(mcAnchorBxS)
        if showPoi and not na(hPoiBTop)
            hPoiBuyBx := box.new(bar_index - 10, hPoiBTop, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, hPoiBBot,
                 border_color = color.new(COL_BULL, 50), bgcolor = color.new(COL_BULL, 95),
                 border_style = line.style_dashed,
                 text = "HTF " + htfTf + " demand" + (nz(hPoiBIn) == 0 ? " · fresh" : " · ×" + str.tostring(nz(hPoiBIn))),
                 text_size = drawSize, text_color = color.new(COL_BULL, 0), text_halign = text.align_right)
        if showPoi and not na(hPoiSTop)
            hPoiSellBx := box.new(bar_index - 10, hPoiSTop, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, hPoiSBot,
                 border_color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 50), bgcolor = color.new(COL_BEAR, 95),
                 border_style = line.style_dashed,
                 text = "HTF " + htfTf + " supply" + (nz(hPoiSIn) == 0 ? " · fresh" : " · ×" + str.tostring(nz(hPoiSIn))),
                 text_size = drawSize, text_color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), text_halign = text.align_right)
        if useFEM and femL == 3
            femFvgBxL := box.new(femLFvg, femLTop, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, femLBot,
                 border_color = color.new(COL_BULL, 35), bgcolor = color.new(COL_BULL, 92),
                 text = "◎ FEM FVG · await retest", text_size = drawSize,
                 text_color = color.new(COL_BULL, 0), text_halign = text.align_right)
        if useFEM and femS == 3
            femFvgBxS := box.new(femSFvg, femSTop, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, femSBot,
                 border_color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 35), bgcolor = color.new(COL_BEAR, 92),
                 text = "◎ FEM FVG · await retest", text_size = drawSize,
                 text_color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), text_halign = text.align_right)
        if suL == 3 and not na(suLAnchorT)
            mcAnchorBxL := box.new(nz(suLFvgBar, bar_index - 5), suLAnchorT, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, suLAnchorB,
                 border_color = color.new(sbTagL != 0 ? color.teal : COL_BULL, 20),
                 bgcolor = color.new(sbTagL != 0 ? color.teal : COL_BULL, 90),
                 text = (sbTagL != 0 ? "◆ SB CE " : mcCeOn and not na(suLFvgBar) ? "◉ CE " : "◉ anchor") +
                      (sbTagL != 0 or (mcCeOn and not na(suLFvgBar)) ? str.tostring((suLAnchorT + suLAnchorB) / 2, format.mintick) : "") + " · await retest",   // FIX-X3
                 text_size = drawSize,
                 text_color = color.new(sbTagL != 0 ? color.teal : COL_BULL, 0), text_halign = text.align_right)
        if suS == 3 and not na(suSAnchorT)
            mcAnchorBxS := box.new(nz(suSFvgBar, bar_index - 5), suSAnchorT, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, suSAnchorB,
                 border_color = color.new(sbTagS != 0 ? color.teal : COL_BEAR, 20),
                 bgcolor = color.new(sbTagS != 0 ? color.teal : COL_BEAR, 90),
                 text = (sbTagS != 0 ? "◆ SB CE " : mcCeOn and not na(suSFvgBar) ? "◉ CE " : "◉ anchor") +
                      (sbTagS != 0 or (mcCeOn and not na(suSFvgBar)) ? str.tostring((suSAnchorT + suSAnchorB) / 2, format.mintick) : "") + " · await retest",   // FIX-X3
                 text_size = drawSize,
                 text_color = color.new(sbTagS != 0 ? color.teal : COL_BEAR, 0), text_halign = text.align_right)
        // ◆ SB-8: the locked FVG's CE (the doctrine limit price) and, while an SB
        // trade is running, the planned liquidity target.
        line.delete(sbCeLnL)
        line.delete(sbCeLnS)
        line.delete(sbTgtLn)
        label.delete(sbTgtLb)
        if sbOn and sbShowPlan and suL == 3 and sbTagL != 0 and not na(suLAnchorT)
            sbCeLnL := line.new(nz(suLFvgBar, bar_index - 5), (suLAnchorT + suLAnchorB) / 2, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, (suLAnchorT + suLAnchorB) / 2,
                 color = color.new(color.teal, 10), width = 2)
        if sbOn and sbShowPlan and suS == 3 and sbTagS != 0 and not na(suSAnchorT)
            sbCeLnS := line.new(nz(suSFvgBar, bar_index - 5), (suSAnchorT + suSAnchorB) / 2, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, (suSAnchorT + suSAnchorB) / 2,
                 color = color.new(color.teal, 10), width = 2)
        if sbOn and sbShowPlan and trdIsSb and trdDir != 0 and not na(trdLiqTgt)
            sbTgtLn := line.new(bar_index - 5, trdLiqTgt, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, trdLiqTgt,
                 color = color.new(color.teal, 25), style = line.style_dotted, width = 2)
            sbTgtLb := label.new(bar_index + TRADE_BARS, trdLiqTgt, "◆ SB target " + str.tostring(trdLiqTgt, format.mintick),
                 style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(color.teal, 30), textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
f_drawPoi()

// TOKEN (v3.4): the order-flow wash and the OTE box were removed to fit the
// compiled-token budget (CE10117). They were the two largest drawings on the
// chart and Focus mode hid them by default. The dealing-range MATH is untouched
// — `pdPos`, `rangeHolds`, `inOTEbuy` / `inOTEsell` still drive the context
// score, the A+ premium/discount test, the OTE score points and the SB entry
// rule. The dashboard "Range pos" row reports the same reading in words.

// ---- NEXT BUY / NEXT SELL level (last bar only) --------------------------------
// The nearest UNSPENT demand below price and the nearest unspent supply above it
// — i.e. where the state machine is waiting for its next POI tap. A LOCATION
// marker, never a signal on its own: the chain still has to run
// sweep → POI → shift → retest before anything fires.
// Drawn with line / box / label objects on purpose: the 64-output budget
// (8 plot + 14 plotchar + 5 bgcolor + 37 alertcondition) is FULL, and drawing
// objects do not count against it.
f_nextLvlTxt(string side, int score, string kind, float lo, float hi) =>
    // FIX-P1-13: price can be INSIDE the zone — a live chart printed "(0% above)"
    // while the candle sat in the band, which reads as "not there yet" when it is
    // the opposite. Say so plainly.
    string pct = not nextLvlPct or close <= 0 ? "" :
         close >= lo and close <= hi ? " (INSIDE)" :
         " (" + str.tostring(math.abs((lo + hi) / 2 - close) / close * 100, "#.##") + "% " + ((lo + hi) / 2 > close ? "above" : "below") + ")"
    side + " T" + str.tostring(score) + (kind != "SD" and kind != "" ? " " + kind : "") + " " +
         str.tostring(lo, format.mintick) + "-" + str.tostring(hi, format.mintick) + pct

f_drawNextLvl() =>
    var line  nbLn = na
    var line  nsLn = na
    var box   nbBx = na
    var box   nsBx = na
    var label nbLb = na
    var label nsLb = na
    if barstate.islast
        line.delete(nbLn)
        line.delete(nsLn)
        box.delete(nbBx)
        box.delete(nsBx)
        label.delete(nbLb)
        label.delete(nsLb)
        int x1 = bar_index - nextLvlBack
        int x2 = bar_index + TRADE_BARS
        // ◆ FIX-W12 (v3.4, OP-13): the two labels shared one x, so whenever the
        // two zones were close — which is most of the time on an index or a
        // ranging chart — they printed on top of each other AND on top of the
        // candles. The sell label is staggered, and the LINE is short (the box
        // still spans `nextLvlBack`), so the marker stops being a rule drawn
        // across an hour of price action.
        int xLn = bar_index - math.min(nextLvlBack, 18)
        // FIX-P1-11: at the right edge the label lands under the dashboard
        // table and the two print on top of each other. Default is the middle
        // of the drawn line, which is clear chart.
        bool midPos = nextLvlPos != "Right edge"
        int  xLbB   = midPos ? xLn + 1  : x2                    // FIX-T15 / FIX-W12
        int  xLbS   = midPos ? xLn + 13 : x2
        string lbSty = midPos ? label.style_label_right : label.style_label_left
        if showNextLvl and not na(nbTop) and not na(nbBot)
            float lvl = nextLvlAnchor == "Zone 50% (CE)" ? (nbTop + nbBot) / 2 : nbTop
            if nextLvlBox
                nbBx := box.new(x1, nbTop, x2, nbBot,
                     border_color = color.new(COL_BULL, 70), bgcolor = color.new(COL_BULL, 92),
                     border_style = line.style_dotted)
            nbLn := line.new(xLn, lvl, x2, lvl, color = color.new(COL_BULL, 10),
                 style = line.style_dashed, width = 1)
            nbLb := label.new(xLbB, lvl, f_nextLvlTxt("BUY", nbScore, nbKind, nbBot, nbTop) + (nbFresh ? " ·fresh" : ""),
                 style = lbSty, color = color.new(COL_BULL, 10),
                 textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
        if showNextLvl and not na(nsTop) and not na(nsBot)
            float lvl = nextLvlAnchor == "Zone 50% (CE)" ? (nsTop + nsBot) / 2 : nsBot
            if nextLvlBox
                nsBx := box.new(x1, nsTop, x2, nsBot,
                     border_color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 70), bgcolor = color.new(COL_BEAR, 92),
                     border_style = line.style_dotted)
            nsLn := line.new(xLn, lvl, x2, lvl, color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 10),
                 style = line.style_dashed, width = 1)
            nsLb := label.new(xLbS, lvl, f_nextLvlTxt("SELL", nsScore, nsKind, nsBot, nsTop) + (nsFresh ? " ·fresh" : ""),
                 style = lbSty, color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 10),
                 textcolor = color.white, size = drawSize)
f_drawNextLvl()

// PERF: extend live drawings once on the last bar
f_extendDraw() =>
    if barstate.islast                 // SCOPE: `for in` everywhere, no size guards
        // FIX-W13: distance filter. A box further than `focusAtr` × ATR from
        // price is made fully transparent rather than deleted — the zone keeps
        // its state, its freshness counter and its place in every gate; it just
        // stops competing for the operator's attention.
        float focusHi = close + focusAtr * atr
        float focusLo = close - focusAtr * atr
        for z in zones
            box.set_right(z.bx, bar_index + 1)
            if focusMode
                bool  zFar = box.get_bottom(z.bx) > focusHi or box.get_top(z.bx) < focusLo
                color zc   = z.isSupply ? COL_BEAR : COL_BULL
                box.set_bgcolor(z.bx, color.new(zc, zFar ? 100 : 93))
                box.set_border_color(z.bx, color.new(zc, zFar ? 100 : 55))
        // FIX-P0-3: with `showEq` off the pool is tracked but not drawn, so the
        // array legitimately holds `na` line ids — skip those.
        for ln in eqhLns
            if not na(ln)
                line.set_x2(ln, bar_index + 1)
        for ln in eqlLns
            if not na(ln)
                line.set_x2(ln, bar_index + 1)
        for f in bullFvgs
            if not na(f.bx)
                box.set_right(f.bx, bar_index + 1)
                if focusMode and (f.bot > focusHi or f.top < focusLo)
                    box.set_bgcolor(f.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 100))
                    box.set_border_color(f.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 100))
        for f in bearFvgs
            if not na(f.bx)
                box.set_right(f.bx, bar_index + 1)
                if focusMode and (f.bot > focusHi or f.top < focusLo)
                    box.set_bgcolor(f.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 100))
                    box.set_border_color(f.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 100))
f_extendDraw()

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 21
// DASHBOARD — Context / Liquidity / Location / Trigger / Setup / Risk
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
f_biasTxt(int b) => b > 0 ? "▲ Bull" : b < 0 ? "▼ Bear" : "—"
f_biasCol(int b) => b > 0 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 75) : b < 0 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 75) : color.new(color.gray, 75)
f_qualTxt(int q) => q >= 4 ? "institutional" : q == 3 ? "strong" : q == 2 ? "valid" : q == 1 ? "weak" : "—"
f_suTxt(int s) => s == 0 ? "idle" : s == 1 ? "SWEPT" : s == 2 ? "AT POI" : s == 3 ? "SHIFTED·await retest" : "ACTIVE"

// CE10295 refactor: the dashboard only reads engine state.
f_dash() =>
    var table dash = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 12,
         border_color = color.new(color.gray, 70), border_width = 1)
    if showDash and barstate.islast
        rowBg = color.new(color.gray, 85)
        table.cell(dash, 0, 0, "LQ-SD-PA v3.4", bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 0, regime, bgcolor = regime == "TRENDING" ? f_biasCol(extDir) : regime == "CHOP" ? color.new(color.red, 70) : rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        // bias: the timeframe the engine actually gates on, the daily context,
        // the HTF structure read and the combined verdict (FIX-F1)
        table.cell(dash, 0, 1, "Bias " + biasTf, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize,
             tooltip = "Selected bias TF (EMA) · daily EMA · HTF structure (BOS/CHoCH) · the combined bias every gate reads.")
        table.cell(dash, 1, 1, f_biasTxt(emaBias) + " · D " + f_biasTxt(bD) + " · str " + f_biasTxt(hsDir) + " → " + f_biasTxt(htfB),
             bgcolor = f_biasCol(htfB), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        sweepTxt = (sslInPlay ? "SSL " + f_qualTxt(lastSslQual) : "") + (sslInPlay and bslInPlay ? " · " : "") + (bslInPlay ? "BSL " + f_qualTxt(lastBslQual) : "")
        table.cell(dash, 0, 2, "Struct · sweep", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 2, "int " + (msDir > 0 ? "▲" : msDir < 0 ? "▼" : "—") + " ext " + (extDir > 0 ? "▲" : extDir < 0 ? "▼" : "—") + " · " + (sweepTxt == "" ? "no sweep" : sweepTxt),
             bgcolor = f_biasCol(extDir), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        pdTxt = na(pdPos) ? "—" : (pdPos >= 0.5 ? "Prem " : "Disc ") + str.tostring(pdPos * 100, "#") + "%" + (rangeHolds ? "" : " ·tight") + (inOTEbuy ? " ·OTE buy" : inOTEsell ? " ·OTE sell" : "")
        table.cell(dash, 0, 3, "Range pos", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 3, pdTxt, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        // ◆ FIX-W14 (v3.4, OP-15): the price RANGE of each level is already on the
        // chart label and in the box. The dashboard's job is the one thing the
        // chart cannot say at a glance — how far away it is, and whether the
        // engine still rates it.
        nbTxt = na(nbTop) ? "none" : "T" + str.tostring(nbScore) + (nbKind != "SD" and nbKind != "" ? " " + nbKind : "") +
             " · " + str.tostring(math.abs(close - nbTop) / atr, "#.#") + " ATR" + (nbFresh ? " ·fresh" : "")
        nsTxt = na(nsBot) ? "none" : "T" + str.tostring(nsScore) + (nsKind != "SD" and nsKind != "" ? " " + nsKind : "") +
             " · " + str.tostring(math.abs(nsBot - close) / atr, "#.#") + " ATR" + (nsFresh ? " ·fresh" : "")
        table.cell(dash, 0, 4, "Next demand", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 4, nbTxt, bgcolor = na(nbTop) ? rowBg : color.new(COL_BULL, 80), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 0, 5, "Next supply", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 5, nsTxt, bgcolor = na(nsBot) ? rowBg : color.new(COL_BEAR, 80), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        trigTxt = (recentMssUp ? "MSS▲ " : recentChUp ? "CHoCH▲ " : recentBosUp ? "BOS▲ " : "") + (recentCisdUp ? "CISD▲ " : "") + (recentMssDn ? "MSS▼ " : recentChDn ? "CHoCH▼ " : recentBosDn ? "BOS▼ " : "") + (recentCisdDn ? "CISD▼" : "")
        femTxt = femL == 3 or femS == 3 ? "FVG locked" : femL == 2 or femS == 2 ? "shift✓" : femL == 1 or femS == 1 ? "POI tap" : "idle"
        table.cell(dash, 0, 6, "Trigger · FEM", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 6, (trigTxt == "" ? "none" : trigTxt) + " · ◎ " + femTxt, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 0, 7, "◉ Setup L / S", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 7, f_suTxt(suL) + "  /  " + f_suTxt(suS),
             bgcolor = suL >= 1 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 60) : suS >= 1 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 60) : rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 0, 8, "Alignment", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize,
             tooltip = "Narrative alignment 0–100, NOT win probability. Context 30 · Liquidity 20 · POI 20 · Trigger 20 · Execution 10.")
        table.cell(dash, 1, 8, "L " + str.tostring(scoreLong) + " (" + f_tierTxt(tierLong) + ")  S " + str.tostring(scoreShort) + " (" + f_tierTxt(tierShort) + ")",
             bgcolor = scoreLong >= 80 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 55) : scoreShort >= 80 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 55) : rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        // session · volatility window · Silver Bullet, in one row
        sbRemTxt = na(sbRemMin) ? "" : " " + str.tostring(sbRemMin, "#") + "m left"
        sbTxt = not sbEnable ? "SB off" : sbActive ? "◆ SB " + f_sbWinTxt(sbWinNow) + sbRemTxt : sbCtx ? "◆ pre " + sbWinTxt : "SB off-window"
        kzTxt = not timeframe.isintraday ? "—" : (inSB ? "SB KZ" : inNY ? "NY AM" : inLon ? "London" : inAsia ? "Asia" : "off-hours") + (inNews ? " · ⚠ vol window" : "") + " · " + sbTxt
        table.cell(dash, 0, 9, "Session", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize,
             tooltip = "Killzone · scheduled volatility window (NOT a live calendar) · Silver Bullet window state. SB windows (NY): London 03–04 · AM 10–11 · PM 14–15.")
        table.cell(dash, 1, 9, kzTxt, bgcolor = inNews and timeframe.isintraday ? color.new(color.red, 60) : sbActive ? color.new(color.teal, 45) : rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        riskTxt = trdDir == 0 ? (pendDir != 0 ? "order pending — fills next open" : "flat") :
             (trdDir == 1 ? "LONG " : "SHORT ") + str.tostring(trdEntry, format.mintick) + " SL " + str.tostring(trdSL, format.mintick) +
             (trdTp1Hit ? " ·TP1✓" : "") + (trdTp2Hit ? " ·TP2✓" : "") + (trdIsSb ? " ·SB" : "")
        table.cell(dash, 0, 10, "Trade", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dash, 1, 10, riskTxt, bgcolor = trdDir == 1 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 70) : trdDir == -1 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 70) : rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        // ◆ FIX-P1-10 the verdict names the gate that actually blocked, and the
        // ADR read rides along (FIX-F4) so the row count stays inside the budget.
        bestScore = math.max(scoreLong, scoreShort)
        // FIX-W15 (OP-16): "ADR 10%" at 01:00 into the session does not mean the
        // day has room — it means the day has barely started. Say which it is.
        float dayFrac = timeframe.isintraday ? math.min(1.0, (time - time("D")) / 86400000.0) : na
        adrTxt = na(adrPct) ? "" : " · ADR " + str.tostring(adrPct * 100, "#") + "%" +
             (not na(dayFrac) and dayFrac < 0.25 ? " (early)" : "") +
             (adrChaseL ? " no-chase▲" : adrChaseS ? " no-chase▼" : "")
        // ◆ FIX-W4 (v3.4, OP-4) THE ACTIVE SIDE'S OWN BLOCKER COMES FIRST.
        // The `dirLock` branch used to be tested before the score / gate
        // branches, so a live XAU M5 chart printed "SHORT narrative live ·
        // longs locked · AT POI" while the blocker for THAT SHORT was its own
        // score (42 < 50). The operator reads "narrative live" as "a trade is
        // coming" and waits for something that is already refused. The lock is
        // context, not a verdict — it rides along at the end of the line.
        int    actSide = suL >= 2 ? 1 : suS >= 2 ? -1 : suL == 1 ? 1 : suS == 1 ? -1 : 0
        string actTag  = actSide == 1 ? "L: " : "S: "
        string actKill = actSide == 1 ? gateKillL : actSide == -1 ? gateKillS : ""
        float  actScore = actSide == 1 ? scoreLong : actSide == -1 ? scoreShort : bestScore
        int    actStage = actSide == 1 ? suL : suS
        string lockTxt = dirLock == 0 ? "" : dirLock == 1 ? " · shorts locked" : " · longs locked"
        noTrade = anyLong or anyShort ? "" :
             planKill != "" ? "NO · " + planKill :
             actKill != "" ? "NO · " + actTag + actKill + lockTxt :
             actSide != 0 and minScore > 0 and actScore < minScore ? "NO · " + actTag + "score " + str.tostring(actScore) + " < " + str.tostring(minScore) + " · " + f_suTxt(actStage) + lockTxt :
             actSide != 0 ? actTag + f_suTxt(actStage) + lockTxt :
             not govOK ? "NO · day closed — " + str.tostring(trdToday) + " signals, " + str.tostring(lossToday) + " losses" :
             trdDir != 0 ? (trdDir == 1 ? "LONG trade live · shorts locked" : "SHORT trade live · longs locked") :
             poiConflict ? "NO · POI conflict — no tie-break" :
             poiConflictRaw ? "NO · POI conflict resolved to " + (poiSideBias > 0 ? "longs" : "shorts") :
             htfB == 0 and neutralMode == "Block both sides" ? "NO · neutral bias" :
             not sbOnlyOK ? "NO · outside SB windows" :
             regime == "CHOP" and not chopOKL and not chopOKS ? "NO · chop" :
             inNews and timeframe.isintraday and useNews and not newsOK ? "NO · volatility window" :
             minScore > 0 and bestScore < minScore ? "NO · score " + str.tostring(bestScore) + " < " + str.tostring(minScore) :
             suL == 0 and suS == 0 and not sslInPlay and not bslInPlay ? "NO · no liquidity event" : "waiting"
        table.cell(dash, 0, 11, "Verdict", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize,
             tooltip = "The gate that is actually blocking, plus today's range vs the average daily range (no-chase = A+ refused in that direction).")
        table.cell(dash, 1, 11, (anyLong ? "LONG signal" : anyShort ? "SHORT signal" : noTrade) + adrTxt,
             bgcolor = anyLong ? color.new(COL_BULL, 40) : anyShort ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 40) : adrExh ? color.new(color.orange, 65) : rowBg,
             text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
f_dash()

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 22
// DEBUG TABLE (ARCH-11)
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CE10295 refactor: the debug table only reads engine state.
f_dbg() =>
    var table dbg = table.new(position.bottom_right, 2, 7,
         border_color = color.new(color.gray, 70), border_width = 1)
    if showDbg and barstate.islast
        rowBg = color.new(color.navy, 80)
        table.cell(dbg, 0, 0, "DEBUG", bgcolor = color.new(color.navy, 55), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 1, 0, "engine state", bgcolor = color.new(color.navy, 55), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 0, 1, "Sweeps", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize,
             tooltip = "raw = the grade the sweeping candle earned · eff = with the MSS upgrade · k = pool kind (1 swing · 2 EQ · 3 PD · 4 session).")
        table.cell(dbg, 1, 1, "SSL raw" + str.tostring(lastSslQualRaw) + "/eff" + str.tostring(lastSslQual) + " k" + str.tostring(lastSslKind) + (sslInPlay ? " live" : " stale") +
             "\nBSL raw" + str.tostring(lastBslQualRaw) + "/eff" + str.tostring(lastBslQual) + " k" + str.tostring(lastBslKind) + (bslInPlay ? " live" : " stale"),
             bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 0, 2, "Machine L", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 1, 2, f_suTxt(suL) + " · " + suLReason, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 0, 3, "Machine S", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 1, 3, f_suTxt(suS) + " · " + suSReason, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 0, 4, "Score C/L/P/T/E", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize,
             tooltip = "context / liquidity / POI / trigger / execution.")
        table.cell(dbg, 1, 4, "L " + str.tostring(ctxLong) + "/" + str.tostring(liqLong) + "/" + str.tostring(poiLong) + "/" + str.tostring(trigLong) + "/" + str.tostring(execLong) +
             "\nS " + str.tostring(ctxShort) + "/" + str.tostring(liqShort) + "/" + str.tostring(poiShort) + "/" + str.tostring(trigShort) + "/" + str.tostring(execShort),
             bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 0, 5, "Counts", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize,
             tooltip = "Signals against the OUTCOMES of the trades the tracker could take. untrk = fired while the tracker was busy, so it has no outcome and must NOT be read against the TP/SL counts. A sample, not a backtest.")
        table.cell(dbg, 1, 5, "A " + str.tostring(cntA) + " A+ " + str.tostring(cntAp) + " L" + str.tostring(cntLong) + " S" + str.tostring(cntShort) +
             "\nTP " + str.tostring(cntTP1) + "/" + str.tostring(cntTP2) + "/" + str.tostring(cntTP3) + " SL " + str.tostring(cntSL) + " exp " + str.tostring(cntExp) + " untrk " + str.tostring(cntUntracked),
             bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 0, 6, "◬ LT · ◆ SB · day", bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
        table.cell(dbg, 1, 6, ltDbgMach + "\n" + ltDbgLast +
             "\nSB win " + str.tostring(sbWinNow) + " tag " + str.tostring(sbTagL) + "/" + str.tostring(sbTagS) + " ent " + str.tostring(cntSbEnt) +
             " · day " + str.tostring(trdToday) + " sig " + str.tostring(lossToday) + " loss",
             bgcolor = ltLState == 1 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 70) : ltSState == 1 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 70) : sbActive ? color.new(color.teal, 60) : rowBg,
             text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
f_dbg()

// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 23
// ALERTS — state transitions only, layer-tagged. Use "Once per bar close".
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// FIX-N1: alerts read the same tier the label prints
tL = finalTierL
tS = finalTierS
alertcondition(anyLong  and tL >= 4, "[SIGNAL] A+ LONG",  "A+ LONG — full narrative (sweep → POI → shift → retest) on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(anyShort and tS >= 4, "[SIGNAL] A+ SHORT", "A+ SHORT — full narrative on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(anyLong  and tL == 3, "[SIGNAL] A LONG",   "A-tier LONG on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(anyShort and tS == 3, "[SIGNAL] A SHORT",  "A-tier SHORT on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(sigMCLong or sigMCShort, "[SIGNAL] ◉ Setup machine entry", "Setup state machine entry on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(anyLong,  "[SIGNAL] Any LONG",  "LONG signal on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(anyShort, "[SIGNAL] Any SHORT", "SHORT signal on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(sigFEMLong or sigFEMShort, "[SIGNAL] ◎ Fractal entry model", "FEM execution on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// setup lifecycle
alertcondition((suL == 1 and suL[1] != 1) or (suS == 1 and suS[1] != 1), "[SETUP] Armed (quality sweep)", "Setup armed by a quality sweep on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((suL == 2 and suL[1] != 2) or (suS == 2 and suS[1] != 2), "[SETUP] POI tapped",            "Setup reached its POI on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((suL == 3 and suL[1] != 3) or (suS == 3 and suS[1] != 3), "[SETUP] Structure shifted",     "Setup structure shift — awaiting retest on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((suL == 0 and suL[1] >= 1 and suL[1] <= 3) or (suS == 0 and suS[1] >= 1 and suS[1] <= 3),
     "[SETUP] Invalidated / expired", "Setup invalidated or expired on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — see debug reason")
// trade management
alertcondition(cntTP1 > cntTP1[1], "[TRADE] TP1 hit", "TP1 (1R) touched on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(cntTP2 > cntTP2[1], "[TRADE] TP2 hit", "TP2 touched on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(cntSL  > cntSL[1],  "[TRADE] SL hit",  "Stop touched on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// triggers
alertcondition(mssUp or mssDn,                  "[TRIG] MSS",           "MSS — sweep + displaced structural shift on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(eChochUp or eChochDn,            "[TRIG] External CHoCH", "External CHoCH on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(eBosUp or eBosDn,                "[TRIG] External BOS",  "External BOS on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(cisdUp or cisdDn,                "[TRIG] CISD",          "Change in state of delivery on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((ifcBull or ifcBear) and confirmed, "[TRIG] IFC candle", "IFC candle on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// liquidity
alertcondition((sslSweep or bslSweep) and confirmed, "[LIQ] Swing sweep", "Swing liquidity swept on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(eqhSwept or eqlSwept,            "[LIQ] EQH/EQL pool swept", "Equal highs/lows pool swept on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(pdhSweep or pdlSweep,            "[LIQ] Prev-day level swept", "Previous day high/low swept on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(sesHiSwept or sesLoSwept,        "[LIQ] Session level raided", "Session level raided on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(sesHiBroke or sesLoBroke,        "[LIQ] Session level broken", "Session level broken on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(tlUpBreak or tlDnBreak,          "[LIQ] Trendline break", "Trendline broken on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// POI
alertcondition(touchNextBuy,  "[POI] Price entered next demand", "Price entered next demand zone on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(touchNextSell, "[POI] Price entered next supply", "Price entered next supply zone on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((femL == 3 and femL[1] != 3) or (femS == 3 and femS[1] != 3), "[POI] FEM FVG locked", "FEM execution FVG locked on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// context
alertcondition(inNews and not inNews[1], "[CTX] Volatility window started", "Scheduled volatility window started on {{ticker}} — entries suppressed")
alertcondition(not inNews and inNews[1], "[CTX] Volatility window ended",   "Scheduled volatility window ended on {{ticker}} — entries re-enabled")
// ◬ liquidity trap engine — consolidated (alertcondition counts toward the
// 64-output limit). WHICH pattern armed / exact class (HIGH vs VERY HIGH) /
// score / level come via the dynamic alert() messages: create ONE alert on
// "Any alert() function call" for the detailed feed.
// FIX-V6: a trap signal is a graded engine entry now — it also fires "Any LONG /
