// SHORT". The two HIGH+ conditions were dropped (class travels in the alert() feed).
alertcondition(ltArmEvt, "[LT] Trap setup detected", "Liquidity-trap setup armed on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — waiting for confirmation (pattern in the alert() feed)")
alertcondition(sigLTLong  and anyLong,  "[LT] Bullish trap entry", "Liquidity-trap LONG entry on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — pattern + class in the label")
alertcondition(sigLTShort and anyShort, "[LT] Bearish trap entry", "Liquidity-trap SHORT entry on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — pattern + class in the label")
// ◆ FIX-W19 (v3.4, OP-20) JOURNAL LINE. Every alert in this file is prose: it
// tells you a trade happened, and nothing a spreadsheet can read. Without the
// setup's own facts — pool kind, raw sweep grade, shift grade, window, tier,
// score, stage, anchor — there is no way to reconcile the chart against the
// broker statement afterwards, which is where the real edge review happens.
// ONE machine-readable line per fired signal, on the same "Any alert()" feed.
jrnlEnable = input.bool(true, "Journal alert() line on every signal", group = grpD,
     tooltip = "Pipe-separated fields for a spreadsheet: side, source, tier, score, pool, grades, regime, E / SL / TP2, day count.")
if jrnlEnable and (anyLong or anyShort) and confirmed
    alert("LQJRNL|" + syminfo.ticker + "|" + timeframe.period + "|" + (anyLong ? "L" : "S") +
         "|" + (sbFireL or sbFireS ? "SB" : sigMCLong or sigMCShort ? "MC" : sigLTLong or sigLTShort ? "LT" : "ENG") +
         "|" + f_tierTxt(anyLong ? finalTierL : finalTierS) +
         "|sc" + str.tostring(anyLong ? scoreLong : scoreShort) +
         "|k" + str.tostring(anyLong ? lastSslKind : lastBslKind) +
         "q" + str.tostring(anyLong ? lastSslQualRaw : lastBslQualRaw) +
         "sh" + str.tostring(anyLong ? sbShiftGL : sbShiftGS) +
         "w" + str.tostring(anyLong ? sbTagL : sbTagS) +
         "|" + regime +
         "|E" + str.tostring(entryP, format.mintick) +
         "|SL" + str.tostring(slP, format.mintick) +
         "|T2" + str.tostring(tp2P, format.mintick) +
         "|d" + str.tostring(trdToday) + "/" + str.tostring(lossToday),
         alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)

// ---- LT dynamic alerts (create one "Any alert() function call" alert) -------------
if ltDynAlert and sigLTLong and anyLong
    alert("◬ LT BUY " + syminfo.ticker + " · " + ltLPats + " · " + str.tostring(ltLongScore) + " · E " + str.tostring(entryP, format.mintick) + " SL " + str.tostring(slP, format.mintick), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
if ltDynAlert and sigLTShort and anyShort
    alert("◬ LT SELL " + syminfo.ticker + " · " + ltSPats + " · " + str.tostring(ltShortScore) + " · E " + str.tostring(entryP, format.mintick) + " SL " + str.tostring(slP, format.mintick), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)

// ◆ SILVER BULLET — ONE consolidated alertcondition. The 64-output plot budget
// is FULL (8 plot + 14 plotchar + 5 bgcolor + 37 alertcondition = 64), so every
// other SB event travels through alert(): create ONE alert on "Any alert()
// function call" to receive window, side, CE-vs-edge, tier, score and the full
// exit plan.
alertcondition(sbFireL or sbFireS, "[SB] ◆ Silver Bullet entry", "Silver Bullet entry on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — window / CE-or-edge / tier / exit plan in the alert() feed")

// CE10295 refactor: the SB alert() feed only reads state and emits messages.
f_sbAlertFeed() =>
    if sbDynAlert and sbOn
        if sbFireL or sbFireS
            alert("◆ SB " + (sbFireL ? "BUY " : "SELL ") + syminfo.ticker +
                 " · " + f_sbWinTxt(sbFireL ? sbTagL : sbTagS) +
                 " · " + ((sbFireL ? sbCeFillL : sbCeFillS) ? "CE" : "edge") +
                 " · " + f_tierTxt(sbFireL ? mcTierL : mcTierS) +
                 " · SL " + str.tostring(slP, format.mintick) +
                 " TP1 " + str.tostring(tp1P, format.mintick) +
                 " TP2 " + str.tostring(tp2P, format.mintick), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        if (sbTagL != 0 and suL == 1 and nz(suL[1]) != 1) or (sbTagS != 0 and suS == 1 and nz(suS[1]) != 1)
            alert("◆ SB armed · " + sbWinTxt + " · q" + str.tostring(sbTagL != 0 ? lastSslQual : lastBslQual) +
                 " k" + str.tostring(sbTagL != 0 ? lastSslKind : lastBslKind), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        if (sbTagL != 0 and suL == 3 and nz(suL[1]) != 3) or (sbTagS != 0 and suS == 3 and nz(suS[1]) != 3)
            alert("◆ SB FVG locked · CE " + str.tostring(sbTagL != 0 ? (suLAnchorT + suLAnchorB) / 2 : (suSAnchorT + suSAnchorB) / 2, format.mintick),
                 alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        if sbBeEvt and trdIsSb
            alert("◆ SB TP1 · stop to BE · SL " + str.tostring(trdSL, format.mintick), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        if sbTimeExitEvt
            alert("◆ SB TIME EXIT · " + (sbTimeMode == "Full" ? "close" : "reduce, rest to BE"), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        if sbStructExitEvt
            alert("◆ SB STRUCTURE EXIT · close the remainder", alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
        // death edge: an SB-TAGGED setup went from armed (1-3) to idle on THIS bar.
        // suL[1] == 4 is excluded, so a completed SB trade never reports as "gone".
        if (suL == 0 and nz(suL[1]) >= 1 and nz(suL[1]) <= 3 and nz(sbTagL[1]) != 0) or (suS == 0 and nz(suS[1]) >= 1 and nz(suS[1]) <= 3 and nz(sbTagS[1]) != 0)
            alert("◆ SB setup gone · " + (suL == 0 and nz(sbTagL[1]) != 0 ? suLReason : suSReason), alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
f_sbAlertFeed()
